     1                                  ; =============================================================================
     2                                  ; CoreVM BIOS — Custom BIOS for libcorevm hypervisor
     3                                  ; =============================================================================
     4                                  ; Assembled as a flat 64KB binary, loaded at physical address 0xF0000.
     5                                  ; All code uses segment 0xF000 with offsets 0x0000-0xFFFF.
     6                                  ; The CPU reset vector at 0xFFFF0 jumps to the POST entry point.
     7                                  ; =============================================================================
     8                                  
     9                                  [BITS 16]
    10                                  [ORG 0]
    11                                  
    12                                  ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    13                                  ; Include modules in layout order.
    14                                  ; Data and tables come first, then routines, then POST entry, then reset vector.
    15                                  ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    16                                  
    17                                  %include "bda.asm"
     1                              <1> ; =============================================================================
     2                              <1> ; bda.asm — BIOS Data Area constants and initialization
     3                              <1> ; =============================================================================
     4                              <1> 
     5                              <1> ; BDA absolute addresses (segment 0x0040).
     6                              <1> BDA_SEG             equ 0x0040
     7                              <1> BDA_COM1            equ 0x0400      ; COM1 base port
     8                              <1> BDA_COM2            equ 0x0402
     9                              <1> BDA_COM3            equ 0x0404
    10                              <1> BDA_COM4            equ 0x0406
    11                              <1> BDA_EQUIP           equ 0x0410      ; Equipment word
    12                              <1> BDA_CONV_MEM_KB     equ 0x0413      ; Conventional memory in KB
    13                              <1> BDA_KBD_FLAGS1      equ 0x0417      ; Keyboard shift flags byte 1
    14                              <1> BDA_KBD_FLAGS2      equ 0x0418      ; Keyboard shift flags byte 2
    15                              <1> BDA_KBD_BUF_HEAD    equ 0x041A      ; Keyboard buffer head offset (rel to 0x400)
    16                              <1> BDA_KBD_BUF_TAIL    equ 0x041C      ; Keyboard buffer tail offset (rel to 0x400)
    17                              <1> BDA_KBD_BUF         equ 0x041E      ; Keyboard buffer (32 bytes, 16 entries)
    18                              <1> BDA_VIDEO_MODE      equ 0x0449      ; Current video mode
    19                              <1> BDA_SCREEN_COLS     equ 0x044A      ; Screen columns (word)
    20                              <1> BDA_VIDEO_PAGE_SZ   equ 0x044C      ; Video page size (word)
    21                              <1> BDA_CURSOR_POS      equ 0x0450      ; Cursor positions (8 pages, word each)
    22                              <1> BDA_CURSOR_SHAPE    equ 0x0460      ; Cursor start/end scanline
    23                              <1> BDA_ACTIVE_PAGE     equ 0x0462      ; Active display page
    24                              <1> BDA_CRTC_PORT       equ 0x0463      ; CRTC base port (0x3D4 for color)
    25                              <1> BDA_TIMER_COUNT     equ 0x046C      ; Timer tick count (dword)
    26                              <1> BDA_TIMER_OVERFLOW  equ 0x0470      ; Timer overflow (midnight flag)
    27                              <1> BDA_BREAK_FLAG      equ 0x0471      ; Ctrl-Break flag
    28                              <1> BDA_SOFT_RESET      equ 0x0472      ; Soft reset flag
    29                              <1> BDA_NUM_HD          equ 0x0475      ; Number of hard disks
    30                              <1> BDA_KBD_BUF_START   equ 0x0480      ; Keyboard buffer start offset
    31                              <1> BDA_KBD_BUF_END     equ 0x0482      ; Keyboard buffer end offset
    32                              <1> BDA_ROWS_MINUS1     equ 0x0484      ; Number of rows minus 1
    33                              <1> 
    34                              <1> ; EBDA location.
    35                              <1> EBDA_SEG            equ 0x9FC0      ; EBDA at 0x9FC00
    36                              <1> EBDA_SIZE_KB        equ 1           ; 1 KB
    37                              <1> 
    38                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    39                              <1> ; bda_init — Initialize the BIOS Data Area at 0x0040:0x0000.
    40                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    41                              <1> bda_init:
    42 00000000 06                  <1>     push es
    43 00000001 57                  <1>     push di
    44 00000002 51                  <1>     push cx
    45 00000003 50                  <1>     push ax
    46                              <1> 
    47                              <1>     ; Zero out BDA (256 bytes at 0x0400).
    48 00000004 31C0                <1>     xor ax, ax
    49 00000006 8EC0                <1>     mov es, ax
    50 00000008 BF0004              <1>     mov di, 0x0400
    51 0000000B B98000              <1>     mov cx, 128             ; 256 bytes / 2
    52 0000000E F3AB                <1>     rep stosw
    53                              <1> 
    54                              <1>     ; COM1 port base.
    55 00000010 26C7060004F803      <1>     mov word [es:BDA_COM1], 0x03F8
    56                              <1> 
    57                              <1>     ; Equipment word: bit 1 = FPU present, bits 4-5 = 11 (80x25 color).
    58                              <1>     ;   0x0022 = FPU + initial video mode 80x25 color
    59 00000017 26C70610042200      <1>     mov word [es:BDA_EQUIP], 0x0022
    60                              <1> 
    61                              <1>     ; Conventional memory: 639 KB (EBDA takes 1 KB from 640).
    62 0000001E 26C70613047F02      <1>     mov word [es:BDA_CONV_MEM_KB], 639
    63                              <1> 
    64                              <1>     ; Keyboard buffer pointers (empty buffer).
    65 00000025 26C7061A041E00      <1>     mov word [es:BDA_KBD_BUF_HEAD], 0x001E  ; Offset relative to 0x400
    66 0000002C 26C7061C041E00      <1>     mov word [es:BDA_KBD_BUF_TAIL], 0x001E
    67 00000033 26C70680041E00      <1>     mov word [es:BDA_KBD_BUF_START], 0x001E
    68 0000003A 26C70682043E00      <1>     mov word [es:BDA_KBD_BUF_END], 0x003E   ; 0x1E + 32
    69                              <1> 
    70                              <1>     ; Video: mode 03h, 80 columns, 25 rows.
    71 00000041 26C606490403        <1>     mov byte [es:BDA_VIDEO_MODE], 0x03
    72 00000047 26C7064A045000      <1>     mov word [es:BDA_SCREEN_COLS], 80
    73 0000004E 26C7064C04A00F      <1>     mov word [es:BDA_VIDEO_PAGE_SZ], 4000   ; 80*25*2
    74 00000055 26C606620400        <1>     mov byte [es:BDA_ACTIVE_PAGE], 0
    75 0000005B 26C7066304D403      <1>     mov word [es:BDA_CRTC_PORT], 0x03D4
    76 00000062 26C606840418        <1>     mov byte [es:BDA_ROWS_MINUS1], 24
    77                              <1> 
    78                              <1>     ; Cursor shape: scanlines 6-7 (underline cursor).
    79 00000068 26C70660040706      <1>     mov word [es:BDA_CURSOR_SHAPE], 0x0607
    80                              <1> 
    81 0000006F 58                  <1>     pop ax
    82 00000070 59                  <1>     pop cx
    83 00000071 5F                  <1>     pop di
    84 00000072 07                  <1>     pop es
    85 00000073 C3                  <1>     ret
    86                              <1> 
    87                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    88                              <1> ; ebda_init — Initialize the Extended BIOS Data Area at 0x9FC00.
    89                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    90                              <1> ebda_init:
    91 00000074 06                  <1>     push es
    92 00000075 57                  <1>     push di
    93 00000076 51                  <1>     push cx
    94 00000077 50                  <1>     push ax
    95                              <1> 
    96                              <1>     ; Zero EBDA (1 KB).
    97 00000078 B8C09F              <1>     mov ax, EBDA_SEG
    98 0000007B 8EC0                <1>     mov es, ax
    99 0000007D 31FF                <1>     xor di, di
   100 0000007F 31C0                <1>     xor ax, ax
   101 00000081 B90002              <1>     mov cx, 512             ; 1024 / 2
   102 00000084 F3AB                <1>     rep stosw
   103                              <1> 
   104                              <1>     ; First byte of EBDA = size in KB.
   105 00000086 26C606000001        <1>     mov byte [es:0x0000], EBDA_SIZE_KB
   106                              <1> 
   107                              <1>     ; Store EBDA segment in BDA at 0x040E.
   108 0000008C 31C0                <1>     xor ax, ax
   109 0000008E 8EC0                <1>     mov es, ax
   110 00000090 26C7060E04C09F      <1>     mov word [es:0x040E], EBDA_SEG
   111                              <1> 
   112 00000097 58                  <1>     pop ax
   113 00000098 59                  <1>     pop cx
   114 00000099 5F                  <1>     pop di
   115 0000009A 07                  <1>     pop es
   116 0000009B C3                  <1>     ret
    18                                  %include "strings.asm"

    19                                  %include "e820.asm"
     1                              <1> ; =============================================================================
     2                              <1> ; e820.asm — E820 memory map table (built during POST)
     3                              <1> ; =============================================================================
     4                              <1> 
     5                              <1> ; Each E820 entry: base(8) + length(8) + type(4) + acpi(4) = 24 bytes.
     6                              <1> ; We support up to 8 entries.
     7                              <1> E820_MAX_ENTRIES    equ 8
     8                              <1> E820_ENTRY_SIZE     equ 24
     9                              <1> 
    10 0000046B 0000                <1> e820_count:         dw 0
    11 0000046D 00<rep C0h>         <1> e820_table:         times (E820_MAX_ENTRIES * E820_ENTRY_SIZE) db 0
    12                              <1> 
    13                              <1> ; RAM size in bytes (filled by memory_detect).
    14 0000052D 0000000000000000    <1> ram_size_bytes:     dd 0, 0         ; 64-bit value (low, high)
    15                              <1> 
    16                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    17                              <1> ; memory_detect — Detect memory size from CMOS and build the E820 table.
    18                              <1> ;
    19                              <1> ; The CMOS device in libcorevm pre-populates:
    20                              <1> ;   Reg 0x17/0x18 = extended memory in KB (1MB-65MB range, capped at 0xFFFF)
    21                              <1> ;   Reg 0x34/0x35 = memory above 16MB in 64KB units
    22                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    23                              <1> memory_detect:
    24 00000535 06                  <1>     push es
    25 00000536 57                  <1>     push di
    26 00000537 6650                <1>     push eax
    27 00000539 6653                <1>     push ebx
    28 0000053B 6651                <1>     push ecx
    29 0000053D 6652                <1>     push edx
    30 0000053F 1E                  <1>     push ds
    31 00000540 0E                  <1>     push cs
    32 00000541 1F                  <1>     pop ds                          ; DS = CS = 0xF000 for BIOS variable writes
    33                              <1> 
    34                              <1>     ; Read extended memory (1MB-16MB) from CMOS 0x17/0x18.
    35 00000542 B017                <1>     mov al, 0x17
    36 00000544 E670                <1>     out 0x70, al
    37 00000546 E471                <1>     in al, 0x71
    38 00000548 0FB6D8              <1>     movzx bx, al               ; Low byte
    39 0000054B B018                <1>     mov al, 0x18
    40 0000054D E670                <1>     out 0x70, al
    41 0000054F E471                <1>     in al, 0x71
    42 00000551 88C4                <1>     mov ah, al
    43 00000553 88D8                <1>     mov al, bl                  ; AX = extended mem in KB (up to 0xFFFF = 65535 KB)
    44 00000555 660FB7C0            <1>     movzx eax, ax
    45 00000559 66C1E00A            <1>     shl eax, 10                 ; Convert KB to bytes
    46 0000055D 6689C2              <1>     mov edx, eax               ; EDX = extended memory bytes (below 16MB portion)
    47                              <1> 
    48                              <1>     ; Read memory above 16MB from CMOS 0x34/0x35 (in 64KB units).
    49 00000560 6652                <1>     push edx
    50 00000562 B034                <1>     mov al, 0x34
    51 00000564 E670                <1>     out 0x70, al
    52 00000566 E471                <1>     in al, 0x71
    53 00000568 0FB6D8              <1>     movzx bx, al
    54 0000056B B035                <1>     mov al, 0x35
    55 0000056D E670                <1>     out 0x70, al
    56 0000056F E471                <1>     in al, 0x71
    57 00000571 88C4                <1>     mov ah, al
    58 00000573 88D8                <1>     mov al, bl                  ; AX = 64KB units above 16MB
    59 00000575 660FB7C0            <1>     movzx eax, ax
    60 00000579 66C1E010            <1>     shl eax, 16                 ; Convert 64KB units to bytes
    61 0000057D 665A                <1>     pop edx
    62                              <1> 
    63                              <1>     ; Total RAM = 1MB + extended + above_16MB.
    64 0000057F 6601D0              <1>     add eax, edx               ; EAX = extended + above_16MB bytes
    65 00000582 660500001000        <1>     add eax, 0x100000          ; Add 1MB base
    66 00000588 66A3[2D05]          <1>     mov [ram_size_bytes], eax
    67 0000058C 66C706[3105]000000- <1>     mov dword [ram_size_bytes + 4], 0
    67 00000594 00                  <1>
    68                              <1> 
    69                              <1>     ; Now build the E820 table.
    70 00000595 BF[6D04]            <1>     mov di, e820_table
    71 00000598 31C9                <1>     xor cx, cx                  ; Entry count
    72                              <1> 
    73                              <1>     ; Entry 0: 0x00000 - 0x9FBFF = usable (639 KB conventional).
    74 0000059A 66C70500000000      <1>     mov dword [di + 0], 0x00000000      ; Base low
    75 000005A1 66C7450400000000    <1>     mov dword [di + 4], 0x00000000      ; Base high
    76 000005A9 66C7450800FC0900    <1>     mov dword [di + 8], 0x0009FC00      ; Length low (639 KB)
    77 000005B1 66C7450C00000000    <1>     mov dword [di + 12], 0x00000000     ; Length high
    78 000005B9 66C7451001000000    <1>     mov dword [di + 16], 1              ; Type: usable
    79 000005C1 66C7451400000000    <1>     mov dword [di + 20], 0              ; ACPI extended
    80 000005C9 83C718              <1>     add di, E820_ENTRY_SIZE
    81 000005CC 41                  <1>     inc cx
    82                              <1> 
    83                              <1>     ; Entry 1: 0x9FC00 - 0x9FFFF = reserved (EBDA, 1 KB).
    84 000005CD 66C70500FC0900      <1>     mov dword [di + 0], 0x0009FC00
    85 000005D4 66C7450400000000    <1>     mov dword [di + 4], 0x00000000
    86 000005DC 66C7450800040000    <1>     mov dword [di + 8], 0x00000400      ; 1 KB
    87 000005E4 66C7450C00000000    <1>     mov dword [di + 12], 0x00000000
    88 000005EC 66C7451002000000    <1>     mov dword [di + 16], 2              ; Type: reserved
    89 000005F4 66C7451400000000    <1>     mov dword [di + 20], 0
    90 000005FC 83C718              <1>     add di, E820_ENTRY_SIZE
    91 000005FF 41                  <1>     inc cx
    92                              <1> 
    93                              <1>     ; Entry 2: 0xE0000 - 0xFFFFF = reserved (BIOS ROM area).
    94 00000600 66C70500000E00      <1>     mov dword [di + 0], 0x000E0000
    95 00000607 66C7450400000000    <1>     mov dword [di + 4], 0x00000000
    96 0000060F 66C7450800000200    <1>     mov dword [di + 8], 0x00020000      ; 128 KB
    97 00000617 66C7450C00000000    <1>     mov dword [di + 12], 0x00000000
    98 0000061F 66C7451002000000    <1>     mov dword [di + 16], 2              ; Type: reserved
    99 00000627 66C7451400000000    <1>     mov dword [di + 20], 0
   100 0000062F 83C718              <1>     add di, E820_ENTRY_SIZE
   101 00000632 41                  <1>     inc cx
   102                              <1> 
   103                              <1>     ; Entry 3: 0x100000 - top_of_ram = usable (extended memory).
   104 00000633 66A1[2D05]          <1>     mov eax, [ram_size_bytes]
   105 00000637 662D00001000        <1>     sub eax, 0x100000                   ; Length = total - 1MB
   106 0000063D 66C70500001000      <1>     mov dword [di + 0], 0x00100000      ; Base: 1 MB
   107 00000644 66C7450400000000    <1>     mov dword [di + 4], 0x00000000
   108 0000064C 66894508            <1>     mov dword [di + 8], eax             ; Length
   109 00000650 66C7450C00000000    <1>     mov dword [di + 12], 0x00000000
   110 00000658 66C7451001000000    <1>     mov dword [di + 16], 1              ; Type: usable
   111 00000660 66C7451400000000    <1>     mov dword [di + 20], 0
   112 00000668 83C718              <1>     add di, E820_ENTRY_SIZE
   113 0000066B 41                  <1>     inc cx
   114                              <1> 
   115                              <1>     ; Entry 4: 0xB0000000 - 0xBFFFFFFF = reserved (PCI MMCONFIG, 256 MB).
   116 0000066C 66C705000000B0      <1>     mov dword [di + 0], 0xB0000000
   117 00000673 66C7450400000000    <1>     mov dword [di + 4], 0x00000000
   118 0000067B 66C7450800000010    <1>     mov dword [di + 8], 0x10000000      ; 256 MB
   119 00000683 66C7450C00000000    <1>     mov dword [di + 12], 0x00000000
   120 0000068B 66C7451002000000    <1>     mov dword [di + 16], 2              ; Type: reserved
   121 00000693 66C7451400000000    <1>     mov dword [di + 20], 0
   122 0000069B 83C718              <1>     add di, E820_ENTRY_SIZE
   123 0000069E 41                  <1>     inc cx
   124                              <1> 
   125                              <1>     ; Entry 5: 0xFEC00000 - 0xFECFFFFF = reserved (IO-APIC, 64 KB).
   126 0000069F 66C7050000C0FE      <1>     mov dword [di + 0], 0xFEC00000
   127 000006A6 66C7450400000000    <1>     mov dword [di + 4], 0x00000000
   128 000006AE 66C7450800000100    <1>     mov dword [di + 8], 0x00010000      ; 64 KB
   129 000006B6 66C7450C00000000    <1>     mov dword [di + 12], 0x00000000
   130 000006BE 66C7451002000000    <1>     mov dword [di + 16], 2              ; Type: reserved
   131 000006C6 66C7451400000000    <1>     mov dword [di + 20], 0
   132 000006CE 83C718              <1>     add di, E820_ENTRY_SIZE
   133 000006D1 41                  <1>     inc cx
   134                              <1> 
   135 000006D2 890E[6B04]          <1>     mov [e820_count], cx
   136                              <1> 
   137 000006D6 1F                  <1>     pop ds
   138 000006D7 665A                <1>     pop edx
   139 000006D9 6659                <1>     pop ecx
   140 000006DB 665B                <1>     pop ebx
   141 000006DD 6658                <1>     pop eax
   142 000006DF 5F                  <1>     pop di
   143 000006E0 07                  <1>     pop es
   144 000006E1 C3                  <1>     ret
    20                                  %include "pic_pit.asm"
     1                              <1> ; =============================================================================
     2                              <1> ; pic_pit.asm — PIC (8259A) remapping and PIT (8254) initialization
     3                              <1> ; =============================================================================
     4                              <1> 
     5                              <1> ; PIC ports.
     6                              <1> PIC1_CMD    equ 0x20
     7                              <1> PIC1_DATA   equ 0x21
     8                              <1> PIC2_CMD    equ 0xA0
     9                              <1> PIC2_DATA   equ 0xA1
    10                              <1> 
    11                              <1> ; PIT ports.
    12                              <1> PIT_CH0     equ 0x40
    13                              <1> PIT_CMD     equ 0x43
    14                              <1> 
    15                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    16                              <1> ; pic_init — Remap the dual 8259A PIC.
    17                              <1> ;   Master: IRQ 0-7  -> INT 08h-0Fh
    18                              <1> ;   Slave:  IRQ 8-15 -> INT 70h-77h
    19                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    20                              <1> pic_init:
    21                              <1>     ; ICW1: edge-triggered, cascade, ICW4 needed.
    22 000006E2 B011                <1>     mov al, 0x11
    23 000006E4 E620                <1>     out PIC1_CMD, al
    24 000006E6 E6A0                <1>     out PIC2_CMD, al
    25                              <1> 
    26                              <1>     ; ICW2: base interrupt vectors.
    27 000006E8 B008                <1>     mov al, 0x08            ; Master: INT 08h
    28 000006EA E621                <1>     out PIC1_DATA, al
    29 000006EC B070                <1>     mov al, 0x70            ; Slave: INT 70h
    30 000006EE E6A1                <1>     out PIC2_DATA, al
    31                              <1> 
    32                              <1>     ; ICW3: cascade wiring.
    33 000006F0 B004                <1>     mov al, 0x04            ; Master: slave on IRQ 2
    34 000006F2 E621                <1>     out PIC1_DATA, al
    35 000006F4 B002                <1>     mov al, 0x02            ; Slave: cascade identity 2
    36 000006F6 E6A1                <1>     out PIC2_DATA, al
    37                              <1> 
    38                              <1>     ; ICW4: 8086 mode, manual EOI.
    39 000006F8 B001                <1>     mov al, 0x01
    40 000006FA E621                <1>     out PIC1_DATA, al
    41 000006FC E6A1                <1>     out PIC2_DATA, al
    42                              <1> 
    43                              <1>     ; OCW1: mask all except IRQ 0 (timer), IRQ 1 (keyboard), IRQ 2 (cascade).
    44 000006FE B0F8                <1>     mov al, 0xF8            ; 1111_1000 = unmask IRQ 0,1,2
    45 00000700 E621                <1>     out PIC1_DATA, al
    46                              <1> 
    47                              <1>     ; Slave: mask all initially.
    48 00000702 B0FF                <1>     mov al, 0xFF
    49 00000704 E6A1                <1>     out PIC2_DATA, al
    50                              <1> 
    51 00000706 C3                  <1>     ret
    52                              <1> 
    53                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    54                              <1> ; pic_unmask_irq14 — Unmask IRQ 14 (IDE primary) on slave PIC.
    55                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    56                              <1> pic_unmask_irq14:
    57 00000707 E4A1                <1>     in al, PIC2_DATA
    58 00000709 24BF                <1>     and al, 0xBF            ; Unmask IRQ 14 (slave bit 6)
    59 0000070B E6A1                <1>     out PIC2_DATA, al
    60                              <1>     ; Also unmask cascade (IRQ 2) on master if not already.
    61 0000070D E421                <1>     in al, PIC1_DATA
    62 0000070F 24FB                <1>     and al, 0xFB            ; Unmask IRQ 2
    63 00000711 E621                <1>     out PIC1_DATA, al
    64 00000713 C3                  <1>     ret
    65                              <1> 
    66                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    67                              <1> ; pit_init — Program PIT channel 0 as rate generator (~18.2 Hz).
    68                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    69                              <1> pit_init:
    70                              <1>     ; Channel 0, access lobyte/hibyte, mode 2 (rate generator), binary.
    71 00000714 B034                <1>     mov al, 0x34            ; 0011_0100
    72 00000716 E643                <1>     out PIT_CMD, al
    73                              <1> 
    74                              <1>     ; Divisor = 65536 (0x0000 wraps to 65536) => 1193182/65536 ~ 18.2 Hz.
    75 00000718 B000                <1>     mov al, 0x00
    76 0000071A E640                <1>     out PIT_CH0, al         ; Low byte
    77 0000071C E640                <1>     out PIT_CH0, al         ; High byte
    78 0000071E C3                  <1>     ret
    21                                  %include "serial.asm"
     1                              <1> ; =============================================================================
     2                              <1> ; serial.asm — COM1 (16550 UART) initialization
     3                              <1> ; =============================================================================
     4                              <1> 
     5                              <1> COM1_BASE   equ 0x03F8
     6                              <1> 
     7                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
     8                              <1> ; serial_init — Initialize COM1 at 9600 baud, 8N1.
     9                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    10                              <1> serial_init:
    11                              <1>     ; Disable interrupts on UART.
    12 0000071F BAF903              <1>     mov dx, COM1_BASE + 1       ; IER
    13 00000722 30C0                <1>     xor al, al
    14 00000724 EE                  <1>     out dx, al
    15                              <1> 
    16                              <1>     ; Enable DLAB (set baud rate divisor).
    17 00000725 BAFB03              <1>     mov dx, COM1_BASE + 3       ; LCR
    18 00000728 B080                <1>     mov al, 0x80
    19 0000072A EE                  <1>     out dx, al
    20                              <1> 
    21                              <1>     ; Set divisor to 12 (9600 baud: 115200 / 9600 = 12).
    22 0000072B BAF803              <1>     mov dx, COM1_BASE + 0       ; DLL (DLAB=1)
    23 0000072E B00C                <1>     mov al, 12
    24 00000730 EE                  <1>     out dx, al
    25 00000731 BAF903              <1>     mov dx, COM1_BASE + 1       ; DLM (DLAB=1)
    26 00000734 30C0                <1>     xor al, al
    27 00000736 EE                  <1>     out dx, al
    28                              <1> 
    29                              <1>     ; 8 bits, no parity, 1 stop bit (clear DLAB).
    30 00000737 BAFB03              <1>     mov dx, COM1_BASE + 3       ; LCR
    31 0000073A B003                <1>     mov al, 0x03
    32 0000073C EE                  <1>     out dx, al
    33                              <1> 
    34                              <1>     ; Enable FIFO, clear buffers, 14-byte threshold.
    35 0000073D BAFA03              <1>     mov dx, COM1_BASE + 2       ; FCR
    36 00000740 B0C7                <1>     mov al, 0xC7
    37 00000742 EE                  <1>     out dx, al
    38                              <1> 
    39                              <1>     ; Set DTR + RTS + OUT2.
    40 00000743 BAFC03              <1>     mov dx, COM1_BASE + 4       ; MCR
    41 00000746 B00B                <1>     mov al, 0x0B
    42 00000748 EE                  <1>     out dx, al
    43                              <1> 
    44 00000749 C3                  <1>     ret
    45                              <1> 
    46                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    47                              <1> ; serial_putchar — Write character in AL to COM1.
    48                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    49                              <1> serial_putchar:
    50 0000074A 52                  <1>     push dx
    51 0000074B 50                  <1>     push ax
    52 0000074C 88C4                <1>     mov ah, al                  ; Save char
    53 0000074E BAFD03              <1>     mov dx, COM1_BASE + 5       ; LSR
    54                              <1> .wait:
    55 00000751 EC                  <1>     in al, dx
    56 00000752 A820                <1>     test al, 0x20               ; THR empty?
    57 00000754 74FB                <1>     jz .wait
    58 00000756 BAF803              <1>     mov dx, COM1_BASE           ; THR
    59 00000759 88E0                <1>     mov al, ah
    60 0000075B EE                  <1>     out dx, al
    61 0000075C 58                  <1>     pop ax
    62 0000075D 5A                  <1>     pop dx
    63 0000075E C3                  <1>     ret
    64                              <1> 
    65                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    66                              <1> ; serial_puts — Write NUL-terminated string at DS:SI to COM1.
    67                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    68                              <1> serial_puts:
    69 0000075F 56                  <1>     push si
    70 00000760 50                  <1>     push ax
    71                              <1> .loop:
    72 00000761 AC                  <1>     lodsb
    73 00000762 84C0                <1>     test al, al
    74 00000764 7405                <1>     jz .done
    75 00000766 E8E1FF              <1>     call serial_putchar
    76 00000769 EBF6                <1>     jmp .loop
    77                              <1> .done:
    78 0000076B 58                  <1>     pop ax
    79 0000076C 5E                  <1>     pop si
    80 0000076D C3                  <1>     ret
    22                                  %include "video_init.asm"
     1                              <1> ; =============================================================================
     2                              <1> ; video_init.asm — VGA text mode initialization and Bochs VBE support
     3                              <1> ; =============================================================================
     4                              <1> 
     5                              <1> VGA_TEXT_SEG    equ 0xB800
     6                              <1> VGA_CRTC_ADDR  equ 0x03D4
     7                              <1> VGA_CRTC_DATA  equ 0x03D5
     8                              <1> VGA_STATUS     equ 0x03DA
     9                              <1> 
    10                              <1> ; Bochs VBE I/O ports.
    11                              <1> VBE_DISPI_PORT_INDEX    equ 0x01CE
    12                              <1> VBE_DISPI_PORT_DATA     equ 0x01CF
    13                              <1> 
    14                              <1> ; Bochs VBE register indices.
    15                              <1> VBE_DISPI_INDEX_ID          equ 0
    16                              <1> VBE_DISPI_INDEX_XRES        equ 1
    17                              <1> VBE_DISPI_INDEX_YRES        equ 2
    18                              <1> VBE_DISPI_INDEX_BPP         equ 3
    19                              <1> VBE_DISPI_INDEX_ENABLE      equ 4
    20                              <1> VBE_DISPI_INDEX_BANK        equ 5
    21                              <1> VBE_DISPI_INDEX_VIRT_WIDTH  equ 6
    22                              <1> VBE_DISPI_INDEX_VIRT_HEIGHT equ 7
    23                              <1> VBE_DISPI_INDEX_X_OFFSET    equ 8
    24                              <1> VBE_DISPI_INDEX_Y_OFFSET    equ 9
    25                              <1> 
    26                              <1> ; VBE enable flags.
    27                              <1> VBE_DISPI_DISABLED          equ 0x00
    28                              <1> VBE_DISPI_ENABLED           equ 0x01
    29                              <1> VBE_DISPI_LFB_ENABLED      equ 0x40
    30                              <1> 
    31                              <1> ; LFB base address (VGA PCI BAR0).
    32                              <1> VGA_LFB_BASE    equ 0xFD000000
    33                              <1> 
    34                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    35                              <1> ; video_init — Set VGA mode 03h (80x25 text, 16 colors) and clear screen.
    36                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    37                              <1> video_init:
    38 0000076E 06                  <1>     push es
    39 0000076F 57                  <1>     push di
    40 00000770 51                  <1>     push cx
    41 00000771 50                  <1>     push ax
    42                              <1> 
    43                              <1>     ; Reset attribute controller flip-flop.
    44 00000772 BADA03              <1>     mov dx, VGA_STATUS
    45 00000775 EC                  <1>     in al, dx
    46                              <1> 
    47                              <1>     ; Disable Bochs VBE (ensure we're in text mode).
    48 00000776 BACE01              <1>     mov dx, VBE_DISPI_PORT_INDEX
    49 00000779 B80400              <1>     mov ax, VBE_DISPI_INDEX_ENABLE
    50 0000077C EF                  <1>     out dx, ax
    51 0000077D BACF01              <1>     mov dx, VBE_DISPI_PORT_DATA
    52 00000780 B80000              <1>     mov ax, VBE_DISPI_DISABLED
    53 00000783 EF                  <1>     out dx, ax
    54                              <1> 
    55                              <1>     ; Clear the text-mode framebuffer (80*25 = 2000 cells).
    56 00000784 B800B8              <1>     mov ax, VGA_TEXT_SEG
    57 00000787 8EC0                <1>     mov es, ax
    58 00000789 31FF                <1>     xor di, di
    59 0000078B B82007              <1>     mov ax, 0x0720          ; Space, light gray on black
    60 0000078E B9D007              <1>     mov cx, 2000
    61 00000791 F3AB                <1>     rep stosw
    62                              <1> 
    63                              <1>     ; Set cursor position to (0, 0).
    64 00000793 BAD403              <1>     mov dx, VGA_CRTC_ADDR
    65 00000796 B00E                <1>     mov al, 0x0E            ; Cursor location high
    66 00000798 EE                  <1>     out dx, al
    67 00000799 BAD503              <1>     mov dx, VGA_CRTC_DATA
    68 0000079C 30C0                <1>     xor al, al
    69 0000079E EE                  <1>     out dx, al
    70 0000079F BAD403              <1>     mov dx, VGA_CRTC_ADDR
    71 000007A2 B00F                <1>     mov al, 0x0F            ; Cursor location low
    72 000007A4 EE                  <1>     out dx, al
    73 000007A5 BAD503              <1>     mov dx, VGA_CRTC_DATA
    74 000007A8 30C0                <1>     xor al, al
    75 000007AA EE                  <1>     out dx, al
    76                              <1> 
    77                              <1>     ; Enable cursor (scanlines 6-7).
    78 000007AB BAD403              <1>     mov dx, VGA_CRTC_ADDR
    79 000007AE B00A                <1>     mov al, 0x0A            ; Cursor start
    80 000007B0 EE                  <1>     out dx, al
    81 000007B1 BAD503              <1>     mov dx, VGA_CRTC_DATA
    82 000007B4 B006                <1>     mov al, 0x06
    83 000007B6 EE                  <1>     out dx, al
    84 000007B7 BAD403              <1>     mov dx, VGA_CRTC_ADDR
    85 000007BA B00B                <1>     mov al, 0x0B            ; Cursor end
    86 000007BC EE                  <1>     out dx, al
    87 000007BD BAD503              <1>     mov dx, VGA_CRTC_DATA
    88 000007C0 B007                <1>     mov al, 0x07
    89 000007C2 EE                  <1>     out dx, al
    90                              <1> 
    91 000007C3 58                  <1>     pop ax
    92 000007C4 59                  <1>     pop cx
    93 000007C5 5F                  <1>     pop di
    94 000007C6 07                  <1>     pop es
    95 000007C7 C3                  <1>     ret
    96                              <1> 
    97                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    98                              <1> ; vbe_set_mode — Set a Bochs VBE linear framebuffer mode.
    99                              <1> ;   Input: BX = width, CX = height, DL = bpp (bits per pixel)
   100                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   101                              <1> vbe_set_mode:
   102 000007C8 50                  <1>     push ax
   103 000007C9 52                  <1>     push dx
   104                              <1> 
   105                              <1>     ; Disable VBE first.
   106 000007CA 52                  <1>     push dx
   107 000007CB BACE01              <1>     mov dx, VBE_DISPI_PORT_INDEX
   108 000007CE B80400              <1>     mov ax, VBE_DISPI_INDEX_ENABLE
   109 000007D1 EF                  <1>     out dx, ax
   110 000007D2 BACF01              <1>     mov dx, VBE_DISPI_PORT_DATA
   111 000007D5 B80000              <1>     mov ax, VBE_DISPI_DISABLED
   112 000007D8 EF                  <1>     out dx, ax
   113 000007D9 5A                  <1>     pop dx
   114                              <1> 
   115                              <1>     ; Set X resolution.
   116 000007DA 52                  <1>     push dx
   117 000007DB BACE01              <1>     mov dx, VBE_DISPI_PORT_INDEX
   118 000007DE B80100              <1>     mov ax, VBE_DISPI_INDEX_XRES
   119 000007E1 EF                  <1>     out dx, ax
   120 000007E2 BACF01              <1>     mov dx, VBE_DISPI_PORT_DATA
   121 000007E5 89D8                <1>     mov ax, bx
   122 000007E7 EF                  <1>     out dx, ax
   123 000007E8 5A                  <1>     pop dx
   124                              <1> 
   125                              <1>     ; Set Y resolution.
   126 000007E9 52                  <1>     push dx
   127 000007EA BACE01              <1>     mov dx, VBE_DISPI_PORT_INDEX
   128 000007ED B80200              <1>     mov ax, VBE_DISPI_INDEX_YRES
   129 000007F0 EF                  <1>     out dx, ax
   130 000007F1 BACF01              <1>     mov dx, VBE_DISPI_PORT_DATA
   131 000007F4 89C8                <1>     mov ax, cx
   132 000007F6 EF                  <1>     out dx, ax
   133 000007F7 5A                  <1>     pop dx
   134                              <1> 
   135                              <1>     ; Set BPP.
   136 000007F8 52                  <1>     push dx
   137 000007F9 0FB6C2              <1>     movzx ax, dl
   138 000007FC 50                  <1>     push ax
   139 000007FD BACE01              <1>     mov dx, VBE_DISPI_PORT_INDEX
   140 00000800 B80300              <1>     mov ax, VBE_DISPI_INDEX_BPP
   141 00000803 EF                  <1>     out dx, ax
   142 00000804 BACF01              <1>     mov dx, VBE_DISPI_PORT_DATA
   143 00000807 58                  <1>     pop ax
   144 00000808 EF                  <1>     out dx, ax
   145 00000809 5A                  <1>     pop dx
   146                              <1> 
   147                              <1>     ; Enable VBE with LFB.
   148 0000080A 52                  <1>     push dx
   149 0000080B BACE01              <1>     mov dx, VBE_DISPI_PORT_INDEX
   150 0000080E B80400              <1>     mov ax, VBE_DISPI_INDEX_ENABLE
   151 00000811 EF                  <1>     out dx, ax
   152 00000812 BACF01              <1>     mov dx, VBE_DISPI_PORT_DATA
   153 00000815 B84100              <1>     mov ax, VBE_DISPI_ENABLED | VBE_DISPI_LFB_ENABLED
   154 00000818 EF                  <1>     out dx, ax
   155 00000819 5A                  <1>     pop dx
   156                              <1> 
   157 0000081A 5A                  <1>     pop dx
   158 0000081B 58                  <1>     pop ax
   159 0000081C C3                  <1>     ret

     1                              <1> ; =============================================================================
     2                              <1> ; pci.asm — PCI bus enumeration via Type 1 configuration (CF8/CFC)
     3                              <1> ; =============================================================================
     4                              <1> 
     5                              <1> PCI_CONFIG_ADDR equ 0x0CF8
     6                              <1> PCI_CONFIG_DATA equ 0x0CFC
     7                              <1> 
     8                              <1> ; PCI device table (filled during enumeration).
     9                              <1> ; Each entry: bus(1) + dev(1) + func(1) + pad(1) + vendor(2) + device(2)
    10                              <1> ;           + class(1) + subclass(1) + progif(1) + hdrtype(1) = 12 bytes
    11                              <1> PCI_TABLE_MAX   equ 16
    12                              <1> PCI_ENTRY_SIZE  equ 12
    13                              <1> 
    14 0000081D 0000                <1> pci_device_count:   dw 0
    15 0000081F 00<rep C0h>         <1> pci_device_table:   times (PCI_TABLE_MAX * PCI_ENTRY_SIZE) db 0
    16                              <1> 
    17                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    18                              <1> ; pci_config_read32 — Read a 32-bit PCI config register.
    19                              <1> ;   Input:  EAX = config address (with enable bit set)
    20                              <1> ;   Output: EAX = 32-bit value
    21                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    22                              <1> pci_config_read32:
    23 000008DF 52                  <1>     push dx
    24 000008E0 BAF80C              <1>     mov dx, PCI_CONFIG_ADDR
    25 000008E3 66EF                <1>     out dx, eax
    26 000008E5 BAFC0C              <1>     mov dx, PCI_CONFIG_DATA
    27 000008E8 66ED                <1>     in eax, dx
    28 000008EA 5A                  <1>     pop dx
    29 000008EB C3                  <1>     ret
    30                              <1> 
    31                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    32                              <1> ; pci_config_write32 — Write a 32-bit PCI config register.
    33                              <1> ;   Input:  EAX = config address, ECX = value to write
    34                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    35                              <1> pci_config_write32:
    36 000008EC 52                  <1>     push dx
    37 000008ED BAF80C              <1>     mov dx, PCI_CONFIG_ADDR
    38 000008F0 66EF                <1>     out dx, eax
    39 000008F2 BAFC0C              <1>     mov dx, PCI_CONFIG_DATA
    40 000008F5 6689C8              <1>     mov eax, ecx
    41 000008F8 66EF                <1>     out dx, eax
    42 000008FA 5A                  <1>     pop dx
    43 000008FB C3                  <1>     ret
    44                              <1> 
    45                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    46                              <1> ; pci_make_addr — Build a PCI config address.
    47                              <1> ;   Input:  BH = bus, BL[7:3] = device, BL[2:0] = function, DI = register
    48                              <1> ;   Output: EAX = config address with enable bit
    49                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    50                              <1> pci_make_addr:
    51 000008FC 6651                <1>     push ecx
    52 000008FE 660FB6C7            <1>     movzx eax, bh              ; Bus
    53 00000902 66C1E010            <1>     shl eax, 16
    54 00000906 660FB6CB            <1>     movzx ecx, bl
    55 0000090A C0E903              <1>     shr cl, 3
    56 0000090D 80E11F              <1>     and cl, 0x1F               ; Device (5 bits)
    57 00000910 66C1E10B            <1>     shl ecx, 11
    58 00000914 6609C8              <1>     or eax, ecx
    59 00000917 660FB6CB            <1>     movzx ecx, bl
    60 0000091B 80E107              <1>     and cl, 0x07               ; Function (3 bits)
    61 0000091E 66C1E108            <1>     shl ecx, 8
    62 00000922 6609C8              <1>     or eax, ecx
    63 00000925 660FB7CF            <1>     movzx ecx, di
    64 00000929 81E1FC00            <1>     and cx, 0x00FC             ; Register (aligned)
    65 0000092D 6609C8              <1>     or eax, ecx
    66 00000930 660D00000080        <1>     or eax, 0x80000000         ; Enable bit
    67 00000936 6659                <1>     pop ecx
    68 00000938 C3                  <1>     ret
    69                              <1> 
    70                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    71                              <1> ; pci_enumerate — Scan PCI bus 0 and fill pci_device_table.
    72                              <1> ;   Output: [pci_device_count] = number of devices found
    73                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    74                              <1> pci_enumerate:
    75 00000939 6650                <1>     push eax
    76 0000093B 6653                <1>     push ebx
    77 0000093D 6651                <1>     push ecx
    78 0000093F 6652                <1>     push edx
    79 00000941 6657                <1>     push edi
    80 00000943 56                  <1>     push si
    81 00000944 1E                  <1>     push ds
    82 00000945 0E                  <1>     push cs
    83 00000946 1F                  <1>     pop ds                          ; DS = CS = 0xF000 for BIOS variable writes
    84                              <1> 
    85 00000947 BE[1F08]            <1>     mov si, pci_device_table
    86 0000094A 31C9                <1>     xor cx, cx                  ; Device count = 0
    87 0000094C 30FF                <1>     xor bh, bh                 ; Bus 0
    88                              <1> 
    89 0000094E C606[5B0A]00        <1>     mov byte [.cur_dev], 0
    90                              <1> .dev_loop:
    91 00000953 803E[5B0A]20        <1>     cmp byte [.cur_dev], 32
    92 00000958 0F8DEE00            <1>     jge .done
    93                              <1> 
    94                              <1>     ; Build BL = (device << 3) | function 0.
    95 0000095C A0[5B0A]            <1>     mov al, [.cur_dev]
    96 0000095F C0E003              <1>     shl al, 3
    97 00000962 88C3                <1>     mov bl, al                  ; BL = dev<<3 | func=0
    98                              <1> 
    99                              <1>     ; Read vendor/device ID (register 0x00).
   100 00000964 31FF                <1>     xor di, di
   101 00000966 E893FF              <1>     call pci_make_addr
   102 00000969 E873FF              <1>     call pci_config_read32
   103 0000096C 83F8FF              <1>     cmp ax, 0xFFFF              ; No device?
   104 0000096F 0F84D000            <1>     je .next_dev
   105 00000973 83F800              <1>     cmp ax, 0x0000              ; Invalid?
   106 00000976 0F84C900            <1>     je .next_dev
   107                              <1> 
   108                              <1>     ; Device found. Check if multi-function.
   109 0000097A 6650                <1>     push eax                    ; Save vendor/device
   110 0000097C BF0C00              <1>     mov di, 0x0C                ; Register 0x0C (header type at byte 0x0E)
   111 0000097F E87AFF              <1>     call pci_make_addr
   112 00000982 E85AFF              <1>     call pci_config_read32
   113 00000985 66C1E810            <1>     shr eax, 16                 ; AL = header type, AH = BIST
   114 00000989 88C2                <1>     mov dl, al                  ; DL = header type
   115 0000098B 6658                <1>     pop eax                     ; Restore vendor/device
   116                              <1> 
   117                              <1>     ; Enumerate functions.
   118 0000098D C606[5C0A]00        <1>     mov byte [.cur_func], 0
   119 00000992 F6C280              <1>     test dl, 0x80               ; Multi-function?
   120 00000995 750C                <1>     jnz .func_loop
   121                              <1>     ; Single function: just store function 0.
   122 00000997 C606[5D0A]01        <1>     mov byte [.max_func], 1
   123 0000099C EB05                <1>     jmp .func_loop
   124                              <1> .multi_func:
   125 0000099E C606[5D0A]08        <1>     mov byte [.max_func], 8
   126                              <1> .func_loop:
   127 000009A3 803E[5C0A]08        <1>     cmp byte [.cur_func], 8
   128 000009A8 0F8D9700            <1>     jge .next_dev
   129 000009AC F6C280              <1>     test dl, 0x80
   130 000009AF 7402                <1>     jz .check_max_func
   131 000009B1 EB09                <1>     jmp .do_func
   132                              <1> .check_max_func:
   133 000009B3 803E[5C0A]01        <1>     cmp byte [.cur_func], 1
   134 000009B8 0F8D8700            <1>     jge .next_dev
   135                              <1> 
   136                              <1> .do_func:
   137                              <1>     ; Build BL for this function.
   138 000009BC A0[5B0A]            <1>     mov al, [.cur_dev]
   139 000009BF C0E003              <1>     shl al, 3
   140 000009C2 0A06[5C0A]          <1>     or al, [.cur_func]
   141 000009C6 88C3                <1>     mov bl, al
   142                              <1> 
   143                              <1>     ; Read vendor/device ID.
   144 000009C8 31FF                <1>     xor di, di
   145 000009CA E82FFF              <1>     call pci_make_addr
   146 000009CD E80FFF              <1>     call pci_config_read32
   147 000009D0 83F8FF              <1>     cmp ax, 0xFFFF
   148 000009D3 7467                <1>     je .next_func
   149 000009D5 83F800              <1>     cmp ax, 0x0000
   150 000009D8 7462                <1>     je .next_func
   151                              <1> 
   152                              <1>     ; Store entry if table not full.
   153 000009DA 83F910              <1>     cmp cx, PCI_TABLE_MAX
   154 000009DD 7D5D                <1>     jge .next_func
   155                              <1> 
   156 000009DF 6650                <1>     push eax
   157                              <1>     ; Store bus, dev, func.
   158 000009E1 883C                <1>     mov byte [si + 0], bh      ; Bus
   159 000009E3 A0[5B0A]            <1>     mov al, [.cur_dev]
   160 000009E6 884401              <1>     mov byte [si + 1], al      ; Device
   161 000009E9 A0[5C0A]            <1>     mov al, [.cur_func]
   162 000009EC 884402              <1>     mov byte [si + 2], al      ; Function
   163 000009EF C6440300            <1>     mov byte [si + 3], 0       ; Padding
   164 000009F3 6658                <1>     pop eax
   165                              <1>     ; Store vendor/device ID.
   166 000009F5 894404              <1>     mov [si + 4], ax            ; Vendor ID (low 16)
   167 000009F8 66C1E810            <1>     shr eax, 16
   168 000009FC 894406              <1>     mov [si + 6], ax            ; Device ID (high 16)
   169                              <1> 
   170                              <1>     ; Read class code (register 0x08).
   171 000009FF 6651                <1>     push ecx
   172 00000A01 BF0800              <1>     mov di, 0x08
   173 00000A04 E8F5FE              <1>     call pci_make_addr
   174 00000A07 E8D5FE              <1>     call pci_config_read32
   175 00000A0A 884408              <1>     mov [si + 8], al            ; Revision ID -> we store class info
   176 00000A0D 66C1E808            <1>     shr eax, 8
   177 00000A11 884408              <1>     mov [si + 8], al            ; Prog IF
   178 00000A14 66C1E808            <1>     shr eax, 8
   179 00000A18 884409              <1>     mov [si + 9], al            ; Subclass
   180 00000A1B 66C1E808            <1>     shr eax, 8
   181 00000A1F 88440A              <1>     mov [si + 10], al           ; Class code
   182 00000A22 6659                <1>     pop ecx
   183                              <1> 
   184                              <1>     ; Read header type.
   185 00000A24 6651                <1>     push ecx
   186 00000A26 BF0C00              <1>     mov di, 0x0C
   187 00000A29 E8D0FE              <1>     call pci_make_addr
   188 00000A2C E8B0FE              <1>     call pci_config_read32
   189 00000A2F 66C1E810            <1>     shr eax, 16
   190 00000A33 88440B              <1>     mov [si + 11], al           ; Header type
   191 00000A36 6659                <1>     pop ecx
   192                              <1> 
   193 00000A38 83C60C              <1>     add si, PCI_ENTRY_SIZE
   194 00000A3B 41                  <1>     inc cx
   195                              <1> 
   196                              <1> .next_func:
   197 00000A3C FE06[5C0A]          <1>     inc byte [.cur_func]
   198 00000A40 E960FF              <1>     jmp .func_loop
   199                              <1> 
   200                              <1> .next_dev:
   201 00000A43 FE06[5B0A]          <1>     inc byte [.cur_dev]
   202 00000A47 E909FF              <1>     jmp .dev_loop
   203                              <1> 
   204                              <1> .done:
   205 00000A4A 890E[1D08]          <1>     mov [pci_device_count], cx
   206                              <1> 
   207 00000A4E 1F                  <1>     pop ds
   208 00000A4F 5E                  <1>     pop si
   209 00000A50 665F                <1>     pop edi
   210 00000A52 665A                <1>     pop edx
   211 00000A54 6659                <1>     pop ecx
   212 00000A56 665B                <1>     pop ebx
   213 00000A58 6658                <1>     pop eax
   214 00000A5A C3                  <1>     ret
   215                              <1> 
   216                              <1> ; Local variables (in BIOS ROM area — read-only issue, use stack or BDA instead).
   217                              <1> ; We place these as mutable data in the BIOS image; since the BIOS is loaded
   218                              <1> ; into RAM (not actual ROM), writes work fine.
   219 00000A5B 00                  <1> .cur_dev:   db 0
   220 00000A5C 00                  <1> .cur_func:  db 0
   221 00000A5D 00                  <1> .max_func:  db 0
    24                                  %include "ide.asm"
     1                              <1> ; =============================================================================
     2                              <1> ; ide.asm — IDE/ATA drive detection (master + slave)
     3                              <1> ; =============================================================================
     4                              <1> 
     5                              <1> ; IDE primary channel ports.
     6                              <1> IDE_DATA        equ 0x01F0
     7                              <1> IDE_ERROR       equ 0x01F1
     8                              <1> IDE_SEC_COUNT   equ 0x01F2
     9                              <1> IDE_LBA_LO      equ 0x01F3
    10                              <1> IDE_LBA_MID     equ 0x01F4
    11                              <1> IDE_LBA_HI      equ 0x01F5
    12                              <1> IDE_DRIVE_HEAD  equ 0x01F6
    13                              <1> IDE_STATUS      equ 0x01F7
    14                              <1> IDE_CMD         equ 0x01F7
    15                              <1> IDE_ALT_STATUS  equ 0x03F6
    16                              <1> 
    17                              <1> ; IDE status bits.
    18                              <1> IDE_SR_BSY      equ 0x80
    19                              <1> IDE_SR_DRDY     equ 0x40
    20                              <1> IDE_SR_DRQ      equ 0x08
    21                              <1> IDE_SR_ERR      equ 0x01
    22                              <1> 
    23                              <1> ; IDE commands.
    24                              <1> IDE_CMD_IDENTIFY        equ 0xEC
    25                              <1> IDE_CMD_READ_SECTORS    equ 0x20
    26                              <1> IDE_CMD_READ_SECTORS_EXT equ 0x24
    27                              <1> 
    28                              <1> ; Master drive parameter storage.
    29 00000A5E 00                  <1> ide_master_present: db 0
    30 00000A5F 0000                <1> ide_master_cyls:    dw 0
    31 00000A61 0000                <1> ide_master_heads:   dw 0
    32 00000A63 0000                <1> ide_master_spt:     dw 0        ; Sectors per track
    33 00000A65 00000000            <1> ide_master_lba28:   dd 0        ; Total LBA28 sectors
    34 00000A69 0000000000000000    <1> ide_master_lba48:   dd 0, 0     ; Total LBA48 sectors (64-bit)
    35                              <1> 
    36                              <1> ; Slave drive parameter storage.
    37 00000A71 00                  <1> ide_slave_present:  db 0
    38 00000A72 0000                <1> ide_slave_cyls:     dw 0
    39 00000A74 0000                <1> ide_slave_heads:    dw 0
    40 00000A76 0000                <1> ide_slave_spt:      dw 0
    41 00000A78 00000000            <1> ide_slave_lba28:    dd 0
    42 00000A7C 0000000000000000    <1> ide_slave_lba48:    dd 0, 0
    43                              <1> 
    44                              <1> ; Drive select byte for ide_read_sectors / ide_write_sectors.
    45                              <1> ; 0xE0 = master+LBA, 0xF0 = slave+LBA.
    46 00000A84 E0                  <1> ide_drive_sel:      db 0xE0
    47                              <1> 
    48                              <1> ; 512-byte IDENTIFY buffer (temporarily used during POST).
    49 00000A85 90                  <1> align 2
    50 00000A86 0000<rep 100h>      <1> ide_identify_buf:   times 256 dw 0
    51                              <1> 
    52                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    53                              <1> ; ide_detect — Detect IDE master and slave drives on primary channel.
    54                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    55                              <1> ide_detect:
    56 00000C86 6650                <1>     push eax
    57 00000C88 6653                <1>     push ebx
    58 00000C8A 6651                <1>     push ecx
    59 00000C8C 6652                <1>     push edx
    60 00000C8E 57                  <1>     push di
    61 00000C8F 06                  <1>     push es
    62                              <1> 
    63                              <1>     ; ── Detect master drive ──────────────────────────────────────────────
    64                              <1> 
    65                              <1>     ; Select master drive.
    66 00000C90 BAF601              <1>     mov dx, IDE_DRIVE_HEAD
    67 00000C93 B0A0                <1>     mov al, 0xA0            ; Master, LBA mode off
    68 00000C95 EE                  <1>     out dx, al
    69                              <1> 
    70                              <1>     ; Small delay: read alt status a few times.
    71 00000C96 BAF603              <1>     mov dx, IDE_ALT_STATUS
    72 00000C99 EC                  <1>     in al, dx
    73 00000C9A EC                  <1>     in al, dx
    74 00000C9B EC                  <1>     in al, dx
    75 00000C9C EC                  <1>     in al, dx
    76                              <1> 
    77                              <1>     ; Check status — if 0xFF, no drive.
    78 00000C9D BAF701              <1>     mov dx, IDE_STATUS
    79 00000CA0 EC                  <1>     in al, dx
    80 00000CA1 3CFF                <1>     cmp al, 0xFF
    81 00000CA3 0F84A100            <1>     je .no_master
    82 00000CA7 84C0                <1>     test al, al
    83 00000CA9 0F849B00            <1>     jz .no_master
    84                              <1> 
    85                              <1>     ; Send IDENTIFY command.
    86 00000CAD BAF201              <1>     mov dx, IDE_SEC_COUNT
    87 00000CB0 30C0                <1>     xor al, al
    88 00000CB2 EE                  <1>     out dx, al
    89 00000CB3 BAF301              <1>     mov dx, IDE_LBA_LO
    90 00000CB6 EE                  <1>     out dx, al
    91 00000CB7 BAF401              <1>     mov dx, IDE_LBA_MID
    92 00000CBA EE                  <1>     out dx, al
    93 00000CBB BAF501              <1>     mov dx, IDE_LBA_HI
    94 00000CBE EE                  <1>     out dx, al
    95 00000CBF BAF701              <1>     mov dx, IDE_CMD
    96 00000CC2 B0EC                <1>     mov al, IDE_CMD_IDENTIFY
    97 00000CC4 EE                  <1>     out dx, al
    98                              <1> 
    99                              <1>     ; Wait for BSY to clear.
   100 00000CC5 BAF701              <1>     mov dx, IDE_STATUS
   101 00000CC8 B9FFFF              <1>     mov cx, 0xFFFF
   102                              <1> .wait_bsy_m:
   103 00000CCB EC                  <1>     in al, dx
   104 00000CCC A880                <1>     test al, IDE_SR_BSY
   105 00000CCE 7405                <1>     jz .bsy_clear_m
   106 00000CD0 49                  <1>     dec cx
   107 00000CD1 75F8                <1>     jnz .wait_bsy_m
   108 00000CD3 EB73                <1>     jmp .no_master           ; Timeout
   109                              <1> .bsy_clear_m:
   110                              <1>     ; Check that LBA_MID and LBA_HI are still 0 (not ATAPI).
   111 00000CD5 BAF401              <1>     mov dx, IDE_LBA_MID
   112 00000CD8 EC                  <1>     in al, dx
   113 00000CD9 84C0                <1>     test al, al
   114 00000CDB 756B                <1>     jnz .no_master           ; ATAPI or SATA signature
   115 00000CDD BAF501              <1>     mov dx, IDE_LBA_HI
   116 00000CE0 EC                  <1>     in al, dx
   117 00000CE1 84C0                <1>     test al, al
   118 00000CE3 7563                <1>     jnz .no_master
   119                              <1> 
   120                              <1>     ; Wait for DRQ.
   121 00000CE5 BAF701              <1>     mov dx, IDE_STATUS
   122 00000CE8 B9FFFF              <1>     mov cx, 0xFFFF
   123                              <1> .wait_drq_m:
   124 00000CEB EC                  <1>     in al, dx
   125 00000CEC A801                <1>     test al, IDE_SR_ERR
   126 00000CEE 7558                <1>     jnz .no_master
   127 00000CF0 A808                <1>     test al, IDE_SR_DRQ
   128 00000CF2 7505                <1>     jnz .drq_set_m
   129 00000CF4 49                  <1>     dec cx
   130 00000CF5 75F4                <1>     jnz .wait_drq_m
   131 00000CF7 EB4F                <1>     jmp .no_master
   132                              <1> .drq_set_m:
   133                              <1>     ; Read 256 words of IDENTIFY data.
   134 00000CF9 BAF001              <1>     mov dx, IDE_DATA
   135 00000CFC BF[860A]            <1>     mov di, ide_identify_buf
   136 00000CFF 1E                  <1>     push ds
   137 00000D00 07                  <1>     pop es
   138 00000D01 B90001              <1>     mov cx, 256
   139 00000D04 F36D                <1>     rep insw
   140                              <1> 
   141                              <1>     ; Parse IDENTIFY data.  Store results in the BIOS ROM segment (cs:) so
   142                              <1>     ; they survive when the boot image reuses low RAM for its own data.
   143                              <1>     ; Word 1: cylinders.
   144 00000D06 A1[880A]            <1>     mov ax, [ide_identify_buf + 2]
   145 00000D09 2EA3[5F0A]          <1>     mov [cs:ide_master_cyls], ax
   146                              <1>     ; Word 3: heads.
   147 00000D0D A1[8C0A]            <1>     mov ax, [ide_identify_buf + 6]
   148 00000D10 2EA3[610A]          <1>     mov [cs:ide_master_heads], ax
   149                              <1>     ; Word 6: sectors per track.
   150 00000D14 A1[920A]            <1>     mov ax, [ide_identify_buf + 12]
   151 00000D17 2EA3[630A]          <1>     mov [cs:ide_master_spt], ax
   152                              <1>     ; Words 60-61: total LBA28 sectors.
   153 00000D1B 66A1[FE0A]          <1>     mov eax, [ide_identify_buf + 120]
   154 00000D1F 2E66A3[650A]        <1>     mov [cs:ide_master_lba28], eax
   155                              <1>     ; Words 100-103: total LBA48 sectors (if supported).
   156 00000D24 66A1[4E0B]          <1>     mov eax, [ide_identify_buf + 200]
   157 00000D28 2E66A3[690A]        <1>     mov [cs:ide_master_lba48], eax
   158 00000D2D 66A1[520B]          <1>     mov eax, [ide_identify_buf + 204]
   159 00000D31 2E66A3[6D0A]        <1>     mov [cs:ide_master_lba48 + 4], eax
   160                              <1> 
   161 00000D36 2EC606[5E0A]01      <1>     mov byte [cs:ide_master_present], 1
   162                              <1> 
   163                              <1>     ; Update BDA: number of hard disks.
   164 00000D3C 31C0                <1>     xor ax, ax
   165 00000D3E 8EC0                <1>     mov es, ax
   166 00000D40 26C606750401        <1>     mov byte [es:BDA_NUM_HD], 1
   167                              <1> 
   168 00000D46 EB06                <1>     jmp .detect_slave
   169                              <1> 
   170                              <1> .no_master:
   171 00000D48 2EC606[5E0A]00      <1>     mov byte [cs:ide_master_present], 0
   172                              <1>     ; BDA_NUM_HD stays 0.
   173                              <1> 
   174                              <1>     ; ── Detect slave drive ───────────────────────────────────────────────
   175                              <1> 
   176                              <1> .detect_slave:
   177                              <1>     ; Select slave drive.
   178 00000D4E BAF601              <1>     mov dx, IDE_DRIVE_HEAD
   179 00000D51 B0B0                <1>     mov al, 0xB0            ; Slave, LBA mode off
   180 00000D53 EE                  <1>     out dx, al
   181                              <1> 
   182                              <1>     ; Small delay.
   183 00000D54 BAF603              <1>     mov dx, IDE_ALT_STATUS
   184 00000D57 EC                  <1>     in al, dx
   185 00000D58 EC                  <1>     in al, dx
   186 00000D59 EC                  <1>     in al, dx
   187 00000D5A EC                  <1>     in al, dx
   188                              <1> 
   189                              <1>     ; Check status.
   190 00000D5B BAF701              <1>     mov dx, IDE_STATUS
   191 00000D5E EC                  <1>     in al, dx
   192 00000D5F 3CFF                <1>     cmp al, 0xFF
   193 00000D61 0F84A000            <1>     je .no_slave
   194 00000D65 84C0                <1>     test al, al
   195 00000D67 0F849A00            <1>     jz .no_slave
   196                              <1> 
   197                              <1>     ; Send IDENTIFY command.
   198 00000D6B BAF201              <1>     mov dx, IDE_SEC_COUNT
   199 00000D6E 30C0                <1>     xor al, al
   200 00000D70 EE                  <1>     out dx, al
   201 00000D71 BAF301              <1>     mov dx, IDE_LBA_LO
   202 00000D74 EE                  <1>     out dx, al
   203 00000D75 BAF401              <1>     mov dx, IDE_LBA_MID
   204 00000D78 EE                  <1>     out dx, al
   205 00000D79 BAF501              <1>     mov dx, IDE_LBA_HI
   206 00000D7C EE                  <1>     out dx, al
   207 00000D7D BAF701              <1>     mov dx, IDE_CMD
   208 00000D80 B0EC                <1>     mov al, IDE_CMD_IDENTIFY
   209 00000D82 EE                  <1>     out dx, al
   210                              <1> 
   211                              <1>     ; Wait for BSY to clear.
   212 00000D83 BAF701              <1>     mov dx, IDE_STATUS
   213 00000D86 B9FFFF              <1>     mov cx, 0xFFFF
   214                              <1> .wait_bsy_s:
   215 00000D89 EC                  <1>     in al, dx
   216 00000D8A A880                <1>     test al, IDE_SR_BSY
   217 00000D8C 7405                <1>     jz .bsy_clear_s
   218 00000D8E 49                  <1>     dec cx
   219 00000D8F 75F8                <1>     jnz .wait_bsy_s
   220 00000D91 EB72                <1>     jmp .no_slave
   221                              <1> .bsy_clear_s:
   222 00000D93 BAF401              <1>     mov dx, IDE_LBA_MID
   223 00000D96 EC                  <1>     in al, dx
   224 00000D97 84C0                <1>     test al, al
   225 00000D99 756A                <1>     jnz .no_slave
   226 00000D9B BAF501              <1>     mov dx, IDE_LBA_HI
   227 00000D9E EC                  <1>     in al, dx
   228 00000D9F 84C0                <1>     test al, al
   229 00000DA1 7562                <1>     jnz .no_slave
   230                              <1> 
   231                              <1>     ; Wait for DRQ.
   232 00000DA3 BAF701              <1>     mov dx, IDE_STATUS
   233 00000DA6 B9FFFF              <1>     mov cx, 0xFFFF
   234                              <1> .wait_drq_s:
   235 00000DA9 EC                  <1>     in al, dx
   236 00000DAA A801                <1>     test al, IDE_SR_ERR
   237 00000DAC 7557                <1>     jnz .no_slave
   238 00000DAE A808                <1>     test al, IDE_SR_DRQ
   239 00000DB0 7505                <1>     jnz .drq_set_s
   240 00000DB2 49                  <1>     dec cx
   241 00000DB3 75F4                <1>     jnz .wait_drq_s
   242 00000DB5 EB4E                <1>     jmp .no_slave
   243                              <1> .drq_set_s:
   244                              <1>     ; Read 256 words of IDENTIFY data (reuse buffer).
   245 00000DB7 BAF001              <1>     mov dx, IDE_DATA
   246 00000DBA BF[860A]            <1>     mov di, ide_identify_buf
   247 00000DBD 1E                  <1>     push ds
   248 00000DBE 07                  <1>     pop es
   249 00000DBF B90001              <1>     mov cx, 256
   250 00000DC2 F36D                <1>     rep insw
   251                              <1> 
   252                              <1>     ; Parse IDENTIFY data for slave.
   253 00000DC4 A1[880A]            <1>     mov ax, [ide_identify_buf + 2]
   254 00000DC7 2EA3[720A]          <1>     mov [cs:ide_slave_cyls], ax
   255 00000DCB A1[8C0A]            <1>     mov ax, [ide_identify_buf + 6]
   256 00000DCE 2EA3[740A]          <1>     mov [cs:ide_slave_heads], ax
   257 00000DD2 A1[920A]            <1>     mov ax, [ide_identify_buf + 12]
   258 00000DD5 2EA3[760A]          <1>     mov [cs:ide_slave_spt], ax
   259 00000DD9 66A1[FE0A]          <1>     mov eax, [ide_identify_buf + 120]
   260 00000DDD 2E66A3[780A]        <1>     mov [cs:ide_slave_lba28], eax
   261 00000DE2 66A1[4E0B]          <1>     mov eax, [ide_identify_buf + 200]
   262 00000DE6 2E66A3[7C0A]        <1>     mov [cs:ide_slave_lba48], eax
   263 00000DEB 66A1[520B]          <1>     mov eax, [ide_identify_buf + 204]
   264 00000DEF 2E66A3[800A]        <1>     mov [cs:ide_slave_lba48 + 4], eax
   265                              <1> 
   266 00000DF4 2EC606[710A]01      <1>     mov byte [cs:ide_slave_present], 1
   267                              <1> 
   268                              <1>     ; Increment BDA hard disk count.
   269 00000DFA 31C0                <1>     xor ax, ax
   270 00000DFC 8EC0                <1>     mov es, ax
   271 00000DFE 26FE067504          <1>     inc byte [es:BDA_NUM_HD]
   272                              <1> 
   273 00000E03 EB06                <1>     jmp .done
   274                              <1> 
   275                              <1> .no_slave:
   276 00000E05 2EC606[710A]00      <1>     mov byte [cs:ide_slave_present], 0
   277                              <1> 
   278                              <1> .done:
   279                              <1>     ; Re-select master drive as default.
   280 00000E0B BAF601              <1>     mov dx, IDE_DRIVE_HEAD
   281 00000E0E B0A0                <1>     mov al, 0xA0
   282 00000E10 EE                  <1>     out dx, al
   283                              <1> 
   284 00000E11 07                  <1>     pop es
   285 00000E12 5F                  <1>     pop di
   286 00000E13 665A                <1>     pop edx
   287 00000E15 6659                <1>     pop ecx
   288 00000E17 665B                <1>     pop ebx
   289 00000E19 6658                <1>     pop eax
   290 00000E1B C3                  <1>     ret
