   341                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   342                              <1> ; AH=0Eh: TTY character output.
   343                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   344                              <1> i10_tty_output:
   345 00001034 1E                  <1>     push ds
   346 00001035 06                  <1>     push es
   347 00001036 57                  <1>     push di
   348 00001037 53                  <1>     push bx
   349 00001038 51                  <1>     push cx
   350 00001039 52                  <1>     push dx
   351 0000103A 50                  <1>     push ax
   352                              <1> 
   353                              <1>     ; Mirror the character to the serial port so all INT 10h text output
   354                              <1>     ; (BIOS POST messages and bootloader text) appears in the serial log.
   355                              <1>     ; serial_putchar preserves AX and DX, so this is safe here.
   356 0000103B E80CF7              <1>     call serial_putchar
   357                              <1> 
   358 0000103E 31DB                <1>     xor bx, bx
   359 00001040 8EDB                <1>     mov ds, bx
   360                              <1> 
   361 00001042 8B165004            <1>     mov dx, [BDA_CURSOR_POS]
   362                              <1> 
   363 00001046 3C0D                <1>     cmp al, 0x0D
   364 00001048 7442                <1>     je .cr
   365 0000104A 3C0A                <1>     cmp al, 0x0A
   366 0000104C 7442                <1>     je .lf
   367 0000104E 3C08                <1>     cmp al, 0x08
   368 00001050 7442                <1>     je .bs
   369 00001052 3C07                <1>     cmp al, 0x07
   370 00001054 7478                <1>     je .done
   371                              <1> 
   372                              <1>     ; Regular character: write to VGA.
   373 00001056 50                  <1>     push ax
   374 00001057 52                  <1>     push dx                         ; Save cursor (MUL clobbers DX)
   375 00001058 0FB6C6              <1>     movzx ax, dh
   376 0000105B BB5000              <1>     mov bx, 80
   377 0000105E F7E3                <1>     mul bx
   378 00001060 5A                  <1>     pop dx                          ; Restore cursor
   379 00001061 0FB6DA              <1>     movzx bx, dl
   380 00001064 01D8                <1>     add ax, bx
   381 00001066 D1E0                <1>     shl ax, 1
   382 00001068 89C7                <1>     mov di, ax
   383 0000106A 58                  <1>     pop ax
   384                              <1> 
   385 0000106B BB00B8              <1>     mov bx, VGA_TEXT_SEG
   386 0000106E 8EC3                <1>     mov es, bx
   387 00001070 B407                <1>     mov ah, 0x07
   388 00001072 268905              <1>     mov [es:di], ax
   389                              <1> 
   390 00001075 FEC2                <1>     inc dl
   391 00001077 80FA50              <1>     cmp dl, 80
   392 0000107A 7221                <1>     jb .update_cursor
   393 0000107C 30D2                <1>     xor dl, dl
   394 0000107E FEC6                <1>     inc dh
   395                              <1> 
   396                              <1> .check_scroll:
   397 00001080 80FE19              <1>     cmp dh, 25
   398 00001083 7218                <1>     jb .update_cursor
   399 00001085 B618                <1>     mov dh, 24
   400 00001087 E84C00              <1>     call i10_scroll_line_up
   401 0000108A EB11                <1>     jmp .update_cursor
   402                              <1> 
   403                              <1> .cr:
   404 0000108C 30D2                <1>     xor dl, dl
   405 0000108E EB0D                <1>     jmp .update_cursor
   406                              <1> 
   407                              <1> .lf:
   408 00001090 FEC6                <1>     inc dh
   409 00001092 EBEC                <1>     jmp .check_scroll
   410                              <1> 
   411                              <1> .bs:
   412 00001094 80FA00              <1>     cmp dl, 0
   413 00001097 7404                <1>     je .update_cursor
   414 00001099 FECA                <1>     dec dl
   415 0000109B EB00                <1>     jmp .update_cursor
   416                              <1> 
   417                              <1> .update_cursor:
   418 0000109D 89165004            <1>     mov [BDA_CURSOR_POS], dx
   419                              <1> 
   420 000010A1 50                  <1>     push ax
   421 000010A2 52                  <1>     push dx                         ; Save cursor (MUL clobbers DX)
   422 000010A3 0FB6C6              <1>     movzx ax, dh
   423 000010A6 BB5000              <1>     mov bx, 80
   424 000010A9 F7E3                <1>     mul bx
   425 000010AB 5A                  <1>     pop dx                          ; Restore cursor
   426 000010AC 0FB6DA              <1>     movzx bx, dl
   427 000010AF 01D8                <1>     add ax, bx
   428                              <1> 
   429 000010B1 52                  <1>     push dx
   430 000010B2 50                  <1>     push ax
   431 000010B3 BAD403              <1>     mov dx, VGA_CRTC_ADDR
   432 000010B6 B00E                <1>     mov al, 0x0E
   433 000010B8 EE                  <1>     out dx, al
   434 000010B9 BAD503              <1>     mov dx, VGA_CRTC_DATA
   435 000010BC 58                  <1>     pop ax
   436 000010BD 50                  <1>     push ax
   437 000010BE 88E0                <1>     mov al, ah
   438 000010C0 EE                  <1>     out dx, al
   439 000010C1 BAD403              <1>     mov dx, VGA_CRTC_ADDR
   440 000010C4 B00F                <1>     mov al, 0x0F
   441 000010C6 EE                  <1>     out dx, al
   442 000010C7 BAD503              <1>     mov dx, VGA_CRTC_DATA
   443 000010CA 58                  <1>     pop ax
   444 000010CB EE                  <1>     out dx, al
   445 000010CC 5A                  <1>     pop dx
   446 000010CD 58                  <1>     pop ax
   447                              <1> 
   448                              <1> .done:
   449 000010CE 58                  <1>     pop ax
   450 000010CF 5A                  <1>     pop dx
   451 000010D0 59                  <1>     pop cx
   452 000010D1 5B                  <1>     pop bx
   453 000010D2 5F                  <1>     pop di
   454 000010D3 07                  <1>     pop es
   455 000010D4 1F                  <1>     pop ds
   456 000010D5 CF                  <1>     iret

   458                              <1> ; Scroll the text screen up one line (used by TTY output).

   464 000010DA 51                  <1>     push cx
   465                              <1> 
   466 000010DB B800B8              <1>     mov ax, VGA_TEXT_SEG
   467 000010DE 8ED8                <1>     mov ds, ax
   468 000010E0 8EC0                <1>     mov es, ax
   469                              <1> 
   470 000010E2 BEA000              <1>     mov si, 160
   471 000010E5 31FF                <1>     xor di, di
   472 000010E7 B98007              <1>     mov cx, 80 * 24
   473 000010EA F3A5                <1>     rep movsw
   474                              <1> 
   475 000010EC BF000F              <1>     mov di, 160 * 24
   476 000010EF B82007              <1>     mov ax, 0x0720
   477 000010F2 B95000              <1>     mov cx, 80
   478 000010F5 F3AB                <1>     rep stosw
   479                              <1> 
   480 000010F7 59                  <1>     pop cx
   481 000010F8 5F                  <1>     pop di
   482 000010F9 5E                  <1>     pop si
   483 000010FA 07                  <1>     pop es
   484 000010FB 1F                  <1>     pop ds
   485 000010FC C3                  <1>     ret
   486                              <1>

   573                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   574                              <1> ; AX=4F00h: Return VBE controller info.
   575                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   576                              <1> i10_vbe_info:
   577 000011AB 1E                  <1>     push ds
   578 000011AC 56                  <1>     push si
   579 000011AD 51                  <1>     push cx
   580 000011AE 57                  <1>     push di
   581                              <1> 
   582 000011AF 2666C70556455341    <1>     mov dword [es:di + 0], 'VESA'

   598                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   599                              <1> ; AX=4F01h: Return VBE mode info.
   600                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   601                              <1> i10_vbe_mode_info:
   602 000011EC 57                  <1>     push di
   603 000011ED 51                  <1>     push cx
   604                              <1> 
   605                              <1>     ; Zero the buffer.
   606 000011EE 51                  <1>     push cx
   607 000011EF 57                  <1>     push di
   608 000011F0 31C0                <1>     xor ax, ax
   609 000011F2 B98000              <1>     mov cx, 128
   610 000011F5 F3AB                <1>     rep stosw
   611 000011F7 5F                  <1>     pop di
   612 000011F8 59                  <1>     pop cx
   613                              <1> 
   614 000011F9 81E1FF01            <1>     and cx, 0x01FF
   615                              <1> 
   616                              <1>     ; Look up mode in table
   617 000011FD BE[4911]            <1>     mov si, vbe_mode_table
   618                              <1> .mi_search:
   619 00001200 2E833CFF            <1>     cmp word [cs:si], 0xFFFF
   620 00001204 740A                <1>     je .mi_not_found
   621 00001206 2E3B0C              <1>     cmp cx, [cs:si]
   622 00001209 740B                <1>     je .mi_found
   623 0000120B 83C608              <1>     add si, 8              ; next entry (mode + xres + yres + pitch)
   624 0000120E EBF0                <1>     jmp .mi_search
   625                              <1> 
   626                              <1> .mi_not_found:
   627 00001210 B84F01              <1>     mov ax, 0x014F
   628 00001213 59                  <1>     pop cx
   629 00001214 5F                  <1>     pop di
   630 00001215 CF                  <1>     iret
   631                              <1> 
   632                              <1> .mi_found:
   633 00001216 2E8B4402            <1>     mov ax, [cs:si + 2]
   634 0000121A A3[4311]            <1>     mov [vbe_mi_xres], ax
   635 0000121D 2E8B4404            <1>     mov ax, [cs:si + 4]
   636 00001221 A3[4511]            <1>     mov [vbe_mi_yres], ax
   637 00001224 2E8B4406            <1>     mov ax, [cs:si + 6]
   638 00001228 A3[4711]            <1>     mov [vbe_mi_pitch], ax
   639                              <1> 
   640                              <1> .mi_fill:
   641 0000122B 26C7059B00          <1>     mov word [es:di + 0], 0x009B
   642 00001230 A1[4711]            <1>     mov ax, [vbe_mi_pitch]
   643 00001233 26894510            <1>     mov [es:di + 16], ax
   644 00001237 A1[4311]            <1>     mov ax, [vbe_mi_xres]
   645 0000123A 26894512            <1>     mov [es:di + 18], ax
   646 0000123E A1[4511]            <1>     mov ax, [vbe_mi_yres]
   647 00001241 26894514            <1>     mov [es:di + 20], ax
   648 00001245 26C6451608          <1>     mov byte [es:di + 22], 8
   649 0000124A 26C6451710          <1>     mov byte [es:di + 23], 16
   650 0000124F 26C6451801          <1>     mov byte [es:di + 24], 1

   655 00001268 26C6452108          <1>     mov byte [es:di + 33], 8

   670                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   671                              <1> ; AX=4F02h: Set VBE mode.
   672                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   673                              <1> i10_vbe_set_mode:
   674 0000129C 51                  <1>     push cx
   675 0000129D 52                  <1>     push dx
   676                              <1> 
   677 0000129E 89D9                <1>     mov cx, bx
   678 000012A0 81E1FF01            <1>     and cx, 0x01FF
   679                              <1> 
   680 000012A4 83F903              <1>     cmp cx, 0x0003
   681 000012A7 742A                <1>     je .sm_text
   682                              <1> 
   683                              <1>     ; Look up mode in table
   684 000012A9 56                  <1>     push si
   685 000012AA BE[4911]            <1>     mov si, vbe_mode_table
   686                              <1> .sm_search:
   687 000012AD 2E833CFF            <1>     cmp word [cs:si], 0xFFFF
   688 000012B1 740A                <1>     je .sm_not_found
   689 000012B3 2E3B0C              <1>     cmp cx, [cs:si]
   690 000012B6 740B                <1>     je .sm_found
   691 000012B8 83C608              <1>     add si, 8
   692 000012BB EBF0                <1>     jmp .sm_search
   693                              <1> 
   694                              <1> .sm_not_found:
   695 000012BD 5E                  <1>     pop si
   696 000012BE B84F01              <1>     mov ax, 0x014F
   697 000012C1 EB1C                <1>     jmp .sm_done
   698                              <1> 
   699                              <1> .sm_found:
   700 000012C3 2E8B5C02            <1>     mov bx, [cs:si + 2]    ; width
   701 000012C7 2E8B4C04            <1>     mov cx, [cs:si + 4]    ; height
   702 000012CB 5E                  <1>     pop si
   703 000012CC B220                <1>     mov dl, 32
   704 000012CE E8F7F4              <1>     call vbe_set_mode
   705 000012D1 EB05                <1>     jmp .sm_ok
   706                              <1> 
   707                              <1> .sm_text:
   708 000012D3 E898F4              <1>     call video_init
   709 000012D6 EB00                <1>     jmp .sm_ok
   710                              <1> 
   711                              <1> .sm_ok:
   712 000012D8 891E[A911]          <1>     mov [current_vbe_mode], bx
   713 000012DC B84F00              <1>     mov ax, 0x004F
   714                              <1> 
   715                              <1> .sm_done:
   716 000012DF 5A                  <1>     pop dx
   717 000012E0 59                  <1>     pop cx
   718 000012E1 CF                  <1>     iret
   719                              <1>

   721                              <1> ; AX=4F03h: Get current VBE mode.
   722                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   723                              <1> i10_vbe_get_mode:
   724 000012E2 8B1E[A911]          <1>     mov bx, [current_vbe_mode]
   725 000012E6 B84F00              <1>     mov ax, 0x004F
   726 000012E9 CF                  <1>     iret
    26                                  %include "int13h.asm"
     1                              <1> ; =============================================================================
     2                              <1> ; int13h.asm — INT 13h: Disk BIOS services
     3                              <1> ; =============================================================================
     4                              <1> 
     5                              <1> ; Last INT 13h status (AH-style code). 0 = success.
     6 000012EA 00                  <1> int13_last_status: db 0
     7 000012EB 00                  <1> int13_last_ah:     db 0
     8 000012EC 00                  <1> int13_last_dl:     db 0
     9 000012ED 0000                <1> int13_call_count:  dw 0
    10 000012EF 0000                <1> int13_trace_counter: dw 0
    11                              <1> INT13_TRACE_MAX     equ 65535
    12                              <1> 
    13                              <1> int13h_handler:
    14 000012F1 2EFF06[ED12]        <1>     inc word [cs:int13_call_count]
    15 000012F6 2E8826[EB12]        <1>     mov [cs:int13_last_ah], ah
    16 000012FB 2E8816[EC12]        <1>     mov [cs:int13_last_dl], dl
    17                              <1> 
    18                              <1>     ; Trace INT 13h calls to serial: "[13 AH=xx DL=xx]".
    19 00001300 50                  <1>     push ax
    20 00001301 53                  <1>     push bx
    21 00001302 52                  <1>     push dx
    22 00001303 88E3                <1>     mov bl, ah
    23 00001305 88D7                <1>     mov bh, dl
    24 00001307 E8CD05              <1>     call int13_trace_enter
    25 0000130A 5A                  <1>     pop dx
    26 0000130B 5B                  <1>     pop bx
    27 0000130C 58                  <1>     pop ax
    28                              <1> 
    29 0000130D 80FC01              <1>     cmp ah, 0x01
    30 00001310 743F                <1>     je .get_last_status
    31 00001312 80FC00              <1>     cmp ah, 0x00
    32 00001315 7447                <1>     je .reset_disk
    33 00001317 80FC02              <1>     cmp ah, 0x02
    34 0000131A 744C                <1>     je .read_sectors_chs
    35 0000131C 80FC08              <1>     cmp ah, 0x08
    36 0000131F 0F843301            <1>     je .get_drive_params
    37 00001323 80FC15              <1>     cmp ah, 0x15
    38 00001326 0F84C501            <1>     je .get_disk_type
    39 0000132A 80FC41              <1>     cmp ah, 0x41
    40 0000132D 0F841602            <1>     je .check_extensions
    41 00001331 80FC42              <1>     cmp ah, 0x42
    42 00001334 0F846302            <1>     je .extended_read
    43 00001338 80FC43              <1>     cmp ah, 0x43
    44 0000133B 0F84E803            <1>     je .extended_write
    45 0000133F 80FC48              <1>     cmp ah, 0x48
    46 00001342 0F844504            <1>     je .get_ext_params
    47 00001346 80FC4B              <1>     cmp ah, 0x4B
    48 00001349 0F840C05            <1>     je .get_cd_emul_status
    49                              <1>     ; Unsupported function.
    50 0000134D B401                <1>     mov ah, 0x01                ; Invalid function
    51 0000134F F9                  <1>     stc
    52 00001350 CF                  <1>     iret
    53                              <1> 
    54                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    55                              <1> ; AH=01h: Get status of last operation.
    56                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    57                              <1> .get_last_status:
    58 00001351 2E8A26[EA12]        <1>     mov ah, [cs:int13_last_status]
    59 00001356 84E4                <1>     test ah, ah
    60 00001358 7402                <1>     jz .gls_ok
    61 0000135A F9                  <1>     stc
    62 0000135B CF                  <1>     iret
    63                              <1> .gls_ok:
    64 0000135C F8                  <1>     clc
    65 0000135D CF                  <1>     iret
    66                              <1> 
    67                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    68                              <1> ; AH=00h: Reset disk system.
    69                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    70                              <1> .reset_disk:
    71 0000135E 2EC606[EA12]00      <1>     mov byte [cs:int13_last_status], 0
    72 00001364 30E4                <1>     xor ah, ah                  ; Success
    73 00001366 F8                  <1>     clc
    74 00001367 CF                  <1>     iret
    75                              <1> 
    76                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    77                              <1> ; AH=02h: Read sectors using CHS.
    78                              <1> ;   AL = sector count, CH = cylinder low, CL[5:0] = sector, CL[7:6]+CH = cyl,
    79                              <1> ;   DH = head, DL = drive, ES:BX = buffer.
    80                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    81                              <1> .read_sectors_chs:
    82 00001368 6650                <1>     push eax
    83 0000136A 6653                <1>     push ebx
    84 0000136C 6651                <1>     push ecx
    85 0000136E 6652                <1>     push edx
    86 00001370 6657                <1>     push edi
    87 00001372 06                  <1>     push es
    88                              <1> 
    89                              <1>     ; Support first HDD (0x80) and virtual El Torito drive (0xE0).
    90 00001373 80FA80              <1>     cmp dl, 0x80
    91 00001376 7408                <1>     je .chs_drive_ok
    92 00001378 80FAE0              <1>     cmp dl, 0xE0
    93 0000137B 7403                <1>     je .chs_drive_ok
    94 0000137D E9CC00              <1>     jmp .chs_error
    95                              <1> 
    96                              <1> .chs_drive_ok:
    97                              <1>     ; Check that the appropriate drive is present.
    98 00001380 80FAE0              <1>     cmp dl, 0xE0
    99 00001383 750C                <1>     jne .chs_check_master
   100 00001385 2E803E[710A]00      <1>     cmp byte [cs:ide_slave_present], 0
   101 0000138B 0F84BD00            <1>     je .chs_error
   102 0000138F EB0A                <1>     jmp .chs_present_ok
   103                              <1> .chs_check_master:
   104 00001391 2E803E[5E0A]00      <1>     cmp byte [cs:ide_master_present], 0
   105 00001397 0F84B100            <1>     je .chs_error
   106                              <1> .chs_present_ok:
   107                              <1> 
   108                              <1>     ; Convert CHS to LBA: LBA = (C * H + h) * S + (s - 1)
   109                              <1>     ; where H = total heads, S = sectors per track.
   110                              <1>     ;
   111                              <1>     ; NOTE: MUL clobbers EDX, so DH (head) and DL (drive) must be read
   112                              <1>     ; from the pushed EDX on the stack ([esp+6]=DL, [esp+7]=DH) after
   113                              <1>     ; any MUL instruction.
   114 0000139B 660FB6C5            <1>     movzx eax, ch               ; Cylinder low 8 bits
   115 0000139F 88CB                <1>     mov bl, cl
   116 000013A1 C0EB06              <1>     shr bl, 6                   ; Cylinder high 2 bits
   117 000013A4 660FB6DB            <1>     movzx ebx, bl
   118 000013A8 66C1E308            <1>     shl ebx, 8
   119 000013AC 6609D8              <1>     or eax, ebx                 ; EAX = cylinder
   120                              <1> 
   121                              <1>     ; CHS geometry for conversion.
   122                              <1>     ; HDD: use IDENTIFY geometry.
   123                              <1>     ; CD no-emulation: expose synthetic geometry (64 heads, 32 spt)
   124                              <1>     ; consistent with AH=08.
   125 000013AF 80FAE0              <1>     cmp dl, 0xE0                ; DL still valid here (before MUL)
   126 000013B2 7508                <1>     jne .chs_geom_hdd
   127 000013B4 66BB40000000        <1>     mov ebx, 64
   128 000013BA EB07                <1>     jmp .chs_heads_ok
   129                              <1> .chs_geom_hdd:
   130 000013BC 2E660FB71E[610A]    <1>     movzx ebx, word [cs:ide_master_heads]
   131                              <1> .chs_heads_ok:
   132 000013C3 66F7E3              <1>     mul ebx                     ; EAX = C * H (EDX clobbered!)
   133 000013C6 66670FB65C2407      <1>     movzx ebx, byte [esp + 7]  ; DH (head) from pushed EDX
   134 000013CD 6601D8              <1>     add eax, ebx                ; EAX = C * H + h
   135                              <1> 
   136 000013D0 67807C2406E0        <1>     cmp byte [esp + 6], 0xE0   ; DL (drive) from pushed EDX
   137 000013D6 7508                <1>     jne .chs_spt_hdd
   138 000013D8 66BB20000000        <1>     mov ebx, 32
   139 000013DE EB07                <1>     jmp .chs_spt_ok
   140                              <1> .chs_spt_hdd:
   141 000013E0 2E660FB71E[630A]    <1>     movzx ebx, word [cs:ide_master_spt]
   142                              <1> .chs_spt_ok:
   143 000013E7 66F7E3              <1>     mul ebx                     ; EAX = (C*H + h) * S
   144                              <1> 
   145 000013EA 88CB                <1>     mov bl, cl
   146 000013EC 80E33F              <1>     and bl, 0x3F                ; Sector (1-based)
   147 000013EF FECB                <1>     dec bl
   148 000013F1 660FB6DB            <1>     movzx ebx, bl
   149 000013F5 6601D8              <1>     add eax, ebx                ; EAX = LBA
   150                              <1> 
   151                              <1>     ; Set drive select for ide_read_sectors.
   152 000013F8 67807C2406E0        <1>     cmp byte [esp + 6], 0xE0   ; DL from pushed EDX
   153 000013FE 7508                <1>     jne .chs_sel_master
   154 00001400 2EC606[840A]F0      <1>     mov byte [cs:ide_drive_sel], 0xF0  ; Slave
   155 00001406 EB06                <1>     jmp .chs_sel_done
   156                              <1> .chs_sel_master:
   157 00001408 2EC606[840A]E0      <1>     mov byte [cs:ide_drive_sel], 0xE0  ; Master
   158                              <1> .chs_sel_done:
   159                              <1> 
   160                              <1>     ; Now read using LBA. Sector count was in AL on entry.
   161                              <1>     ; We saved original regs, so recover sector count.
   162                              <1>     ; Stack frame: push eax(4)+ebx(4)+ecx(4)+edx(4)+edi(4)+es(2) = 22 bytes.
   163                              <1>     ; push es is 2 bytes in [BITS 16] mode, so offsets are:
   164                              <1>     ;   [esp+0]=ES, [esp+2]=EDI, [esp+6]=EDX, [esp+10]=ECX,
   165                              <1>     ;   [esp+14]=EBX, [esp+18]=EAX.
   166 0000140E 07                  <1>     pop es
   167 0000140F 06                  <1>     push es
   168 00001410 66678B4C2412        <1>     mov ecx, [esp + 18]         ; Original EAX (AL = count)
   169 00001416 660FB6C9            <1>     movzx ecx, cl               ; ECX = sector count
   170                              <1>     ; EAX = LBA, ECX = count, ES:BX was set by caller.
   171                              <1>     ; Recover original BX from stack.
   172 0000141A 66678B7C240E        <1>     mov edi, [esp + 14]         ; Original EBX
   173 00001420 6681E7FFFF0000      <1>     and edi, 0xFFFF             ; DI = buffer offset (original BX)
   174                              <1> 
   175 00001427 E83305              <1>     call ide_read_sectors
   176 0000142A 7215                <1>     jc .chs_error_pop
   177                              <1> 
   178 0000142C 07                  <1>     pop es
   179 0000142D 665F                <1>     pop edi
   180 0000142F 665A                <1>     pop edx
   181 00001431 6659                <1>     pop ecx
   182 00001433 665B                <1>     pop ebx
   183 00001435 6658                <1>     pop eax
   184                              <1>     ; AL = sectors read (set by ide_read_sectors).
   185 00001437 2EC606[EA12]00      <1>     mov byte [cs:int13_last_status], 0
   186 0000143D 30E4                <1>     xor ah, ah
   187 0000143F F8                  <1>     clc
   188 00001440 CF                  <1>     iret
   189                              <1> 
   190                              <1> .chs_error_pop:
   191 00001441 07                  <1>     pop es
   192 00001442 665F                <1>     pop edi
   193 00001444 665A                <1>     pop edx
   194 00001446 6659                <1>     pop ecx
   195 00001448 665B                <1>     pop ebx
   196 0000144A 6658                <1>     pop eax
   197                              <1> .chs_error:
   198 0000144C 2EC606[EA12]01      <1>     mov byte [cs:int13_last_status], 0x01
   199 00001452 B401                <1>     mov ah, 0x01
   200 00001454 F9                  <1>     stc
   201 00001455 CF                  <1>     iret
   202                              <1> 
   203                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   204                              <1> ; AH=08h: Get drive parameters.
   205                              <1> ;   DL = drive number.
   206                              <1> ;   Returns: CH = max cyl low, CL[7:6] = max cyl high, CL[5:0] = max sector,
   207                              <1> ;            DH = max head, DL = number of drives.
   208                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   209                              <1> .get_drive_params:
   210 00001456 80FA80              <1>     cmp dl, 0x80
   211 00001459 7408                <1>     je .gdp_hdd
   212 0000145B 80FAE0              <1>     cmp dl, 0xE0
   213 0000145E 7436                <1>     je .gdp_cd
   214 00001460 E98000              <1>     jmp .gdp_no_drive
   215                              <1> 
   216                              <1> .gdp_hdd:
   217 00001463 2E803E[5E0A]00      <1>     cmp byte [cs:ide_master_present], 0
   218 00001469 7478                <1>     je .gdp_no_drive
   219                              <1> 
   220 0000146B 53                  <1>     push bx
   221 0000146C 2EA1[5F0A]          <1>     mov ax, [cs:ide_master_cyls]
   222 00001470 48                  <1>     dec ax                      ; Max cylinder (0-based)
   223 00001471 88C5                <1>     mov ch, al                  ; Low 8 bits of cylinder
   224 00001473 88E1                <1>     mov cl, ah
   225 00001475 C0E106              <1>     shl cl, 6                   ; High 2 bits into CL[7:6]
   226                              <1> 
   227 00001478 2EA1[630A]          <1>     mov ax, [cs:ide_master_spt]
   228 0000147C 243F                <1>     and al, 0x3F
   229 0000147E 08C1                <1>     or cl, al                   ; Max sector in CL[5:0]
   230                              <1> 
   231 00001480 2EA1[610A]          <1>     mov ax, [cs:ide_master_heads]
   232 00001484 48                  <1>     dec ax
   233 00001485 88C6                <1>     mov dh, al                  ; Max head (0-based)
   234                              <1> 
   235 00001487 B201                <1>     mov dl, 1                   ; 1 drive
   236 00001489 2EC606[EA12]00      <1>     mov byte [cs:int13_last_status], 0
   237 0000148F 31C0                <1>     xor ax, ax                  ; AH = 0 (success)
   238 00001491 B300                <1>     mov bl, 0                   ; Drive type (not applicable for HD)
   239 00001493 5B                  <1>     pop bx
   240 00001494 F8                  <1>     clc
   241 00001495 CF                  <1>     iret
   242                              <1> 
   243                              <1> .gdp_cd:
   244 00001496 2E803E[710A]00      <1>     cmp byte [cs:ide_slave_present], 0
   245 0000149C 7445                <1>     je .gdp_no_drive
   246                              <1> 
   247                              <1>     ; Synthetic CHS for CD no-emulation compatibility.
   248                              <1>     ; Heads = 64, SPT = 32, cylinders derived from total sectors.
   249 0000149E 53                  <1>     push bx
   250 0000149F 2E66A1[780A]        <1>     mov eax, [cs:ide_slave_lba28]
   251 000014A4 6631D2              <1>     xor edx, edx
   252 000014A7 66BB00080000        <1>     mov ebx, 2048               ; 64 * 32
   253 000014AD 66F7F3              <1>     div ebx                     ; EAX = cylinders
   254 000014B0 6685C0              <1>     test eax, eax
   255 000014B3 7402                <1>     jz .gdp_cd_cyl_zero
   256 000014B5 6648                <1>     dec eax
   257                              <1> .gdp_cd_cyl_zero:
   258 000014B7 663DFF030000        <1>     cmp eax, 1023
   259 000014BD 7606                <1>     jbe .gdp_cd_cyl_ok
   260 000014BF 66B8FF030000        <1>     mov eax, 1023
   261                              <1> .gdp_cd_cyl_ok:
   262                              <1> 
   263 000014C5 88C5                <1>     mov ch, al                  ; Cylinder low 8 bits
   264 000014C7 88E1                <1>     mov cl, ah                  ; Cylinder high 2 bits in AH[1:0]
   265 000014C9 80E103              <1>     and cl, 0x03
   266 000014CC C0E106              <1>     shl cl, 6
   267 000014CF 80C920              <1>     or cl, 32                   ; SPT = 32
   268                              <1> 
   269 000014D2 B63F                <1>     mov dh, 63                  ; Heads = 64 => max head 63
   270 000014D4 B201                <1>     mov dl, 1                   ; 1 drive
   271 000014D6 2EC606[EA12]00      <1>     mov byte [cs:int13_last_status], 0
   272 000014DC 31C0                <1>     xor ax, ax
   273 000014DE B300                <1>     mov bl, 0
   274 000014E0 5B                  <1>     pop bx
   275 000014E1 F8                  <1>     clc
   276 000014E2 CF                  <1>     iret
   277                              <1> 
   278                              <1> .gdp_no_drive:
   279 000014E3 2EC606[EA12]07      <1>     mov byte [cs:int13_last_status], 0x07
   280 000014E9 B407                <1>     mov ah, 0x07                ; Drive parameter error
   281 000014EB B200                <1>     mov dl, 0
   282 000014ED F9                  <1>     stc
   283 000014EE CF                  <1>     iret
   284                              <1> 
   285                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   286                              <1> ; AH=15h: Get disk type.
   287                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   288                              <1> .get_disk_type:
   289 000014EF 80FA80              <1>     cmp dl, 0x80
   290 000014F2 7407                <1>     je .gdt_hdd
   291 000014F4 80FAE0              <1>     cmp dl, 0xE0
   292 000014F7 7423                <1>     je .gdt_cd
   293 000014F9 EB42                <1>     jmp .gdt_none
   294                              <1> 
   295                              <1> .gdt_hdd:
   296 000014FB 2E803E[5E0A]00      <1>     cmp byte [cs:ide_master_present], 0
   297 00001501 743A                <1>     je .gdt_none
   298                              <1> 
   299 00001503 B403                <1>     mov ah, 0x03                ; Type 3 = hard disk
   300                              <1>     ; CX:DX = total sectors.
   301 00001505 2E66A1[650A]        <1>     mov eax, [cs:ide_master_lba28]
   302 0000150A 89C1                <1>     mov cx, ax
   303 0000150C 66C1E810            <1>     shr eax, 16
   304 00001510 89C2                <1>     mov dx, ax
   305 00001512 87CA                <1>     xchg cx, dx                 ; CX = high, DX = low
   306 00001514 2EC606[EA12]00      <1>     mov byte [cs:int13_last_status], 0
   307 0000151A F8                  <1>     clc
   308 0000151B CF                  <1>     iret
   309                              <1> 
   310                              <1> .gdt_cd:
   311 0000151C 2E803E[710A]00      <1>     cmp byte [cs:ide_slave_present], 0
   312 00001522 7419                <1>     je .gdt_none
   313                              <1> 
   314 00001524 B403                <1>     mov ah, 0x03                ; Type 3 = hard disk (CD presented as disk)
   315                              <1>     ; CX:DX = total sectors.
   316 00001526 2E66A1[780A]        <1>     mov eax, [cs:ide_slave_lba28]
   317 0000152B 89C1                <1>     mov cx, ax
   318 0000152D 66C1E810            <1>     shr eax, 16
   319 00001531 89C2                <1>     mov dx, ax
   320 00001533 87CA                <1>     xchg cx, dx
   321 00001535 2EC606[EA12]00      <1>     mov byte [cs:int13_last_status], 0
   322 0000153B F8                  <1>     clc
   323 0000153C CF                  <1>     iret
   324                              <1>

   326 0000153D 2EC606[EA12]00      <1>     mov byte [cs:int13_last_status], 0
   327 00001543 B400                <1>     mov ah, 0x00                ; No drive
   328 00001545 F8                  <1>     clc
   329 00001546 CF                  <1>     iret
   330                              <1> 
   331                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   332                              <1> ; AH=41h: Check INT 13h extensions.
   333                              <1> ;   BX = 0x55AA, DL = drive.
   334                              <1> ;   Returns: BX = 0xAA55, AH = version, CX = API bitmap.
   335                              <1> ;
   336                              <1> ;   Supported drives: 0x80 (HDD master) and 0xE0 (CD-ROM slave).
   337                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   338                              <1> .check_extensions:
   339                              <1>     ; Accept 0x80 (HDD) and 0xE0 (virtual CD) only.
   340 00001547 80FA80              <1>     cmp dl, 0x80
   341 0000154A 7407                <1>     je .chk_ext_master
   342 0000154C 80FAE0              <1>     cmp dl, 0xE0
   343 0000154F 740C                <1>     je .chk_ext_slave
   344 00001551 EB28                <1>     jmp .chk_ext_fail
   345                              <1> 
   346                              <1> .chk_ext_master:
   347 00001553 2E803E[5E0A]00      <1>     cmp byte [cs:ide_master_present], 0
   348 00001559 7420                <1>     je .chk_ext_fail
   349 0000155B EB08                <1>     jmp .chk_ext_proceed
   350                              <1> 
   351                              <1> .chk_ext_slave:
   352 0000155D 2E803E[710A]00      <1>     cmp byte [cs:ide_slave_present], 0
   353 00001563 7416                <1>     je .chk_ext_fail
   354                              <1> 
   355                              <1> .chk_ext_proceed:
   356 00001565 81FBAA55            <1>     cmp bx, 0x55AA
   357 00001569 7510                <1>     jne .chk_ext_fail
   358                              <1> 
   359 0000156B BB55AA              <1>     mov bx, 0xAA55
   360 0000156E B430                <1>     mov ah, 0x30                ; Version 3.0
   361 00001570 B90100              <1>     mov cx, 0x0001              ; Extended disk access supported
   362 00001573 2EC606[EA12]00      <1>     mov byte [cs:int13_last_status], 0
   363 00001579 F8                  <1>     clc
   364 0000157A CF                  <1>     iret
   365                              <1> 
   366                              <1> .chk_ext_fail:
   367                              <1>     ; Trace: 41h failed for this drive
   368 0000157B 50                  <1>     push ax
   369 0000157C B034                <1>     mov al, '4'
   370 0000157E E8DB03              <1>     call int13_trace_putchar
   371 00001581 B031                <1>     mov al, '1'
   372 00001583 E8D603              <1>     call int13_trace_putchar
   373 00001586 B046                <1>     mov al, 'F'
   374 00001588 E8D103              <1>     call int13_trace_putchar
   375 0000158B B00A                <1>     mov al, 10
   376 0000158D E8CC03              <1>     call int13_trace_putchar
   377 00001590 58                  <1>     pop ax
   378                              <1> 
   379 00001591 2EC606[EA12]01      <1>     mov byte [cs:int13_last_status], 0x01
   380 00001597 B401                <1>     mov ah, 0x01
   381 00001599 F9                  <1>     stc
   382 0000159A CF                  <1>     iret
   383                              <1> 
   384                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   385                              <1> ; AH=42h: Extended read sectors (LBA).
   386                              <1> ;   DL = drive, DS:SI = Disk Address Packet (DAP).
   387                              <1> ;
   388                              <1> ;   DAP format:
   389                              <1> ;     Byte 0: size (16)
   390                              <1> ;     Byte 1: reserved (0)
   391                              <1> ;     Word 2: sector count
   392                              <1> ;     Word 4: buffer offset
   393                              <1> ;     Word 6: buffer segment
   394                              <1> ;     Qword 8: starting LBA
   395                              <1> ;
   396                              <1> ;   For DL=0x80 (HDD master): DAP LBA and count are in 512-byte sectors.
   397                              <1> ;   For DL=0xE0 (virtual CD-ROM slave): the DAP LBA and count are in
   398                              <1> ;   2048-byte CD sector units (matching the 2048-byte sector size reported
   399                              <1> ;   by AH=48h). The code below converts them to 512-byte ATA sectors (x4)
   400                              <1> ;   before calling ide_read_sectors.
   401                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   402                              <1> .extended_read:
   403 0000159B 6650                <1>     push eax
   404 0000159D 6653                <1>     push ebx
   405 0000159F 6651                <1>     push ecx
   406 000015A1 6652                <1>     push edx
   407 000015A3 6657                <1>     push edi
   408 000015A5 06                  <1>     push es
   409 000015A6 1E                  <1>     push ds
   410 000015A7 56                  <1>     push si
   411                              <1> 
   412                              <1>     ; BIOS variables use cs: prefix (in the ROM segment) so they are safe
   413                              <1>     ; regardless of caller's DS.  Save caller's DS in BX for DAP access.
   414 000015A8 8CDB                <1>     mov bx, ds
   415                              <1> 
   416 000015AA 80FA80              <1>     cmp dl, 0x80
   417 000015AD 7408                <1>     je .ext_read_check_master
   418 000015AF 80FAE0              <1>     cmp dl, 0xE0
   419 000015B2 740F                <1>     je .ext_read_check_slave
   420 000015B4 E93901              <1>     jmp .ext_read_fail_pop
   421                              <1> 
   422                              <1> .ext_read_check_master:
   423 000015B7 2E803E[5E0A]00      <1>     cmp byte [cs:ide_master_present], 0
   424 000015BD 0F842F01            <1>     je .ext_read_fail_pop
   425 000015C1 EB24                <1>     jmp .ext_read_hdd
   426                              <1> 
   427                              <1> .ext_read_check_slave:
   428 000015C3 2E803E[710A]00      <1>     cmp byte [cs:ide_slave_present], 0
   429 000015C9 7403                <1>     je .ext_read_slave_missing
   430 000015CB E98100              <1>     jmp .ext_read_cd
   431                              <1> 
   432                              <1> .ext_read_slave_missing:
   433                              <1>     ; Trace: slave not present for AH=42h
   434 000015CE 50                  <1>     push ax
   435 000015CF B021                <1>     mov al, '!'
   436 000015D1 E88803              <1>     call int13_trace_putchar
   437 000015D4 B053                <1>     mov al, 'S'
   438 000015D6 E88303              <1>     call int13_trace_putchar
   439 000015D9 B04C                <1>     mov al, 'L'
   440 000015DB E87E03              <1>     call int13_trace_putchar
   441 000015DE B00A                <1>     mov al, 10
   442 000015E0 E87903              <1>     call int13_trace_putchar
   443 000015E3 58                  <1>     pop ax
   444 000015E4 E90901              <1>     jmp .ext_read_fail_pop
   445                              <1> 
   446                              <1> .ext_read_hdd:
   447                              <1>     ; Read DAP fields via caller's DS (saved in BX), using ES as proxy segment.
   448 000015E7 2EC606[840A]E0      <1>     mov byte [cs:ide_drive_sel], 0xE0  ; Master
   449 000015ED 53                  <1>     push bx
   450 000015EE 07                  <1>     pop es                          ; ES = caller's DS
   451 000015EF 268B4404            <1>     mov ax, [es:si + 4]
   452 000015F3 83F8FF              <1>     cmp ax, 0xFFFF
   453 000015F6 7539                <1>     jne .ext_read_hdd_seg
   454 000015F8 268B4406            <1>     mov ax, [es:si + 6]
   455 000015FC 83F8FF              <1>     cmp ax, 0xFFFF
   456 000015FF 7530                <1>     jne .ext_read_hdd_seg
   457 00001601 26803C18            <1>     cmp byte [es:si + 0], 0x18
   458 00001605 722A                <1>     jb .ext_read_hdd_seg
   459 00001607 26660FB74C02        <1>     movzx ecx, word [es:si + 2]    ; Sector count (512-byte sectors)
   460 0000160D 26668B4408          <1>     mov eax, [es:si + 8]           ; LBA (512-byte sector units)
   461 00001612 26668B7C10          <1>     mov edi, [es:si + 16]          ; Flat destination address (low dword)
   462 00001617 26668B5414          <1>     mov edx, [es:si + 20]          ; High dword must be zero
   463 0000161C 6685D2              <1>     test edx, edx
   464 0000161F 0F85CD00            <1>     jnz .ext_read_fail_pop
   465 00001623 31DB                <1>     xor bx, bx
   466 00001625 8EC3                <1>     mov es, bx                      ; ES = 0 for flat ES:EDI addressing
   467 00001627 E8EC03              <1>     call ide_read_sectors_flat
   468 0000162A 0F82C200            <1>     jc .ext_read_fail_pop
   469 0000162E E98D00              <1>     jmp .ext_read_ok
   470                              <1> .ext_read_hdd_seg:
   471 00001631 26660FB74C02        <1>     movzx ecx, word [es:si + 2]    ; Sector count (512-byte sectors)
   472 00001637 268B7C04            <1>     mov di, [es:si + 4]            ; Buffer offset
   473 0000163B 268B5C06            <1>     mov bx, [es:si + 6]            ; Buffer segment
   474 0000163F 26668B4408          <1>     mov eax, [es:si + 8]           ; LBA (512-byte sector units)
   475 00001644 8EC3                <1>     mov es, bx                      ; ES = buffer segment
   476 00001646 E81403              <1>     call ide_read_sectors
   477 00001649 0F82A300            <1>     jc .ext_read_fail_pop
   478 0000164D EB6F                <1>     jmp .ext_read_ok
   479                              <1> 
   480                              <1> .ext_read_cd:
   481                              <1>     ; CD-ROM: AH=48h reports 2048-byte CD sectors, so ISOLINUX (and other
   482                              <1>     ; El Torito bootloaders) pass LBAs and counts in 2048-byte units.
   483                              <1>     ; Convert to 512-byte ATA sectors for ide_read_sectors.
   484 0000164F 2EC606[840A]F0      <1>     mov byte [cs:ide_drive_sel], 0xF0  ; Slave
   485 00001655 53                  <1>     push bx
   486 00001656 07                  <1>     pop es                          ; ES = caller's DS
   487 00001657 268B4404            <1>     mov ax, [es:si + 4]
   488 0000165B 83F8FF              <1>     cmp ax, 0xFFFF
   489 0000165E 753C                <1>     jne .ext_read_cd_seg
   490 00001660 268B4406            <1>     mov ax, [es:si + 6]
   491 00001664 83F8FF              <1>     cmp ax, 0xFFFF
   492 00001667 7533                <1>     jne .ext_read_cd_seg
   493 00001669 26803C18            <1>     cmp byte [es:si + 0], 0x18
   494 0000166D 722D                <1>     jb .ext_read_cd_seg
   495 0000166F 26660FB74C02        <1>     movzx ecx, word [es:si + 2]    ; Sector count (2048-byte CD sectors)
   496 00001675 66C1E102            <1>     shl ecx, 2                     ; × 4 → 512-byte ATA sectors
   497 00001679 26668B4408          <1>     mov eax, [es:si + 8]           ; LBA (2048-byte CD sector units)
   498 0000167E 66C1E002            <1>     shl eax, 2                     ; × 4 → ATA LBA (512-byte units)
   499 00001682 26668B7C10          <1>     mov edi, [es:si + 16]          ; Flat destination address (low dword)
   500 00001687 26668B5414          <1>     mov edx, [es:si + 20]          ; High dword must be zero
   501 0000168C 6685D2              <1>     test edx, edx
   502 0000168F 755F                <1>     jnz .ext_read_fail_pop
   503 00001691 31DB                <1>     xor bx, bx
   504 00001693 8EC3                <1>     mov es, bx                      ; ES = 0 for flat ES:EDI addressing
   505 00001695 E87E03              <1>     call ide_read_sectors_flat
   506 00001698 7256                <1>     jc .ext_read_fail_pop
   507 0000169A EB22                <1>     jmp .ext_read_ok
   508                              <1> .ext_read_cd_seg:
   509 0000169C 26660FB74C02        <1>     movzx ecx, word [es:si + 2]    ; Sector count (2048-byte CD sectors)
   510 000016A2 66C1E102            <1>     shl ecx, 2                     ; × 4 → 512-byte ATA sectors
   511 000016A6 268B7C04            <1>     mov di, [es:si + 4]            ; Buffer offset
   512 000016AA 268B5C06            <1>     mov bx, [es:si + 6]            ; Buffer segment
   513 000016AE 26668B4408          <1>     mov eax, [es:si + 8]           ; LBA (2048-byte CD sector units)
   514 000016B3 66C1E002            <1>     shl eax, 2                     ; × 4 → ATA LBA (512-byte units)
   515                              <1> 
   516 000016B7 8EC3                <1>     mov es, bx                      ; ES = buffer segment
   517 000016B9 E8A102              <1>     call ide_read_sectors
   518 000016BC 7232                <1>     jc .ext_read_fail_pop
   519                              <1> 
   520                              <1> .ext_read_ok:
   521                              <1>     ; Trace success
   522 000016BE 50                  <1>     push ax
   523 000016BF B034                <1>     mov al, '4'
   524 000016C1 E89802              <1>     call int13_trace_putchar
   525 000016C4 B032                <1>     mov al, '2'
   526 000016C6 E89302              <1>     call int13_trace_putchar
   527 000016C9 B06F                <1>     mov al, 'o'
   528 000016CB E88E02              <1>     call int13_trace_putchar
   529 000016CE B06B                <1>     mov al, 'k'
   530 000016D0 E88902              <1>     call int13_trace_putchar
   531 000016D3 B00A                <1>     mov al, 10
   532 000016D5 E88402              <1>     call int13_trace_putchar
   533 000016D8 58                  <1>     pop ax
   534                              <1> 
   535 000016D9 5E                  <1>     pop si
   536 000016DA 1F                  <1>     pop ds
   537 000016DB 07                  <1>     pop es
   538 000016DC 665F                <1>     pop edi
   539 000016DE 665A                <1>     pop edx
   540 000016E0 6659                <1>     pop ecx
   541 000016E2 665B                <1>     pop ebx

   543 000016E6 2EC606[EA12]00      <1>     mov byte [cs:int13_last_status], 0

   628 0000177B 6659                <1>     pop ecx

   632 00001781 2EC606[EA12]01      <1>     mov byte [cs:int13_last_status], 0x01

   635 0000178A CF                  <1>     iret
   636                              <1> 
   637                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   638                              <1> ; AH=48h: Get extended drive parameters.
   639                              <1> ;   DL = drive, DS:SI = result buffer.
   640                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   641                              <1> .get_ext_params:
   642 0000178B 80FA80              <1>     cmp dl, 0x80
   643 0000178E 7408                <1>     je .gep_hdd
   644 00001790 80FAE0              <1>     cmp dl, 0xE0
   645 00001793 7457                <1>     je .gep_cd
   646 00001795 E9A100              <1>     jmp .gep_fail
   647                              <1> 
   648                              <1> .gep_hdd:
   649 00001798 2E803E[5E0A]00      <1>     cmp byte [cs:ide_master_present], 0
   650 0000179E 0F849700            <1>     je .gep_fail
   651                              <1> 
   652                              <1>     ; Buffer size (minimum 26 bytes).
   653 000017A2 C7041A00            <1>     mov word [si + 0], 26       ; Size of result
   654 000017A6 C744020200          <1>     mov word [si + 2], 0x0002   ; Flags: CHS valid
   655                              <1> 
   656                              <1>     ; CHS geometry.
   657 000017AB 2E660FB706[5F0A]    <1>     movzx eax, word [cs:ide_master_cyls]
   658 000017B2 66894404            <1>     mov [si + 4], eax           ; Cylinders
   659 000017B6 2E660FB706[610A]    <1>     movzx eax, word [cs:ide_master_heads]
   660 000017BD 66894408            <1>     mov [si + 8], eax           ; Heads
   661 000017C1 2E660FB706[630A]    <1>     movzx eax, word [cs:ide_master_spt]
   662 000017C8 6689440C            <1>     mov [si + 12], eax          ; Sectors per track
   663                              <1> 
   664                              <1>     ; Total sectors (64-bit, in 512-byte units).
   665 000017CC 2E66A1[650A]        <1>     mov eax, [cs:ide_master_lba28]
   666 000017D1 66894410            <1>     mov [si + 16], eax
   667 000017D5 66C7441400000000    <1>     mov dword [si + 20], 0      ; High dword
   668                              <1> 
   669                              <1>     ; Bytes per sector.
   670 000017DD C744180002          <1>     mov word [si + 24], 512
   671                              <1> 
   672 000017E2 2EC606[EA12]00      <1>     mov byte [cs:int13_last_status], 0
   673 000017E8 30E4                <1>     xor ah, ah
   674 000017EA F8                  <1>     clc
   675 000017EB CF                  <1>     iret
   676                              <1> 
   677                              <1> .gep_cd:
   678 000017EC 2E803E[710A]00      <1>     cmp byte [cs:ide_slave_present], 0
   679 000017F2 7445                <1>     je .gep_fail
   680                              <1> 
   681                              <1>     ; Virtual CD-ROM: report 2048-byte sectors.
   682                              <1>     ; Total capacity in 2048-byte sectors = total_ata_sectors / 4.
   683 000017F4 C7041A00            <1>     mov word [si + 0], 26       ; Size of result
   684 000017F8 C744020000          <1>     mov word [si + 2], 0x0000   ; Flags: no CHS (CD-ROM)
   685 000017FD 66C7440400000000    <1>     mov dword [si + 4],  0      ; Cylinders (N/A)
   686 00001805 66C7440800000000    <1>     mov dword [si + 8],  0      ; Heads (N/A)
   687 0000180D 66C7440C00000000    <1>     mov dword [si + 12], 0      ; Sectors per track (N/A)
   688                              <1> 
   689                              <1>     ; Total ISO sectors = ATA sectors / 4.
   690 00001815 2E66A1[780A]        <1>     mov eax, [cs:ide_slave_lba28]
   691 0000181A 66C1E802            <1>     shr eax, 2
   692 0000181E 66894410            <1>     mov [si + 16], eax
   693 00001822 66C7441400000000    <1>     mov dword [si + 20], 0      ; High dword
   694                              <1> 
   695                              <1>     ; Bytes per sector: 2048.
   696 0000182A C744180008          <1>     mov word [si + 24], 2048
   697                              <1> 
   698 0000182F 2EC606[EA12]00      <1>     mov byte [cs:int13_last_status], 0
   699 00001835 30E4                <1>     xor ah, ah
   700 00001837 F8                  <1>     clc
   701 00001838 CF                  <1>     iret
   702                              <1> 
   703                              <1> .gep_fail:
   704                              <1>     ; Trace: 48h failed
   705 00001839 50                  <1>     push ax
   706 0000183A B034                <1>     mov al, '4'
   707 0000183C E81D01              <1>     call int13_trace_putchar
   708 0000183F B038                <1>     mov al, '8'
   709 00001841 E81801              <1>     call int13_trace_putchar
   710 00001844 B046                <1>     mov al, 'F'
   711 00001846 E81301              <1>     call int13_trace_putchar
   712 00001849 B00A                <1>     mov al, 10
   713 0000184B E80E01              <1>     call int13_trace_putchar
   714 0000184E 58                  <1>     pop ax
   715                              <1> 
   716 0000184F 2EC606[EA12]01      <1>     mov byte [cs:int13_last_status], 0x01
   717 00001855 B401                <1>     mov ah, 0x01
   718 00001857 F9                  <1>     stc
   719 00001858 CF                  <1>     iret
   720                              <1> 
   721                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   722                              <1> ; AH=4Bh: Get/Terminate Disk Emulation Status (El Torito).
   723                              <1> ;   AL = 0x00 → Terminate disk emulation (we treat as status query too)
   724                              <1> ;   AL = 0x01 → Return Emulation Status
   725                              <1> ;   DL = drive number
   726                              <1> ;
   727                              <1> ; Fills the 20-byte El Torito Specification Packet at DS:SI:
   728                              <1> ;   +0   (byte)  packet size = 0x13
   729                              <1> ;   +1   (byte)  boot media type
   730                              <1> ;   +2   (byte)  drive number of the emulated drive
   731                              <1> ;   +3   (byte)  controller index (0)
   732                              <1> ;   +4   (dword) image start (Load RBA in CD-sector units)
   733                              <1> ;   +8   (word)  device specification (0)
   734                              <1> ;   +10  (word)  user buffer segment (0)
   735                              <1> ;   +12  (word)  load segment
   736                              <1> ;   +14  (word)  sector count (512-byte virtual sectors)
   737                              <1> ;   +16  (word)  CHS of emulated drive (0 for no-emulation)
   738                              <1> ;   +18  (byte)  reserved (0)
   739                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   740                              <1> .get_cd_emul_status:
   741                              <1>         ; Only support subfunctions 00h (terminate/query) and 01h (status).
   742 00001859 3C00                <1>         cmp al, 0x00
   743 0000185B 7406                <1>         je .gcds_sub_ok
   744 0000185D 3C01                <1>         cmp al, 0x01
   745 0000185F 7402                <1>         je .gcds_sub_ok
   746 00001861 EB6A                <1>         jmp .gcds_fail          ; Nothing pushed yet — skip pops
   747                              <1>     .gcds_sub_ok:
   748                              <1> 
   749                              <1>     ; Save caller's DS in ES for output-buffer writes (ES:SI).
   750 00001863 06                  <1>     push es
   751 00001864 8CD8                <1>     mov ax, ds
   752 00001866 8EC0                <1>     mov es, ax          ; ES = caller's DS
   753                              <1> 
   754                              <1>     ; All el_torito_* variables are in the BIOS ROM segment (cs:).
   755                              <1>     ; No DS change needed — use cs: prefix for every BIOS variable read.
   756                              <1> 
   757                              <1>     ; Caller's DL must match our El Torito drive.
   758 00001868 2E3A16[4D1D]        <1>     cmp dl, [cs:el_torito_drive]
   759 0000186D 755D                <1>     jne .gcds_fail_pop
   760                              <1> 
   761 0000186F 2E803E[4C1D]00      <1>     cmp byte [cs:el_torito_present], 0
   762 00001875 7455                <1>     je .gcds_fail_pop
   763                              <1> 
   764                              <1>     ; Fill the 19-byte specification packet at ES:SI (caller's DS:SI).
   765 00001877 26C60413            <1>     mov byte [es:si + 0],  0x13    ; Packet size

   768 00001883 2EA0[4D1D]          <1>     mov al, [cs:el_torito_drive]
   769 00001887 26884402            <1>     mov [es:si + 2], al            ; Drive number

   771                              <1>

   783                              <1>

   887                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   888                              <1> ; ide_read_sectors — Read sectors from IDE using PIO LBA28.
   889                              <1> ;   Input:  EAX = start LBA, ECX = sector count, ES:DI = buffer
   890                              <1> ;           cs:ide_drive_sel = 0xE0 (master) or 0xF0 (slave)
   891                              <1> ;   Output: CF set on error
   892                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   893                              <1> ide_read_sectors:
   894 0000195D 6650                <1>     push eax
   895 0000195F 6653                <1>     push ebx
   896 00001961 6651                <1>     push ecx
   897 00001963 6652                <1>     push edx
   898 00001965 6657                <1>     push edi
   899                              <1> 
   900                              <1> .read_loop:
   901 00001967 6685C9              <1>     test ecx, ecx
   902 0000196A 0F849000            <1>     jz .read_done
   903                              <1> 
   904                              <1>     ; Select drive/LBA bits 24-27.
   905 0000196E 6650                <1>     push eax
   906 00001970 6689C2              <1>     mov edx, eax
   907 00001973 66C1EA18            <1>     shr edx, 24
   908 00001977 80E20F              <1>     and dl, 0x0F
   909 0000197A 2E0A16[840A]        <1>     or dl, [cs:ide_drive_sel]   ; Master (0xE0) or Slave (0xF0)
   910 0000197F 88D0                <1>     mov al, dl
   911 00001981 BAF601              <1>     mov dx, IDE_DRIVE_HEAD
   912 00001984 EE                  <1>     out dx, al
   913 00001985 6658                <1>     pop eax
   914                              <1> 
   915                              <1>     ; Sector count = 1.
   916 00001987 6650                <1>     push eax
   917 00001989 BAF201              <1>     mov dx, IDE_SEC_COUNT
   918 0000198C B001                <1>     mov al, 1
   919 0000198E EE                  <1>     out dx, al
   920 0000198F 6658                <1>     pop eax
   921                              <1> 
   922                              <1>     ; LBA low byte.
   923 00001991 6650                <1>     push eax
   924 00001993 BAF301              <1>     mov dx, IDE_LBA_LO
   925 00001996 EE                  <1>     out dx, al
   926 00001997 6658                <1>     pop eax
   927                              <1> 
   928                              <1>     ; LBA mid byte.
   929 00001999 6650                <1>     push eax
   930 0000199B 66C1E808            <1>     shr eax, 8
   931 0000199F BAF401              <1>     mov dx, IDE_LBA_MID
   932 000019A2 EE                  <1>     out dx, al
   933 000019A3 6658                <1>     pop eax
   934                              <1> 
   935                              <1>     ; LBA high byte.
   936 000019A5 6650                <1>     push eax
   937 000019A7 66C1E810            <1>     shr eax, 16
   938 000019AB BAF501              <1>     mov dx, IDE_LBA_HI
   939 000019AE EE                  <1>     out dx, al
   940 000019AF 6658                <1>     pop eax
   941                              <1> 
   942                              <1>     ; Send READ SECTORS command.
   943                              <1>     ; Save EAX (LBA) — the command byte and status polling clobber AL.
   944 000019B1 6650                <1>     push eax
   945 000019B3 BAF701              <1>     mov dx, IDE_CMD
   946 000019B6 B020                <1>     mov al, IDE_CMD_READ_SECTORS
   947 000019B8 EE                  <1>     out dx, al
   948                              <1> 
   949                              <1>     ; Wait for DRQ.
   950 000019B9 BAF701              <1>     mov dx, IDE_STATUS
   951 000019BC 51                  <1>     push cx
   952 000019BD B9FFFF              <1>     mov cx, 0xFFFF
   953                              <1> .read_wait:
   954 000019C0 EC                  <1>     in al, dx
   955 000019C1 A880                <1>     test al, IDE_SR_BSY
   956 000019C3 7508                <1>     jnz .read_wait_cont
   957 000019C5 A808                <1>     test al, IDE_SR_DRQ
   958 000019C7 7511                <1>     jnz .read_ready
   959 000019C9 A801                <1>     test al, IDE_SR_ERR
   960 000019CB 7508                <1>     jnz .read_err
   961                              <1> .read_wait_cont:
   962 000019CD 49                  <1>     dec cx
   963 000019CE 75F0                <1>     jnz .read_wait
   964 000019D0 59                  <1>     pop cx
   965 000019D1 6658                <1>     pop eax
   966 000019D3 EB35                <1>     jmp .read_error
   967                              <1> 
   968                              <1> .read_err:
   969 000019D5 59                  <1>     pop cx
   970 000019D6 6658                <1>     pop eax
   971 000019D8 EB30                <1>     jmp .read_error
   972                              <1> 
   973                              <1> .read_ready:
   974 000019DA 59                  <1>     pop cx
   975                              <1> 
   976                              <1>     ; Read 256 words (512 bytes).
   977 000019DB 57                  <1>     push di
   978 000019DC 51                  <1>     push cx
   979 000019DD 52                  <1>     push dx
   980 000019DE BAF001              <1>     mov dx, IDE_DATA
   981 000019E1 B90001              <1>     mov cx, 256
   982 000019E4 F36D                <1>     rep insw
   983 000019E6 5A                  <1>     pop dx
   984 000019E7 59                  <1>     pop cx
   985 000019E8 5B                  <1>     pop bx
   986 000019E9 39DF                <1>     cmp di, bx
   987 000019EB 7308                <1>     jae .read_no_wrap
   988                              <1>     ; DI wrapped across 64 KiB boundary: advance ES by 0x1000 paragraphs
   989                              <1>     ; so linear destination still advances by +0x10000 bytes.
   990 000019ED 8CC3                <1>     mov bx, es
   991 000019EF 81C30010            <1>     add bx, 0x1000
   992 000019F3 8EC3                <1>     mov es, bx
   993                              <1> .read_no_wrap:
   994                              <1> 
   995 000019F5 6658                <1>     pop eax                     ; Restore EAX (LBA) before incrementing
   996 000019F7 6640                <1>     inc eax                     ; Next LBA
   997 000019F9 6649                <1>     dec ecx                     ; Decrement count
   998 000019FB E969FF              <1>     jmp .read_loop
   999                              <1> 
  1000                              <1> .read_done:
  1001 000019FE 665F                <1>     pop edi
  1002 00001A00 665A                <1>     pop edx
  1003 00001A02 6659                <1>     pop ecx
  1004 00001A04 665B                <1>     pop ebx
  1005 00001A06 6658                <1>     pop eax
  1006 00001A08 F8                  <1>     clc
  1007 00001A09 C3                  <1>     ret
  1008                              <1> 
  1009                              <1> .read_error:
  1010 00001A0A 665F                <1>     pop edi
  1011 00001A0C 665A                <1>     pop edx
  1012 00001A0E 6659                <1>     pop ecx
  1013 00001A10 665B                <1>     pop ebx
  1014 00001A12 6658                <1>     pop eax
  1015 00001A14 F9                  <1>     stc
  1016 00001A15 C3                  <1>     ret
  1017                              <1>
