  1175                              <1>     ; LBA bytes.
  1176 00001AFE 6650                <1>     push eax
  1177 00001B00 BAF301              <1>     mov dx, IDE_LBA_LO
  1178 00001B03 EE                  <1>     out dx, al
  1179 00001B04 66C1E808            <1>     shr eax, 8
  1180 00001B08 BAF401              <1>     mov dx, IDE_LBA_MID
  1181 00001B0B EE                  <1>     out dx, al
  1182 00001B0C 66C1E808            <1>     shr eax, 8
  1183 00001B10 BAF501              <1>     mov dx, IDE_LBA_HI
  1184 00001B13 EE                  <1>     out dx, al
  1185 00001B14 6658                <1>     pop eax
  1186                              <1> 
  1187                              <1>     ; WRITE SECTORS command.
  1188                              <1>     ; Save EAX (LBA) — the command byte, status polling, and flush clobber AL.
  1189 00001B16 6650                <1>     push eax
  1190 00001B18 BAF701              <1>     mov dx, IDE_CMD
  1191 00001B1B B030                <1>     mov al, 0x30                ; WRITE SECTORS
  1192 00001B1D EE                  <1>     out dx, al
  1193                              <1> 
  1194                              <1>     ; Wait for DRQ.
  1195 00001B1E BAF701              <1>     mov dx, IDE_STATUS
  1196 00001B21 51                  <1>     push cx
  1197 00001B22 B9FFFF              <1>     mov cx, 0xFFFF
  1198                              <1> .write_wait:
  1199 00001B25 EC                  <1>     in al, dx
  1200 00001B26 A880                <1>     test al, IDE_SR_BSY
  1201 00001B28 75FB                <1>     jnz .write_wait
  1202 00001B2A A808                <1>     test al, IDE_SR_DRQ
  1203 00001B2C 7511                <1>     jnz .write_ready
  1204 00001B2E A801                <1>     test al, IDE_SR_ERR
  1205 00001B30 7508                <1>     jnz .write_err
  1206 00001B32 49                  <1>     dec cx
  1207 00001B33 75F0                <1>     jnz .write_wait
  1208 00001B35 59                  <1>     pop cx
  1209 00001B36 6658                <1>     pop eax
  1210 00001B38 EB37                <1>     jmp .write_error
  1211                              <1> .write_err:
  1212 00001B3A 59                  <1>     pop cx
  1213 00001B3B 6658                <1>     pop eax
  1214 00001B3D EB32                <1>     jmp .write_error
  1215                              <1> .write_ready:
  1216 00001B3F 59                  <1>     pop cx
  1217                              <1> 
  1218                              <1>     ; Write 256 words.
  1219 00001B40 51                  <1>     push cx
  1220 00001B41 52                  <1>     push dx
  1221 00001B42 BAF001              <1>     mov dx, IDE_DATA
  1222 00001B45 B90001              <1>     mov cx, 256
  1223 00001B48 F36F                <1>     rep outsw
  1224 00001B4A 5A                  <1>     pop dx
  1225 00001B4B 59                  <1>     pop cx
  1226                              <1> 
  1227                              <1>     ; Flush: wait for BSY clear.
  1228 00001B4C BAF701              <1>     mov dx, IDE_STATUS
  1229 00001B4F 51                  <1>     push cx
  1230 00001B50 B9FFFF              <1>     mov cx, 0xFFFF
  1231                              <1> .write_flush:
  1232 00001B53 EC                  <1>     in al, dx
  1233 00001B54 A880                <1>     test al, IDE_SR_BSY
  1234 00001B56 7403                <1>     jz .write_flushed
  1235 00001B58 49                  <1>     dec cx
  1236 00001B59 75F8                <1>     jnz .write_flush
  1237                              <1> .write_flushed:
  1238 00001B5B 59                  <1>     pop cx
  1239                              <1> 
  1240 00001B5C 6658                <1>     pop eax                     ; Restore EAX (LBA) before incrementing
  1241 00001B5E 6640                <1>     inc eax
  1242 00001B60 6649                <1>     dec ecx
  1243 00001B62 E96FFF              <1>     jmp .write_loop
  1244                              <1> 
  1245                              <1> .write_done:
  1246 00001B65 665E                <1>     pop esi
  1247 00001B67 665A                <1>     pop edx
  1248 00001B69 6659                <1>     pop ecx
  1249 00001B6B 665B                <1>     pop ebx
  1250 00001B6D 6658                <1>     pop eax
  1251 00001B6F F8                  <1>     clc
  1252 00001B70 C3                  <1>     ret
  1253                              <1> 
  1254                              <1> .write_error:
  1255 00001B71 665E                <1>     pop esi
  1256 00001B73 665A                <1>     pop edx
  1257 00001B75 6659                <1>     pop ecx
  1258 00001B77 665B                <1>     pop ebx
  1259 00001B79 6658                <1>     pop eax
  1260 00001B7B F9                  <1>     stc
  1261 00001B7C C3                  <1>     ret
    27                                  %include "int15h.asm"
     1                              <1> ; =============================================================================
     2                              <1> ; int15h.asm — INT 15h: System services (memory map, extended memory)
     3                              <1> ; =============================================================================
     4                              <1> 
     5                              <1> int15h_handler:
     6 00001B7D 3D20E8              <1>     cmp ax, 0xE820
     7 00001B80 742E                <1>     je i15_e820
     8 00001B82 3D01E8              <1>     cmp ax, 0xE801
     9 00001B85 0F84CE00            <1>     je i15_e801
    10 00001B89 80FC88              <1>     cmp ah, 0x88
    11 00001B8C 0F84A400            <1>     je i15_get_ext_mem
    12 00001B90 80FCC0              <1>     cmp ah, 0xC0
    13 00001B93 0F84FA00            <1>     je i15_get_config_table
    14 00001B97 3D0124              <1>     cmp ax, 0x2401
    15 00001B9A 0F840B01            <1>     je i15_enable_a20
    16 00001B9E 3D0224              <1>     cmp ax, 0x2402
    17 00001BA1 0F840801            <1>     je i15_get_a20_status
    18 00001BA5 3D0324              <1>     cmp ax, 0x2403
    19 00001BA8 0F840701            <1>     je i15_get_a20_support
    20                              <1> 
    21                              <1>     ; Unsupported function.
    22 00001BAC B486                <1>     mov ah, 0x86
    23 00001BAE F9                  <1>     stc
    24 00001BAF CF                  <1>     iret
    25                              <1> 
    26                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    27                              <1> ; AX=E820h: Get system memory map.
    28                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    29                              <1> i15_e820:
    30 00001BB0 6681FA50414D53      <1>     cmp edx, 0x534D4150
    31 00001BB7 7577                <1>     jne .fail
    32                              <1> 
    33 00001BB9 6656                <1>     push esi
    34 00001BBB 1E                  <1>     push ds
    35                              <1> 
    36                              <1>     ; The E820 table lives in the BIOS ROM segment (cs:).
    37                              <1>     ; memory_detect wrote it with DS=CS; we read it with DS=CS too.
    38 00001BBC 0E                  <1>     push cs
    39 00001BBD 1F                  <1>     pop ds
    40                              <1> 
    41 00001BBE 660FB706[6B04]      <1>     movzx eax, word [e820_count]
    42 00001BC4 6639C3              <1>     cmp ebx, eax
    43 00001BC7 7D64                <1>     jge .e820_fail_pop
    44                              <1> 
    45 00001BC9 6689D8              <1>     mov eax, ebx
    46 00001BCC 66BE18000000        <1>     mov esi, E820_ENTRY_SIZE
    47 00001BD2 66F7E6              <1>     mul esi
    48 00001BD5 6605[6D040000]      <1>     add eax, e820_table         ; ESI = &e820_table[entry_index]
    49 00001BDB 6689C6              <1>     mov esi, eax
    50                              <1> 
    51 00001BDE 6683F914            <1>     cmp ecx, 20
    52 00001BE2 7249                <1>     jb .e820_fail_pop
    53                              <1> 
    54 00001BE4 51                  <1>     push cx
    55 00001BE5 83F918              <1>     cmp cx, 24
    56 00001BE8 7205                <1>     jb .e820_copy_20
    57 00001BEA B91800              <1>     mov cx, 24
    58 00001BED EB03                <1>     jmp .e820_copy_ready
    59                              <1> .e820_copy_20:
    60 00001BEF B91400              <1>     mov cx, 20
    61                              <1> .e820_copy_ready:
    62 00001BF2 89CA                <1>     mov dx, cx
    63 00001BF4 57                  <1>     push di
    64 00001BF5 FC                  <1>     cld
    65 00001BF6 F3A4                <1>     rep movsb                   ; DS:SI (segment 0) → ES:DI
    66 00001BF8 5F                  <1>     pop di
    67 00001BF9 83FA18              <1>     cmp dx, 24
    68 00001BFC 7509                <1>     jne .e820_copy_done
    69 00001BFE 2666C7451401000000  <1>     mov dword [es:di + 20], 1   ; ACPI 3.x ext-attrs: entry enabled/valid
    70                              <1> .e820_copy_done:
    71 00001C07 59                  <1>     pop cx
    72                              <1> 
    73 00001C08 66B850414D53        <1>     mov eax, 0x534D4150
    74 00001C0E 66BA50414D53        <1>     mov edx, 0x534D4150
    75 00001C14 6643                <1>     inc ebx
    76 00001C16 660FB70E[6B04]      <1>     movzx ecx, word [e820_count]
    77 00001C1C 6639CB              <1>     cmp ebx, ecx
    78 00001C1F 7C03                <1>     jl .e820_not_last
    79 00001C21 6631DB              <1>     xor ebx, ebx
    80                              <1> .e820_not_last:
    81 00001C24 660FB7CA            <1>     movzx ecx, dx
    82                              <1> 
    83 00001C28 1F                  <1>     pop ds
    84 00001C29 665E                <1>     pop esi
    85 00001C2B F8                  <1>     clc
    86 00001C2C CF                  <1>     iret
    87                              <1> 
    88                              <1> .e820_fail_pop:
    89 00001C2D 1F                  <1>     pop ds
    90 00001C2E 665E                <1>     pop esi
    91                              <1> .fail:
    92 00001C30 B486                <1>     mov ah, 0x86
    93 00001C32 F9                  <1>     stc
    94 00001C33 CF                  <1>     iret
    95                              <1> 
    96                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    97                              <1> ; AH=88h: Get extended memory size.
    98                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    99                              <1> i15_get_ext_mem:
   100 00001C34 6653                <1>     push ebx
   101 00001C36 2E66A1[2D05]        <1>     mov eax, [cs:ram_size_bytes]
   102 00001C3B 662D00001000        <1>     sub eax, 0x100000
   103 00001C41 66C1E80A            <1>     shr eax, 10
   104 00001C45 663DFFFF0000        <1>     cmp eax, 0xFFFF
   105 00001C4B 7606                <1>     jbe .ok
   106 00001C4D 66B8FFFF0000        <1>     mov eax, 0xFFFF
   107                              <1> .ok:
   108 00001C53 665B                <1>     pop ebx
   109 00001C55 F8                  <1>     clc
   110 00001C56 CF                  <1>     iret
   111                              <1> 
   112                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   113                              <1> ; AX=E801h: Get extended memory size (two ranges).
   114                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   115                              <1> i15_e801:
   116 00001C57 6656                <1>     push esi
   117 00001C59 2E66A1[2D05]        <1>     mov eax, [cs:ram_size_bytes]
   118                              <1> 
   119 00001C5E 663D00000001        <1>     cmp eax, 0x1000000
   120 00001C64 7615                <1>     jbe .below_16
   121                              <1> 
   122 00001C66 B9003C              <1>     mov cx, 15360
   123 00001C69 662D00000001        <1>     sub eax, 0x1000000
   124 00001C6F 66C1E810            <1>     shr eax, 16
   125 00001C73 89C3                <1>     mov bx, ax
   126 00001C75 89C2                <1>     mov dx, ax
   127 00001C77 89C8                <1>     mov ax, cx
   128 00001C79 EB12                <1>     jmp .done
   129                              <1> 
   130                              <1> .below_16:
   131 00001C7B 662D00001000        <1>     sub eax, 0x100000
   132 00001C81 66C1E80A            <1>     shr eax, 10
   133 00001C85 89C1                <1>     mov cx, ax
   134 00001C87 31DB                <1>     xor bx, bx
   135 00001C89 31D2                <1>     xor dx, dx
   136 00001C8B EB00                <1>     jmp .done
   137                              <1> 
   138                              <1> .done:
   139 00001C8D 665E                <1>     pop esi
   140 00001C8F F8                  <1>     clc
   141 00001C90 CF                  <1>     iret
   142                              <1> 
   143                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   144                              <1> ; AH=C0h: Get system configuration table pointer.
   145                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   146                              <1> i15_get_config_table:
   147 00001C91 BB[9F1C]            <1>     mov bx, i15_sys_config_table
   148 00001C94 50                  <1>     push ax
   149 00001C95 B800F0              <1>     mov ax, 0xF000
   150 00001C98 8EC0                <1>     mov es, ax
   151 00001C9A 58                  <1>     pop ax
   152 00001C9B 30E4                <1>     xor ah, ah
   153 00001C9D F8                  <1>     clc
   154 00001C9E CF                  <1>     iret
   155                              <1> 
   156                              <1> i15_sys_config_table:
   157 00001C9F 0800                <1>     dw 8                        ; Table length
   158 00001CA1 FC                  <1>     db 0xFC                     ; Model: AT compatible
   159 00001CA2 01                  <1>     db 0x01                     ; Sub-model
   160 00001CA3 00                  <1>     db 0x00                     ; BIOS revision
   161 00001CA4 74                  <1>     db 0x74                     ; Feature byte 1
   162 00001CA5 00000000            <1>     db 0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00  ; Feature bytes 2-5
   163                              <1> 
   164                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   165                              <1> ; AX=2401h: Enable A20 gate (always enabled in libcorevm).
   166                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   167                              <1> i15_enable_a20:
   168 00001CA9 30E4                <1>     xor ah, ah
   169 00001CAB F8                  <1>     clc
   170 00001CAC CF                  <1>     iret
   171                              <1>

   173                              <1> ; AX=2402h: Get A20 gate status.

   177 00001CAF 30E4                <1>     xor ah, ah
   178 00001CB1 F8                  <1>     clc
   179 00001CB2 CF                  <1>     iret
   180                              <1>

   186 00001CB6 30E4                <1>     xor ah, ah
   187 00001CB8 F8                  <1>     clc
   188 00001CB9 CF                  <1>     iret
    28                                  %include "int16h.asm"
     1                              <1> ; =============================================================================
     2                              <1> ; int16h.asm — INT 16h: Keyboard BIOS services
     3                              <1> ; =============================================================================
     4                              <1> 
     5                              <1> int16h_handler:
     6 00001CBA 80FC00              <1>     cmp ah, 0x00
     7 00001CBD 741A                <1>     je .wait_key
     8 00001CBF 80FC01              <1>     cmp ah, 0x01
     9 00001CC2 7443                <1>     je .check_key
    10 00001CC4 80FC02              <1>     cmp ah, 0x02
    11 00001CC7 746B                <1>     je .get_shift_flags
    12 00001CC9 80FC10              <1>     cmp ah, 0x10
    13 00001CCC 740B                <1>     je .wait_key              ; Enhanced: same as 00h for us
    14 00001CCE 80FC11              <1>     cmp ah, 0x11
    15 00001CD1 7434                <1>     je .check_key             ; Enhanced: same as 01h
    16 00001CD3 80FC12              <1>     cmp ah, 0x12
    17 00001CD6 7466                <1>     je .get_ext_shift_flags
    18                              <1>     ; Unsupported.
    19 00001CD8 CF                  <1>     iret
    20                              <1> 
    21                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    22                              <1> ; AH=00h/10h: Wait for keypress.
    23                              <1> ;   Returns: AH = scan code, AL = ASCII code.
    24                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    25                              <1> .wait_key:
    26 00001CD9 1E                  <1>     push ds
    27 00001CDA 53                  <1>     push bx
    28 00001CDB 31DB                <1>     xor bx, bx
    29 00001CDD 8EDB                <1>     mov ds, bx
    30                              <1> 
    31                              <1> .wait_loop:
    32 00001CDF FA                  <1>     cli
    33 00001CE0 8B1E1A04            <1>     mov bx, [BDA_KBD_BUF_HEAD]
    34 00001CE4 3B1E1C04            <1>     cmp bx, [BDA_KBD_BUF_TAIL]
    35 00001CE8 7504                <1>     jne .key_available
    36 00001CEA FB                  <1>     sti
    37 00001CEB F4                  <1>     hlt                         ; Wait for keyboard IRQ
    38 00001CEC EBF1                <1>     jmp .wait_loop
    39                              <1> 
    40                              <1> .key_available:
    41                              <1>     ; Read key from buffer.
    42 00001CEE 8B870004            <1>     mov ax, [bx + 0x0400]      ; BDA buffer is at 0x400 + offset
    43                              <1>     ; Advance head pointer.
    44 00001CF2 83C302              <1>     add bx, 2
    45 00001CF5 3B1E8204            <1>     cmp bx, [BDA_KBD_BUF_END]
    46 00001CF9 7204                <1>     jb .head_ok
    47 00001CFB 8B1E8004            <1>     mov bx, [BDA_KBD_BUF_START]
    48                              <1> .head_ok:
    49 00001CFF 891E1A04            <1>     mov [BDA_KBD_BUF_HEAD], bx
    50 00001D03 FB                  <1>     sti
    51                              <1> 
    52 00001D04 5B                  <1>     pop bx
    53 00001D05 1F                  <1>     pop ds
    54 00001D06 CF                  <1>     iret
    55                              <1> 
    56                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    57                              <1> ; AH=01h/11h: Check keyboard buffer (non-blocking).
    58                              <1> ;   Returns: ZF=1 if empty, ZF=0 if key available (AX = key, not removed).
    59                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    60                              <1> .check_key:
    61 00001D07 1E                  <1>     push ds
    62 00001D08 53                  <1>     push bx
    63 00001D09 31DB                <1>     xor bx, bx
    64 00001D0B 8EDB                <1>     mov ds, bx
    65                              <1> 
    66 00001D0D FA                  <1>     cli
    67 00001D0E 8B1E1A04            <1>     mov bx, [BDA_KBD_BUF_HEAD]
    68 00001D12 3B1E1C04            <1>     cmp bx, [BDA_KBD_BUF_TAIL]
    69 00001D16 7410                <1>     je .buf_empty
    70                              <1> 
    71                              <1>     ; Key available: peek without removing.
    72 00001D18 8B870004            <1>     mov ax, [bx + 0x0400]
    73 00001D1C FB                  <1>     sti
    74                              <1> 
    75 00001D1D 5B                  <1>     pop bx
    76 00001D1E 1F                  <1>     pop ds
    77                              <1>     ; Clear ZF to indicate key available.
    78 00001D1F 55                  <1>     push bp
    79 00001D20 89E5                <1>     mov bp, sp
    80 00001D22 836606BF            <1>     and word [bp + 6], ~0x0040  ; Clear ZF in saved FLAGS on stack
    81 00001D26 5D                  <1>     pop bp
    82 00001D27 CF                  <1>     iret
    83                              <1> 
    84                              <1> .buf_empty:
    85 00001D28 FB                  <1>     sti
    86 00001D29 5B                  <1>     pop bx
    87 00001D2A 1F                  <1>     pop ds
    88                              <1>     ; Set ZF to indicate empty.
    89 00001D2B 55                  <1>     push bp
    90 00001D2C 89E5                <1>     mov bp, sp
    91 00001D2E 834E0640            <1>     or word [bp + 6], 0x0040    ; Set ZF in saved FLAGS on stack
    92 00001D32 5D                  <1>     pop bp
    93 00001D33 CF                  <1>     iret
    94                              <1> 
    95                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    96                              <1> ; AH=02h: Get shift flags.
    97                              <1> ;   Returns: AL = shift flags byte 1.
    98                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    99                              <1> .get_shift_flags:
   100 00001D34 1E                  <1>     push ds
   101 00001D35 31C0                <1>     xor ax, ax
   102 00001D37 8ED8                <1>     mov ds, ax
   103 00001D39 A01704              <1>     mov al, [BDA_KBD_FLAGS1]
   104 00001D3C 1F                  <1>     pop ds
   105 00001D3D CF                  <1>     iret
   106                              <1> 
   107                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   108                              <1> ; AH=12h: Get extended shift flags.
   109                              <1> ;   Returns: AL = flags byte 1, AH = flags byte 2.
   110                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   111                              <1> .get_ext_shift_flags:
   112 00001D3E 1E                  <1>     push ds
   113 00001D3F 31C0                <1>     xor ax, ax
   114 00001D41 8ED8                <1>     mov ds, ax
   115 00001D43 A01704              <1>     mov al, [BDA_KBD_FLAGS1]
   116 00001D46 8A261804            <1>     mov ah, [BDA_KBD_FLAGS2]
   117 00001D4A 1F                  <1>     pop ds
   118 00001D4B CF                  <1>     iret
    29                                  %include "int19h.asm"
     1                              <1> ; =============================================================================
     2                              <1> ; int19h.asm — INT 19h: Bootstrap loader
     3                              <1> ;
     4                              <1> ; Boot priority:
     5                              <1> ;   1. El Torito CD boot — probes the slave drive first (ISO/CD).
     6                              <1> ;      If no slave is present, falls back to probing master.
     7                              <1> ;   2. MBR hard-disk boot (reads ATA LBA 0 from master, checks 0xAA55).
     8                              <1> ;
     9                              <1> ; ISO ↔ ATA sector translation:
    10                              <1> ;   ISO uses 2048-byte logical sectors; the ATA device uses 512-byte sectors.
    11                              <1> ;   ATA LBA = ISO sector × 4.
    12                              <1> ;
    13                              <1> ; After a successful El Torito boot the following variables are filled and
    14                              <1> ; can be queried by INT 13h AH=4Bh:
    15                              <1> ;   el_torito_drive     — virtual drive number handed to the boot image in DL
    16                              <1> ;   el_torito_emul      — Boot Media Type byte from the catalog entry
    17                              <1> ;   el_torito_load_seg  — segment the boot image was loaded into
    18                              <1> ;   el_torito_rba       — Load RBA from the catalog (ISO 2048-byte sector units)
    19                              <1> ;   el_torito_count     — virtual sector count that was loaded
    20                              <1> ; =============================================================================
    21                              <1> 
    22                              <1> ; ── El Torito runtime data (queried by INT 13h AH=4Bh) ─────────────────────
    23 00001D4C 00                  <1> el_torito_present:  db 0       ; 1 after successful El Torito detection
    24 00001D4D E0                  <1> el_torito_drive:    db 0xE0    ; Virtual drive number returned in DL (0xE0=CD)
    25 00001D4E 00                  <1> el_torito_emul:     db 0       ; Boot Media Type (0=no-emul, 1-3=floppy, 4=HD)
    26 00001D4F C007                <1> el_torito_load_seg: dw 0x07C0  ; Load segment (from catalog or default 0x07C0)
    27 00001D51 00000000            <1> el_torito_rba:      dd 0       ; Boot image Load RBA (ISO 2048-byte sectors)
    28 00001D55 0100                <1> el_torito_count:    dw 1       ; Sector count (512-byte virtual sectors loaded)
    29 00001D57 00                  <1> el_torito_handoff:  db 0       ; Set to 1 right before jumping into boot image
    30                              <1> 
    31                              <1> ; ── Work buffer ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    32                              <1> ; ide_identify_buf is only needed during POST (ide_detect). After POST it is
    33                              <1> ; safe to reuse those 512 bytes as a scratch buffer for El Torito parsing.
    34                              <1> el_torito_buf       equ ide_identify_buf
    35                              <1> 
    36                              <1> ; ── INT 19h entry ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    37                              <1> int19h_handler:
    38 00001D58 FB                  <1>     sti
    39                              <1> 
    40                              <1>     ; Need at least one drive to boot.
    41 00001D59 2E803E[5E0A]00      <1>     cmp byte [cs:ide_master_present], 0
    42 00001D5F 750A                <1>     jne .have_drive
    43 00001D61 2E803E[710A]00      <1>     cmp byte [cs:ide_slave_present], 0
    44 00001D67 7502                <1>     jne .have_drive
    45 00001D69 EB46                <1>     jmp .no_boot
    46                              <1> .have_drive:
    47                              <1> 
    48                              <1>     ; ── Step 1: Try El Torito ──────────────────────────────────────────────
    49 00001D6B E84D00              <1>     call detect_el_torito
    50 00001D6E 7205                <1>     jc .try_hd             ; CF=1 → not an El Torito disk
    51                              <1> 
    52                              <1>     ; El Torito signature verified — load and jump.
    53 00001D70 E87101              <1>     call boot_el_torito
    54                              <1>     ; boot_el_torito only returns on failure.
    55 00001D73 EB3C                <1>     jmp .no_boot
    56                              <1> 
    57                              <1>     ; ── Step 2: Fall back to MBR/HDD boot ─────────────────────────────────
    58                              <1> .try_hd:
    59 00001D75 2E803E[5E0A]00      <1>     cmp byte [cs:ide_master_present], 0
    60 00001D7B 7434                <1>     je .no_boot
    61                              <1> 
    62 00001D7D BE[0301]            <1>     mov si, str_booting_hd
    63 00001D80 E8630D              <1>     call bios_print
    64                              <1> 
    65                              <1>     ; Select master drive for MBR read.
    66 00001D83 2EC606[840A]E0      <1>     mov byte [cs:ide_drive_sel], 0xE0  ; Master
    67                              <1> 
    68                              <1>     ; Read LBA 0 (MBR) to 0x0000:0x7C00.
    69 00001D89 66B800000000        <1>     mov eax, 0
    70 00001D8F 66B901000000        <1>     mov ecx, 1
    71 00001D95 06                  <1>     push es
    72 00001D96 6A00                <1>     push word 0x0000
    73 00001D98 07                  <1>     pop es
    74 00001D99 BF007C              <1>     mov di, 0x7C00
    75 00001D9C E8BEFB              <1>     call ide_read_sectors
    76 00001D9F 07                  <1>     pop es
    77 00001DA0 720F                <1>     jc .no_boot
    78                              <1> 
    79                              <1>     ; Verify MBR signature.
    80 00001DA2 813EFE7D55AA        <1>     cmp word [0x7C00 + 510], 0xAA55
    81 00001DA8 7507                <1>     jne .no_boot
    82                              <1> 
    83                              <1>     ; Valid MBR — launch it.
    84 00001DAA B280                <1>     mov dl, 0x80            ; First HDD
    85 00001DAC EA007C0000          <1>     jmp 0x0000:0x7C00
    86                              <1> 
    87                              <1> .no_boot:
    88 00001DB1 BE[3801]            <1>     mov si, str_no_boot
    89 00001DB4 E82F0D              <1>     call bios_print
    90                              <1> .halt:
    91 00001DB7 FA                  <1>     cli
    92 00001DB8 F4                  <1>     hlt
    93 00001DB9 EBFC                <1>     jmp .halt
    94                              <1> 
    95                              <1> 
    96                              <1> ; =============================================================================
    97                              <1> ; detect_el_torito
    98                              <1> ;
    99                              <1> ; Checks whether the attached IDE disk is an El Torito CD image.
   100                              <1> ; Probes slave first (if present), then master as fallback.
   101                              <1> ; Reads ISO sector 17 (ATA LBA 68) which, for a standards-compliant ISO,
   102                              <1> ; contains the Boot Record Volume Descriptor.  On success it reads the Boot
   103                              <1> ; Catalog and fills all el_torito_* variables.
   104                              <1> ;
   105                              <1> ; Returns: CF=0 on success, CF=1 if this is not an El Torito disk.
   106                              <1> ; Clobbers: el_torito_buf (= ide_identify_buf)
   107                              <1> ; =============================================================================
   108                              <1> detect_el_torito:
   109 00001DBB 6650                <1>     push eax
   110 00001DBD 6651                <1>     push ecx
   111 00001DBF 6657                <1>     push edi
   112 00001DC1 06                  <1>     push es
   113                              <1> 
   114                              <1>     ; Decide which drive to probe for El Torito.
   115                              <1>     ; Prefer slave (CD/ISO), fall back to master.
   116 00001DC2 2E803E[710A]01      <1>     cmp byte [cs:ide_slave_present], 1
   117 00001DC8 7412                <1>     je .et_use_slave
   118 00001DCA 2E803E[5E0A]00      <1>     cmp byte [cs:ide_master_present], 0
   119 00001DD0 0F840701            <1>     je .fail
   120 00001DD4 2EC606[840A]E0      <1>     mov byte [cs:ide_drive_sel], 0xE0  ; Master
   121 00001DDA EB06                <1>     jmp .et_read_brvd
   122                              <1> .et_use_slave:
   123 00001DDC 2EC606[840A]F0      <1>     mov byte [cs:ide_drive_sel], 0xF0  ; Slave
   124                              <1> 
   125                              <1> .et_read_brvd:
   126                              <1>     ; ── Read ISO sector 17 = ATA LBA 68 ──────────────────────────────────
   127                              <1>     ; The Boot Record Volume Descriptor occupies bytes 0–2047 of ISO sector 17.
   128                              <1>     ; We only need the first 512 bytes (fits in one ATA sector).
   129 00001DE2 66B844000000        <1>     mov eax, 68             ; ATA LBA 68 = ISO sector 17 (17 × 4 = 68)
   130 00001DE8 66B901000000        <1>     mov ecx, 1
   131 00001DEE 1E                  <1>     push ds
   132 00001DEF 07                  <1>     pop es
   133 00001DF0 BF[860A]            <1>     mov di, el_torito_buf
   134 00001DF3 E867FB              <1>     call ide_read_sectors
   135 00001DF6 0F82E100            <1>     jc .fail
   136                              <1> 
   137                              <1>     ; ── Validate Volume Descriptor type = 0 (Boot Record) ─────────────────
   138 00001DFA 803E[860A]00        <1>     cmp byte [el_torito_buf + 0], 0x00
   139 00001DFF 0F85D800            <1>     jne .fail
   140                              <1> 
   141                              <1>     ; ── Validate Standard Identifier "CD001" at bytes 1–5 ─────────────────
   142 00001E03 803E[870A]43        <1>     cmp byte [el_torito_buf + 1], 'C'
   143 00001E08 0F85CF00            <1>     jne .fail
   144 00001E0C 803E[880A]44        <1>     cmp byte [el_torito_buf + 2], 'D'
   145 00001E11 0F85C600            <1>     jne .fail
   146 00001E15 803E[890A]30        <1>     cmp byte [el_torito_buf + 3], '0'
   147 00001E1A 0F85BD00            <1>     jne .fail
   148 00001E1E 803E[8A0A]30        <1>     cmp byte [el_torito_buf + 4], '0'
   149 00001E23 0F85B400            <1>     jne .fail
   150 00001E27 803E[8B0A]31        <1>     cmp byte [el_torito_buf + 5], '1'
   151 00001E2C 0F85AB00            <1>     jne .fail
   152                              <1> 
   153                              <1>     ; ── Validate Boot System Identifier = "EL TORITO SPECIFICATION" ───────
   154                              <1>     ; Bytes 7–38.  We check the first 8 characters ("EL TORIT") for speed.
   155 00001E30 66813E[8D0A]454C20- <1>     cmp dword [el_torito_buf + 7],  'EL T'
   155 00001E38 54                  <1>
   156 00001E39 0F859E00            <1>     jne .fail
   157 00001E3D 66813E[910A]4F5249- <1>     cmp dword [el_torito_buf + 11], 'ORIT'
   157 00001E45 54                  <1>
   158 00001E46 0F859100            <1>     jne .fail
   159                              <1> 
   160                              <1>     ; ── Read Boot Catalog ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
   161                              <1>     ; Boot Catalog location in ISO sector units is at bytes 71–74 (DWORD LE).
   162                              <1>     ; Convert to ATA LBA by multiplying by 4.
   163 00001E4A 66A1[CD0A]          <1>     mov eax, [el_torito_buf + 71]   ; ISO sector of Boot Catalog
   164 00001E4E 66C1E002            <1>     shl eax, 2                       ; × 4 → ATA LBA
   165 00001E52 66B901000000        <1>     mov ecx, 1
   166 00001E58 1E                  <1>     push ds
   167 00001E59 07                  <1>     pop es
   168 00001E5A BF[860A]            <1>     mov di, el_torito_buf
   169 00001E5D E8FDFA              <1>     call ide_read_sectors
   170 00001E60 7279                <1>     jc .fail
   171                              <1> 
   172                              <1>     ; ── Validate Validation Entry ─────────────────────────────────────────
   173                              <1>     ; Byte 0 must be 0x01 (Header ID for Validation Entry).
   174 00001E62 803E[860A]01        <1>     cmp byte [el_torito_buf + 0], 0x01
   175 00001E67 7572                <1>     jne .fail
   176                              <1>     ; Bytes 30–31 must be 0x55, 0xAA (key).
   177 00001E69 803E[A40A]55        <1>     cmp byte [el_torito_buf + 30], 0x55
   178 00001E6E 756B                <1>     jne .fail
   179 00001E70 803E[A50A]AA        <1>     cmp byte [el_torito_buf + 31], 0xAA
   180 00001E75 7564                <1>     jne .fail
   181                              <1> 
   182                              <1>     ; ── Read Initial/Default Entry (bytes 32–63 of the catalog) ──────────
   183                              <1>     ; Byte 32: Boot Indicator — 0x88 = bootable.
   184 00001E77 803E[A60A]88        <1>     cmp byte [el_torito_buf + 32], 0x88
   185 00001E7C 755D                <1>     jne .fail
   186                              <1> 
   187                              <1>     ; Byte 33: Boot Media Type.
   188 00001E7E A0[A70A]            <1>     mov al, [el_torito_buf + 33]
   189 00001E81 2EA2[4E1D]          <1>     mov [cs:el_torito_emul], al
   190                              <1> 
   191                              <1>     ; Bytes 34–35: Load Segment (0 → default 0x07C0).
   192 00001E85 A1[A80A]            <1>     mov ax, [el_torito_buf + 34]
   193 00001E88 85C0                <1>     test ax, ax
   194 00001E8A 7503                <1>     jnz .store_seg
   195 00001E8C B8C007              <1>     mov ax, 0x07C0
   196                              <1> .store_seg:
   197 00001E8F 2EA3[4F1D]          <1>     mov [cs:el_torito_load_seg], ax
   198                              <1> 
   199                              <1>     ; Bytes 38–39: Sector Count (512-byte virtual sectors to transfer).
   200                              <1>     ; A count of 0 is treated as 1.
   201 00001E93 A1[AC0A]            <1>     mov ax, [el_torito_buf + 38]
   202 00001E96 85C0                <1>     test ax, ax
   203 00001E98 7503                <1>     jnz .store_count
   204 00001E9A B80100              <1>     mov ax, 1
   205                              <1> .store_count:
   206 00001E9D 2EA3[551D]          <1>     mov [cs:el_torito_count], ax
   207                              <1> 
   208                              <1>     ; Bytes 40–43: Load RBA (2048-byte CD sector of the boot image).
   209 00001EA1 66A1[AE0A]          <1>     mov eax, [el_torito_buf + 40]
   210 00001EA5 2E66A3[511D]        <1>     mov [cs:el_torito_rba], eax
   211                              <1> 
   212                              <1>     ; ── Mark detection as successful ──────────────────────────────────────
   213 00001EAA 2EC606[4C1D]01      <1>     mov byte [cs:el_torito_present], 1
   214                              <1> 
   215                              <1>     ; Set el_torito_drive based on emulation type.
   216                              <1>     ; All BIOS variables live in the ROM segment (cs:) to avoid conflicts
   217                              <1>     ; with bootloader data in low RAM.
   218 00001EB0 2EA0[4E1D]          <1>     mov al, [cs:el_torito_emul]
   219 00001EB4 3C04                <1>     cmp al, 0x04
   220 00001EB6 740C                <1>     je .drive_hd
   221 00001EB8 3C00                <1>     cmp al, 0x00
   222 00001EBA 7410                <1>     je .drive_cd
   223                              <1>     ; Floppy emulation (1–3).
   224 00001EBC 2EC606[4D1D]00      <1>     mov byte [cs:el_torito_drive], 0x00
   225 00001EC2 EB0E                <1>     jmp .drive_done
   226                              <1> .drive_hd:
   227 00001EC4 2EC606[4D1D]80      <1>     mov byte [cs:el_torito_drive], 0x80
   228 00001ECA EB06                <1>     jmp .drive_done
   229                              <1> .drive_cd:
   230 00001ECC 2EC606[4D1D]E0      <1>     mov byte [cs:el_torito_drive], 0xE0
   231                              <1> .drive_done:
   232                              <1> 
   233 00001ED2 07                  <1>     pop es
   234 00001ED3 665F                <1>     pop edi
   235 00001ED5 6659                <1>     pop ecx
   236 00001ED7 6658                <1>     pop eax
   237 00001ED9 F8                  <1>     clc
   238 00001EDA C3                  <1>     ret
   239                              <1> 
   240                              <1> .fail:
   241 00001EDB 07                  <1>     pop es
   242 00001EDC 665F                <1>     pop edi
   243 00001EDE 6659                <1>     pop ecx
   244 00001EE0 6658                <1>     pop eax
   245 00001EE2 F9                  <1>     stc
   246 00001EE3 C3                  <1>     ret
   247                              <1> 
   248                              <1> 
   249                              <1> ; =============================================================================
   250                              <1> ; boot_el_torito
   251                              <1> ;
   252                              <1> ; Loads and executes the El Torito boot image.
   253                              <1> ; Assumes detect_el_torito succeeded and el_torito_* variables are filled.
   254                              <1> ; The ide_drive_sel variable is already set to the correct drive from
   255                              <1> ; detect_el_torito.
   256                              <1> ;
   257                              <1> ; The boot image is read from ATA LBA (el_torito_rba × 4) into
   258                              <1> ; (el_torito_load_seg):0x0000.
   259                              <1> ;
   260                              <1> ; DL is set to the appropriate drive number before jumping:
   261                              <1> ;   No emulation (0x00) → DL = 0xE0   (virtual CD-ROM)
   262                              <1> ;   HD  emulation (0x04) → DL = 0x80   (first HD)
   263                              <1> ;   Floppy emulation     → DL = 0x00   (first floppy)
   264                              <1> ;
   265                              <1> ; DS:SI is set to 0x0000:0x0000 (most bootloaders don't require a specific
   266                              <1> ; value; those that query INT 13h AH=4Bh will call it themselves).
   267                              <1> ;
   268                              <1> ; Returns: only on error (CF=1).
   269                              <1> ; =============================================================================
   270                              <1> boot_el_torito:
   271 00001EE4 BE[1F01]            <1>     mov si, str_booting_cd
   272 00001EE7 E8FC0B              <1>     call bios_print
   273                              <1> 
   274                              <1>     ; Print El Torito parameters for diagnostics.
   275 00001EEA BE[5701]            <1>     mov si, str_et_rba
   276 00001EED E8F60B              <1>     call bios_print
   277 00001EF0 2E66A1[511D]        <1>     mov eax, [cs:el_torito_rba]
   278 00001EF5 E8360C              <1>     call bios_print_hex32
   279 00001EF8 BE[5F01]            <1>     mov si, str_et_cnt
   280 00001EFB E8E80B              <1>     call bios_print
   281 00001EFE 2EA1[551D]          <1>     mov ax, [cs:el_torito_count]
   282 00001F02 E8F70B              <1>     call bios_print_dec16
   283 00001F05 BE[6501]            <1>     mov si, str_et_seg
   284 00001F08 E8DB0B              <1>     call bios_print
   285 00001F0B 2EA1[4F1D]          <1>     mov ax, [cs:el_torito_load_seg]
   286 00001F0F E8110C              <1>     call bios_print_hex16
   287 00001F12 BE[6B01]            <1>     mov si, str_et_emul
   288 00001F15 E8CE0B              <1>     call bios_print
   289 00001F18 2E0FB606[4E1D]      <1>     movzx ax, byte [cs:el_torito_emul]
   290 00001F1E E81C0C              <1>     call bios_print_hex8
   291 00001F21 BE[7201]            <1>     mov si, str_et_load
   292 00001F24 E8BF0B              <1>     call bios_print
   293                              <1> 
   294                              <1>     ; Convert Load RBA (ISO 2048-byte sector units) to ATA LBA.
   295 00001F27 2E66A1[511D]        <1>     mov eax, [cs:el_torito_rba]
   296 00001F2C 66C1E002            <1>     shl eax, 2              ; × 4 → ATA LBA
   297                              <1> 
   298                              <1>     ; Number of 512-byte sectors to load.
   299                              <1>     ; Many ISOs set the El Torito sector count to just 4 (2 KB), expecting the
   300                              <1>     ; bootloader bootstrap to reload the rest.  ISOLINUX keeps the self-load
   301                              <1>     ; code in the upper portion of the image, so if only 4 sectors are loaded
   302                              <1>     ; that code is missing.  To work around this, enforce a minimum of 64
   303                              <1>     ; sectors (32 KB) which covers a full ISOLINUX/SYSLINUX image.
   304 00001F30 2E660FB70E[551D]    <1>     movzx ecx, word [cs:el_torito_count]
   305 00001F37 6683F940            <1>     cmp ecx, 64
   306 00001F3B 7306                <1>     jae .count_ok
   307 00001F3D 66B940000000        <1>     mov ecx, 64
   308                              <1> .count_ok:
   309                              <1> 
   310                              <1>     ; ide_drive_sel is already set by detect_el_torito.
   311                              <1>     ; Set ES:DI to (load_seg):0x0000 for the read.
   312 00001F43 06                  <1>     push es
   313 00001F44 2E8B1E[4F1D]        <1>     mov bx, [cs:el_torito_load_seg]
   314 00001F49 8EC3                <1>     mov es, bx
   315 00001F4B BF0000              <1>     mov di, 0x0000
   316 00001F4E E80CFA              <1>     call ide_read_sectors
   317 00001F51 07                  <1>     pop es
   318 00001F52 7230                <1>     jc .fail
   319                              <1> 
   320 00001F54 BE[8C01]            <1>     mov si, str_et_loaded
   321 00001F57 E88C0B              <1>     call bios_print
   322                              <1> 
   323                              <1>     ; ── Choose DL based on emulation type ─────────────────────────────────
   324 00001F5A 2EA0[4E1D]          <1>     mov al, [cs:el_torito_emul]
   325 00001F5E 3C04                <1>     cmp al, 0x04
   326 00001F60 7408                <1>     je .hd_emul
   327 00001F62 3C00                <1>     cmp al, 0x00
   328 00001F64 7408                <1>     je .no_emul
   329                              <1>     ; Floppy emulation (media type 1, 2, or 3).
   330 00001F66 B200                <1>     mov dl, 0x00
   331 00001F68 EB06                <1>     jmp .jump
   332                              <1> 
   333                              <1> .hd_emul:
   334 00001F6A B280                <1>     mov dl, 0x80
   335 00001F6C EB02                <1>     jmp .jump
   336                              <1> 
   337                              <1> .no_emul:
   338 00001F6E B2E0                <1>     mov dl, 0xE0
   339                              <1> 
   340                              <1> .jump:
   341                              <1>     ; DS:SI = 0 (boot image will call INT 13h AH=4Bh if it needs the packet).
   342 00001F70 31C0                <1>     xor ax, ax
   343 00001F72 8ED8                <1>     mov ds, ax
   344 00001F74 31F6                <1>     xor si, si
   345                              <1> 
   346                              <1>     ; Mark that we are handing off to CD boot image.
   347 00001F76 2EC606[571D]01      <1>     mov byte [cs:el_torito_handoff], 1
   348                              <1> 
   349                              <1>     ; Far-jump to (load_seg):0x0000 via a far return.
   350                              <1>     ; Stack layout for retf: [SP+0]=offset(IP), [SP+2]=segment(CS).
   351 00001F7C 2EA1[4F1D]          <1>     mov ax, [cs:el_torito_load_seg]
   352 00001F80 50                  <1>     push ax             ; CS
   353 00001F81 6A00                <1>     push word 0x0000    ; IP
   354 00001F83 CB                  <1>     retf                ; CS:IP ← load_seg:0x0000
   355                              <1> 
   356                              <1> .fail:
   357 00001F84 F9                  <1>     stc
   358 00001F85 C3                  <1>     ret
    30                                  %include "int1ah.asm"
     1                              <1> ; =============================================================================
     2                              <1> ; int1ah.asm — INT 1Ah: PCI BIOS services + RTC time/date
     3                              <1> ; =============================================================================
     4                              <1> 
     5                              <1> CMOS_ADDR   equ 0x70
     6                              <1> CMOS_DATA   equ 0x71
     7                              <1> 
     8                              <1> int1ah_handler:
     9 00001F86 80FC00              <1>     cmp ah, 0x00
    10 00001F89 7426                <1>     je .get_tick_count
    11 00001F8B 80FC01              <1>     cmp ah, 0x01
    12 00001F8E 7439                <1>     je .set_tick_count
    13 00001F90 80FC02              <1>     cmp ah, 0x02
    14 00001F93 7449                <1>     je .get_rtc_time
    15 00001F95 80FC03              <1>     cmp ah, 0x03
    16 00001F98 0F848100            <1>     je .set_rtc_time
    17 00001F9C 80FC04              <1>     cmp ah, 0x04
    18 00001F9F 747E                <1>     je .get_rtc_date
    19 00001FA1 80FC05              <1>     cmp ah, 0x05
    20 00001FA4 0F84C900            <1>     je .set_rtc_date
    21 00001FA8 80FCB1              <1>     cmp ah, 0xB1
    22 00001FAB 0F84D200            <1>     je .pci_bios
    23                              <1>     ; Unsupported.
    24 00001FAF F9                  <1>     stc
    25 00001FB0 CF                  <1>     iret
    26                              <1> 
    27                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    28                              <1> ; AH=00h: Get system timer tick count.
    29                              <1> ;   Returns: CX:DX = tick count, AL = midnight flag.
    30                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    31                              <1> .get_tick_count:
    32 00001FB1 1E                  <1>     push ds
    33 00001FB2 31C0                <1>     xor ax, ax
    34 00001FB4 8ED8                <1>     mov ds, ax
    35 00001FB6 8B166C04            <1>     mov dx, [BDA_TIMER_COUNT]
    36 00001FBA 8B0E6E04            <1>     mov cx, [BDA_TIMER_COUNT + 2]
    37 00001FBE A07004              <1>     mov al, [BDA_TIMER_OVERFLOW]
    38 00001FC1 C606700400          <1>     mov byte [BDA_TIMER_OVERFLOW], 0
    39 00001FC6 1F                  <1>     pop ds
    40 00001FC7 F8                  <1>     clc
    41 00001FC8 CF                  <1>     iret
    42                              <1> 
    43                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    44                              <1> ; AH=01h: Set system timer tick count.
    45                              <1> ;   CX:DX = new tick count.
    46                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    47                              <1> .set_tick_count:
    48 00001FC9 1E                  <1>     push ds
    49 00001FCA 31C0                <1>     xor ax, ax
    50 00001FCC 8ED8                <1>     mov ds, ax
    51 00001FCE 89166C04            <1>     mov [BDA_TIMER_COUNT], dx
    52 00001FD2 890E6E04            <1>     mov [BDA_TIMER_COUNT + 2], cx
    53 00001FD6 C606700400          <1>     mov byte [BDA_TIMER_OVERFLOW], 0
    54 00001FDB 1F                  <1>     pop ds
    55 00001FDC F8                  <1>     clc
    56 00001FDD CF                  <1>     iret
    57                              <1> 
    58                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    59                              <1> ; AH=02h: Get RTC time.
    60                              <1> ;   Returns: CH = hours (BCD), CL = minutes (BCD), DH = seconds (BCD).
    61                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    62                              <1> .get_rtc_time:
    63                              <1>     ; Read CMOS status register B to check BCD vs binary mode.
    64 00001FDE B00B                <1>     mov al, 0x0B
    65 00001FE0 E670                <1>     out CMOS_ADDR, al
    66 00001FE2 E471                <1>     in al, CMOS_DATA
    67 00001FE4 50                  <1>     push ax                     ; Save status B
    68                              <1> 
    69                              <1>     ; Read seconds.
    70 00001FE5 B000                <1>     mov al, 0x00
    71 00001FE7 E670                <1>     out CMOS_ADDR, al
    72 00001FE9 E471                <1>     in al, CMOS_DATA
    73 00001FEB 88C6                <1>     mov dh, al                  ; Seconds
    74                              <1> 
    75                              <1>     ; Read minutes.
    76 00001FED B002                <1>     mov al, 0x02
    77 00001FEF E670                <1>     out CMOS_ADDR, al
    78 00001FF1 E471                <1>     in al, CMOS_DATA
    79 00001FF3 88C1                <1>     mov cl, al                  ; Minutes
    80                              <1> 
    81                              <1>     ; Read hours.
    82 00001FF5 B004                <1>     mov al, 0x04
    83 00001FF7 E670                <1>     out CMOS_ADDR, al
    84 00001FF9 E471                <1>     in al, CMOS_DATA
    85 00001FFB 88C5                <1>     mov ch, al                  ; Hours
    86                              <1> 
    87                              <1>     ; Convert binary to BCD if CMOS is in binary mode (bit 2 of status B).
    88 00001FFD 58                  <1>     pop ax
    89 00001FFE A804                <1>     test al, 0x04
    90 00002000 7415                <1>     jz .rtc_time_done           ; Already BCD
    91                              <1> 
    92                              <1>     ; Convert binary to BCD.
    93 00002002 88F0                <1>     mov al, dh
    94 00002004 E86C00              <1>     call .bin_to_bcd
    95 00002007 88C6                <1>     mov dh, al
    96 00002009 88C8                <1>     mov al, cl
    97 0000200B E86500              <1>     call .bin_to_bcd
    98 0000200E 88C1                <1>     mov cl, al
    99 00002010 88E8                <1>     mov al, ch
   100 00002012 E85E00              <1>     call .bin_to_bcd
   101 00002015 88C5                <1>     mov ch, al
   102                              <1> 
   103                              <1> .rtc_time_done:
   104 00002017 B200                <1>     mov dl, 0                   ; Daylight saving: off
   105 00002019 30E4                <1>     xor ah, ah
   106 0000201B F8                  <1>     clc
   107 0000201C CF                  <1>     iret
   108                              <1> 
   109                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   110                              <1> ; AH=03h: Set RTC time.
   111                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   112                              <1> .set_rtc_time:
   113                              <1>     ; Not implemented for now.
   114 0000201D F8                  <1>     clc
   115 0000201E CF                  <1>     iret
   116                              <1> 
   117                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   118                              <1> ; AH=04h: Get RTC date.
   119                              <1> ;   Returns: CH = century (BCD), CL = year (BCD), DH = month (BCD),
   120                              <1> ;            DL = day (BCD).
   121                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   122                              <1> .get_rtc_date:
   123                              <1>     ; Read CMOS status B.
   124 0000201F B00B                <1>     mov al, 0x0B
   125 00002021 E670                <1>     out CMOS_ADDR, al
   126 00002023 E471                <1>     in al, CMOS_DATA
   127 00002025 50                  <1>     push ax
   128                              <1> 
   129                              <1>     ; Day of month.
   130 00002026 B007                <1>     mov al, 0x07
   131 00002028 E670                <1>     out CMOS_ADDR, al
   132 0000202A E471                <1>     in al, CMOS_DATA
   133 0000202C 88C2                <1>     mov dl, al
   134                              <1> 
   135                              <1>     ; Month.
   136 0000202E B008                <1>     mov al, 0x08
   137 00002030 E670                <1>     out CMOS_ADDR, al
   138 00002032 E471                <1>     in al, CMOS_DATA
   139 00002034 88C6                <1>     mov dh, al
   140                              <1> 
   141                              <1>     ; Year (2-digit).
   142 00002036 B009                <1>     mov al, 0x09
   143 00002038 E670                <1>     out CMOS_ADDR, al
   144 0000203A E471                <1>     in al, CMOS_DATA
   145 0000203C 88C1                <1>     mov cl, al
   146                              <1> 
   147                              <1>     ; Century (reg 0x32, if available — default to 0x20).
   148 0000203E B032                <1>     mov al, 0x32
   149 00002040 E670                <1>     out CMOS_ADDR, al
   150 00002042 E471                <1>     in al, CMOS_DATA
   151 00002044 84C0                <1>     test al, al
   152 00002046 7502                <1>     jnz .have_century
   153 00002048 B020                <1>     mov al, 0x20                ; Default: 20xx
   154                              <1> .have_century:
   155 0000204A 88C5                <1>     mov ch, al
   156                              <1> 
   157                              <1>     ; Convert if binary mode.
   158 0000204C 58                  <1>     pop ax
   159 0000204D A804                <1>     test al, 0x04
   160 0000204F 741C                <1>     jz .rtc_date_done
   161                              <1> 
   162 00002051 88D0                <1>     mov al, dl
   163 00002053 E81D00              <1>     call .bin_to_bcd
   164 00002056 88C2                <1>     mov dl, al
   165 00002058 88F0                <1>     mov al, dh
   166 0000205A E81600              <1>     call .bin_to_bcd
   167 0000205D 88C6                <1>     mov dh, al
   168 0000205F 88C8                <1>     mov al, cl
   169 00002061 E80F00              <1>     call .bin_to_bcd
   170 00002064 88C1                <1>     mov cl, al
   171 00002066 88E8                <1>     mov al, ch
   172 00002068 E80800              <1>     call .bin_to_bcd
   173 0000206B 88C5                <1>     mov ch, al
   174                              <1> 
   175                              <1> .rtc_date_done:
   176 0000206D 30E4                <1>     xor ah, ah
   177 0000206F F8                  <1>     clc
   178 00002070 CF                  <1>     iret
   179                              <1> 
   180                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   181                              <1> ; AH=05h: Set RTC date.
   182                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   183                              <1> .set_rtc_date:
   184 00002071 F8                  <1>     clc
   185 00002072 CF                  <1>     iret
   186                              <1> 
   187                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   188                              <1> ; Binary to BCD conversion. AL = binary value, returns AL = BCD.
   189                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   190                              <1> .bin_to_bcd:
   191 00002073 51                  <1>     push cx
   192 00002074 30E4                <1>     xor ah, ah
   193 00002076 B10A                <1>     mov cl, 10
   194 00002078 F6F1                <1>     div cl                      ; AL = tens, AH = ones
   195 0000207A C0E004              <1>     shl al, 4
   196 0000207D 08E0                <1>     or al, ah
   197 0000207F 59                  <1>     pop cx
   198 00002080 C3                  <1>     ret
   199                              <1> 
   200                              <1> ; =============================================================================
   201                              <1> ; PCI BIOS services (AH=B1h)
   202                              <1> ; =============================================================================
   203                              <1> 
   204                              <1> .pci_bios:
   205 00002081 3C01                <1>     cmp al, 0x01
   206 00002083 7432                <1>     je .pci_present
   207 00002085 3C02                <1>     cmp al, 0x02
   208 00002087 7440                <1>     je .pci_find_device
   209 00002089 3C03                <1>     cmp al, 0x03
   210 0000208B 0F848900            <1>     je .pci_find_class
   211 0000208F 3C08                <1>     cmp al, 0x08
   212 00002091 0F84DE00            <1>     je .pci_read_byte
   213 00002095 3C09                <1>     cmp al, 0x09
   214 00002097 0F84FF00            <1>     je .pci_read_word
   215 0000209B 3C0A                <1>     cmp al, 0x0A
   216 0000209D 0F841E01            <1>     je .pci_read_dword
   217 000020A1 3C0B                <1>     cmp al, 0x0B
   218 000020A3 0F843401            <1>     je .pci_write_byte
   219 000020A7 3C0C                <1>     cmp al, 0x0C
   220 000020A9 0F847F01            <1>     je .pci_write_word
   221 000020AD 3C0D                <1>     cmp al, 0x0D
   222 000020AF 0F84BA01            <1>     je .pci_write_dword
   223                              <1>     ; Unknown PCI function.
   224 000020B3 B481                <1>     mov ah, 0x81                ; Function not supported
   225 000020B5 F9                  <1>     stc
   226 000020B6 CF                  <1>     iret
   227                              <1> 
   228                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   229                              <1> ; AX=B101h: PCI BIOS present check.
   230                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   231                              <1> .pci_present:
   232 000020B7 66BA50434920        <1>     mov edx, 0x20494350        ; "PCI "
   233 000020BD B400                <1>     mov ah, 0x00                ; Success
   234 000020BF B702                <1>     mov bh, 0x02                ; PCI revision 2.1 major
   235 000020C1 B310                <1>     mov bl, 0x10                ; PCI revision 2.1 minor
   236 000020C3 B100                <1>     mov cl, 0x00                ; Max bus number
   237 000020C5 B001                <1>     mov al, 0x01                ; Hardware mechanism 1
   238 000020C7 F8                  <1>     clc
   239 000020C8 CF                  <1>     iret
   240                              <1> 
   241                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   242                              <1> ; AX=B102h: Find PCI device by vendor/device ID.
   243                              <1> ;   CX = device ID, DX = vendor ID, SI = index (0 = first match).
   244                              <1> ;   Returns: BH = bus, BL = dev/func.
   245                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   246                              <1> .pci_find_device:
   247 000020C9 1E                  <1>     push ds
   248 000020CA 57                  <1>     push di
   249 000020CB 50                  <1>     push ax
   250                              <1> 
   251 000020CC B800F0              <1>     mov ax, 0xF000
   252 000020CF 8ED8                <1>     mov ds, ax
   253                              <1> 
   254 000020D1 8B3E[1D08]          <1>     mov di, [pci_device_count]
   255 000020D5 85FF                <1>     test di, di
   256 000020D7 7438                <1>     jz .pci_fd_notfound
   257                              <1> 
   258 000020D9 56                  <1>     push si
   259 000020DA BB[1F08]            <1>     mov bx, pci_device_table
   260 000020DD 31C0                <1>     xor ax, ax                  ; Match index counter
   261                              <1> 
   262                              <1> .pci_fd_loop:
   263 000020DF 39F8                <1>     cmp ax, di
   264 000020E1 7D2D                <1>     jge .pci_fd_notfound_pop
   265                              <1> 
   266                              <1>     ; Check vendor ID at offset 4.
   267 000020E3 3B5704              <1>     cmp dx, [bx + 4]
   268 000020E6 750D                <1>     jne .pci_fd_next
   269                              <1>     ; Check device ID at offset 6.
   270 000020E8 3B4F06              <1>     cmp cx, [bx + 6]
   271 000020EB 7508                <1>     jne .pci_fd_next
   272                              <1> 
   273                              <1>     ; Match found — check index.
   274 000020ED 83FE00              <1>     cmp si, 0
   275 000020F0 7409                <1>     je .pci_fd_found
   276 000020F2 4E                  <1>     dec si
   277 000020F3 EB00                <1>     jmp .pci_fd_next
   278                              <1> 
   279                              <1> .pci_fd_next:
   280 000020F5 83C30C              <1>     add bx, PCI_ENTRY_SIZE
   281 000020F8 40                  <1>     inc ax
   282 000020F9 EBE4                <1>     jmp .pci_fd_loop
   283                              <1> 
   284                              <1> .pci_fd_found:
   285 000020FB 5E                  <1>     pop si
   286                              <1>     ; Build BH = bus, BL = (dev << 3) | func.
   287 000020FC 8A3F                <1>     mov bh, [bx + 0]           ; Bus
   288 000020FE 8A4701              <1>     mov al, [bx + 1]           ; Device
   289 00002101 C0E003              <1>     shl al, 3
   290 00002104 0A4702              <1>     or al, [bx + 2]            ; Function
   291 00002107 88C3                <1>     mov bl, al
   292                              <1> 
   293 00002109 58                  <1>     pop ax
   294 0000210A 5F                  <1>     pop di
   295 0000210B 1F                  <1>     pop ds
   296 0000210C B400                <1>     mov ah, 0x00
   297 0000210E F8                  <1>     clc
   298 0000210F CF                  <1>     iret
   299                              <1> 
   300                              <1> .pci_fd_notfound_pop:
   301 00002110 5E                  <1>     pop si
   302                              <1> .pci_fd_notfound:
   303 00002111 58                  <1>     pop ax
   304 00002112 5F                  <1>     pop di
   305 00002113 1F                  <1>     pop ds
   306 00002114 B486                <1>     mov ah, 0x86                ; Device not found
   307 00002116 F9                  <1>     stc
   308 00002117 CF                  <1>     iret
   309                              <1> 
   310                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   311                              <1> ; AX=B103h: Find PCI device by class code.
   312                              <1> ;   ECX = class code (class:subclass:progif), SI = index.
   313                              <1> ;   Returns: BH = bus, BL = dev/func.
   314                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   315                              <1> .pci_find_class:
   316 00002118 1E                  <1>     push ds
   317 00002119 57                  <1>     push di
   318 0000211A 50                  <1>     push ax
   319                              <1> 
   320 0000211B B800F0              <1>     mov ax, 0xF000
   321 0000211E 8ED8                <1>     mov ds, ax
   322                              <1> 
   323 00002120 8B3E[1D08]          <1>     mov di, [pci_device_count]
   324 00002124 85FF                <1>     test di, di
   325 00002126 7444                <1>     jz .pci_fc_notfound
   326                              <1> 
   327 00002128 56                  <1>     push si
   328 00002129 BB[1F08]            <1>     mov bx, pci_device_table
   329 0000212C 31C0                <1>     xor ax, ax
   330                              <1> 
   331                              <1> .pci_fc_loop:
   332 0000212E 39F8                <1>     cmp ax, di
   333 00002130 7D39                <1>     jge .pci_fc_notfound_pop
   334                              <1> 
   335                              <1>     ; Build class code from table: class(10) : subclass(9) : progif(8).
   336 00002132 660FB6570A          <1>     movzx edx, byte [bx + 10]  ; Class
   337 00002137 66C1E208            <1>     shl edx, 8
   338 0000213B 8A5709              <1>     mov dl, [bx + 9]           ; Subclass
   339 0000213E 66C1E208            <1>     shl edx, 8
   340 00002142 8A5708              <1>     mov dl, [bx + 8]           ; Prog IF
   341                              <1> 
   342 00002145 6639CA              <1>     cmp edx, ecx
   343 00002148 7506                <1>     jne .pci_fc_next
   344                              <1> 
   345 0000214A 83FE00              <1>     cmp si, 0
   346 0000214D 7407                <1>     je .pci_fc_found
   347 0000214F 4E                  <1>     dec si
   348                              <1> 
   349                              <1> .pci_fc_next:
   350 00002150 83C30C              <1>     add bx, PCI_ENTRY_SIZE
   351 00002153 40                  <1>     inc ax
   352 00002154 EBD8                <1>     jmp .pci_fc_loop
   353                              <1> 
   354                              <1> .pci_fc_found:
   355 00002156 5E                  <1>     pop si
   356 00002157 8A3F                <1>     mov bh, [bx + 0]
   357 00002159 8A4701              <1>     mov al, [bx + 1]
   358 0000215C C0E003              <1>     shl al, 3
   359 0000215F 0A4702              <1>     or al, [bx + 2]
   360 00002162 88C3                <1>     mov bl, al
   361                              <1> 
   362 00002164 58                  <1>     pop ax
   363 00002165 5F                  <1>     pop di
   364 00002166 1F                  <1>     pop ds
   365 00002167 B400                <1>     mov ah, 0x00
   366 00002169 F8                  <1>     clc
   367 0000216A CF                  <1>     iret
   368                              <1> 
   369                              <1> .pci_fc_notfound_pop:
   370 0000216B 5E                  <1>     pop si
   371                              <1> .pci_fc_notfound:
   372 0000216C 58                  <1>     pop ax
   373 0000216D 5F                  <1>     pop di
   374 0000216E 1F                  <1>     pop ds
   375 0000216F B486                <1>     mov ah, 0x86
   376 00002171 F9                  <1>     stc
   377 00002172 CF                  <1>     iret
   378                              <1> 
   379                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   380                              <1> ; AX=B108h: Read PCI config byte.
   381                              <1> ;   BH = bus, BL = dev/func, DI = register.
   382                              <1> ;   Returns: CL = byte value.
   383                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   384                              <1> .pci_read_byte:
   385 00002173 6650                <1>     push eax
   386 00002175 6652                <1>     push edx
   387                              <1> 
   388 00002177 E80F01              <1>     call .pci_build_addr
   389 0000217A BAF80C              <1>     mov dx, PCI_CONFIG_ADDR
   390 0000217D 66EF                <1>     out dx, eax
   391                              <1> 
   392                              <1>     ; Read dword and extract byte.
   393 0000217F BAFC0C              <1>     mov dx, PCI_CONFIG_DATA
   394 00002182 66ED                <1>     in eax, dx
   395 00002184 6689F9              <1>     mov ecx, edi
   396 00002187 80E103              <1>     and cl, 3                   ; Byte offset within dword
   397 0000218A C0E103              <1>     shl cl, 3                   ; Bit shift
   398 0000218D 66D3E8              <1>     shr eax, cl
   399 00002190 88C1                <1>     mov cl, al
   400                              <1> 
   401 00002192 665A                <1>     pop edx
   402 00002194 6658                <1>     pop eax
   403 00002196 B400                <1>     mov ah, 0x00
   404 00002198 F8                  <1>     clc
   405 00002199 CF                  <1>     iret
   406                              <1> 
   407                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   408                              <1> ; AX=B109h: Read PCI config word.
   409                              <1> ;   BH = bus, BL = dev/func, DI = register.
   410                              <1> ;   Returns: CX = word value.
   411                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   412                              <1> .pci_read_word:
   413 0000219A 6650                <1>     push eax
   414 0000219C 6652                <1>     push edx
   415                              <1> 
   416 0000219E E8E800              <1>     call .pci_build_addr
   417 000021A1 BAF80C              <1>     mov dx, PCI_CONFIG_ADDR
   418 000021A4 66EF                <1>     out dx, eax
   419                              <1> 
   420 000021A6 BAFC0C              <1>     mov dx, PCI_CONFIG_DATA
   421 000021A9 66ED                <1>     in eax, dx
   422 000021AB F7C70200            <1>     test di, 2
   423 000021AF 7404                <1>     jz .rw_low
   424 000021B1 66C1E810            <1>     shr eax, 16
   425                              <1> .rw_low:
   426 000021B5 89C1                <1>     mov cx, ax
   427                              <1> 
   428 000021B7 665A                <1>     pop edx
   429 000021B9 6658                <1>     pop eax
   430 000021BB B400                <1>     mov ah, 0x00
   431 000021BD F8                  <1>     clc
   432 000021BE CF                  <1>     iret
   433                              <1> 
   434                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   435                              <1> ; AX=B10Ah: Read PCI config dword.
   436                              <1> ;   BH = bus, BL = dev/func, DI = register.
   437                              <1> ;   Returns: ECX = dword value.
   438                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   439                              <1> .pci_read_dword:
   440 000021BF 6650                <1>     push eax
   441 000021C1 6652                <1>     push edx
   442                              <1> 
   443 000021C3 E8C300              <1>     call .pci_build_addr
   444 000021C6 BAF80C              <1>     mov dx, PCI_CONFIG_ADDR
   445 000021C9 66EF                <1>     out dx, eax
   446                              <1> 
   447 000021CB BAFC0C              <1>     mov dx, PCI_CONFIG_DATA
   448 000021CE 66ED                <1>     in eax, dx
   449 000021D0 6689C1              <1>     mov ecx, eax
   450                              <1> 
   451 000021D3 665A                <1>     pop edx
   452 000021D5 6658                <1>     pop eax
   453 000021D7 B400                <1>     mov ah, 0x00
   454 000021D9 F8                  <1>     clc
   455 000021DA CF                  <1>     iret
   456                              <1> 
   457                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   458                              <1> ; AX=B10Bh: Write PCI config byte.
   459                              <1> ;   BH = bus, BL = dev/func, DI = register, CL = byte value.
   460                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   461                              <1> .pci_write_byte:
   462 000021DB 6650                <1>     push eax
   463 000021DD 6652                <1>     push edx
   464                              <1> 
   465 000021DF E8A700              <1>     call .pci_build_addr
   466 000021E2 BAF80C              <1>     mov dx, PCI_CONFIG_ADDR
   467 000021E5 66EF                <1>     out dx, eax
   468                              <1> 
   469                              <1>     ; Read-modify-write.
   470 000021E7 BAFC0C              <1>     mov dx, PCI_CONFIG_DATA
   471 000021EA 66ED                <1>     in eax, dx
   472 000021EC 6651                <1>     push ecx
   473 000021EE 6689F9              <1>     mov ecx, edi
   474 000021F1 80E103              <1>     and cl, 3
   475 000021F4 C0E103              <1>     shl cl, 3

   555                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   556                              <1> ; Helper: Build PCI config address from BH=bus, BL=dev/func, DI=register.

   567 000022A4 6609C8              <1>     or eax, ecx
   568 000022A7 660D00000080        <1>     or eax, 0x80000000
