    31                              <1> ; INT 09h: Keyboard interrupt handler (IRQ 1).
    32                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    33                              <1> keyboard_irq_handler:
    34 000022D9 1E                  <1>     push ds
    35 000022DA 50                  <1>     push ax
    36 000022DB 53                  <1>     push bx
    37 000022DC 51                  <1>     push cx
    38                              <1> 
    39 000022DD 31C0                <1>     xor ax, ax
    40 000022DF 8ED8                <1>     mov ds, ax
    41                              <1> 
    42                              <1>     ; Read scancode from PS/2 controller.
    43 000022E1 E460                <1>     in al, 0x60
    44                              <1> 
    45                              <1>     ; Ignore break codes (key release, bit 7 set).
    46 000022E3 A880                <1>     test al, 0x80
    47 000022E5 7534                <1>     jnz .eoi
    48                              <1> 
    49                              <1>     ; Translate scancode to ASCII via table in BIOS segment (CS: override).
    50 000022E7 0FB6D8              <1>     movzx bx, al               ; BX = scancode index
    51 000022EA 83FB54              <1>     cmp bx, scancode_table_size
    52 000022ED 732C                <1>     jae .eoi
    53                              <1> 
    54 000022EF 88C4                <1>     mov ah, al                  ; AH = scan code
    55 000022F1 2E8A87[2423]        <1>     mov al, [cs:scancode_table + bx]    ; AL = ASCII
    56                              <1> 
    57                              <1>     ; If ASCII is 0, skip (non-printable/unmapped key).
    58 000022F6 84C0                <1>     test al, al
    59 000022F8 7421                <1>     jz .eoi
    60                              <1> 
    61                              <1>     ; Store in keyboard buffer (AH=scancode, AL=ASCII).
    62 000022FA 8B1E1C04            <1>     mov bx, [BDA_KBD_BUF_TAIL]
    63 000022FE 89D9                <1>     mov cx, bx
    64 00002300 83C102              <1>     add cx, 2
    65 00002303 3B0E8204            <1>     cmp cx, [BDA_KBD_BUF_END]
    66 00002307 7204                <1>     jb .no_wrap
    67 00002309 8B0E8004            <1>     mov cx, [BDA_KBD_BUF_START]
    68                              <1> .no_wrap:
    69 0000230D 3B0E1A04            <1>     cmp cx, [BDA_KBD_BUF_HEAD]
    70 00002311 7408                <1>     je .eoi                     ; Buffer full
    71                              <1> 
    72 00002313 89870004            <1>     mov [bx + 0x0400], ax
    73 00002317 890E1C04            <1>     mov [BDA_KBD_BUF_TAIL], cx
    74                              <1> 
    75                              <1> .eoi:

    77 0000231D E620                <1>     out PIC1_CMD, al
    78                              <1> 
    79 0000231F 59                  <1>     pop cx
    80 00002320 5B                  <1>     pop bx
    81 00002321 58                  <1>     pop ax
    82 00002322 1F                  <1>     pop ds
    83 00002323 CF                  <1>     iret

    85                              <1> ; Scancode set 1 to ASCII translation table (US keyboard layout).
    86                              <1> scancode_table:
    87 00002324 00                  <1>     db 0                        ; 0x00: none
    88 00002325 1B                  <1>     db 27                       ; 0x01: Escape
    89 00002326 313233343536373839- <1>     db '1','2','3','4','5','6','7','8','9','0'  ; 0x02-0x0B
    89 0000232F 30                  <1>

    91 00002332 08                  <1>     db 8                        ; 0x0E: Backspace
    92 00002333 09                  <1>     db 9                        ; 0x0F: Tab
    93 00002334 71776572747975696F- <1>     db 'q','w','e','r','t','y','u','i','o','p'  ; 0x10-0x19
    93 0000233D 70                  <1>
    94 0000233E 5B5D                <1>     db '[',']'                  ; 0x1A-0x1B
    95 00002340 0D                  <1>     db 13                       ; 0x1C: Enter
    96 00002341 00                  <1>     db 0                        ; 0x1D: Left Ctrl
    97 00002342 6173646667686A6B6C  <1>     db 'a','s','d','f','g','h','j','k','l'      ; 0x1E-0x26
    98 0000234B 3B27                <1>     db ';',"'"                  ; 0x27-0x28
    99 0000234D 60                  <1>     db '`'                      ; 0x29: Backtick
   100 0000234E 00                  <1>     db 0                        ; 0x2A: Left Shift
   101 0000234F 5C                  <1>     db '\'                      ; 0x2B: Backslash
   102 00002350 7A786376626E6D      <1>     db 'z','x','c','v','b','n','m'              ; 0x2C-0x32

   108 0000235E 00                  <1>     db 0                        ; 0x3A: Caps Lock
   109 0000235F 00<rep Ah>          <1>     times 10 db 0               ; 0x3B-0x44: F1-F10
   110 00002369 00<rep Fh>          <1>     times 15 db 0               ; 0x45-0x53: misc keys

   532                              <1> ; Test 12: Timer tick — verify PIT fires IRQ 0 and BDA timer increments.
   533                              <1> ; ===========================================================================

   538                              <1>     ; Read BDA timer count directly (faster than INT 1Ah).
   539 0000281E 31C0                <1>     xor ax, ax
   540 00002820 8ED8                <1>     mov ds, ax
   541 00002822 66A16C04            <1>     mov eax, [BDA_TIMER_COUNT]
   542 00002826 6689C3              <1>     mov ebx, eax                ; Save initial value
   543                              <1> 
   544                              <1>     ; Spin waiting for a tick.  PIT fires at ~18.2 Hz (every ~55ms).
   545                              <1>     ; With wall-clock-based PIT, we just need enough iterations for
   546                              <1>     ; ~55ms of real time to elapse.  Use a generous limit.
   547 00002829 66B9FFFFFF00        <1>     mov ecx, 0x00FFFFFF
   548                              <1> .wait:

   694 00002941 58                  <1>     pop ax

   701 0000294E C3                  <1>     ret
   702                              <1> 
   703                              <1> ; ===========================================================================
   704                              <1> ; Test 16: Protected mode — enter and return to real mode.
   705                              <1> ;   Critical for ISOLINUX/Linux which switch to PM immediately.
   706                              <1> ;
   707                              <1> ;   GDT layout:
   708                              <1> ;     0x08 — Code 16-bit, base=0xF0000, limit=0xFFFF  (BIOS ROM offsets)
   709                              <1> ;     0x10 — Data flat,    base=0,       limit=4GB     (all physical memory)
   710                              <1> ;   The code segment base matches CS=0xF000 in real mode, so the same
   711                              <1> ;   label offsets work in both real mode and protected mode.
   712                              <1> ; ===========================================================================
   713                              <1> selftest_pmode:
   714 0000294F BE[F603]            <1>     mov si, str_st_pmode
   715 00002952 E89101              <1>     call bios_print
   716                              <1> 
   717 00002955 FA                  <1>     cli
   718                              <1> 
   719 00002956 2E8C1E[CB29]        <1>     mov [cs:.save_ds], ds
   720 0000295B 2E8C06[CD29]        <1>     mov [cs:.save_es], es
   721 00002960 2E8C16[CF29]        <1>     mov [cs:.save_ss], ss
   722 00002965 2E8926[D129]        <1>     mov [cs:.save_sp], sp
   723                              <1> 
   724                              <1>     ; Load GDT.
   725 0000296A 2E0F0116[F029]      <1>     lgdt [cs:.gdt_ptr]
   726                              <1> 
   727                              <1>     ; Set CR0.PE.
   728 00002970 0F20C0              <1>     mov eax, cr0
   729 00002973 0C01                <1>     or al, 1
   730 00002975 0F22C0              <1>     mov cr0, eax
   731                              <1> 
   732                              <1>     ; Far jump to enter PM.  Selector 0x08 has base=0xF0000, so the
   733                              <1>     ; 16-bit offset .in_pm maps to physical 0xF0000 + .in_pm — correct.
   734 00002978 EA[7D29]0800        <1>     jmp 0x08:.in_pm
   735                              <1> 
   736                              <1> .in_pm:
   737                              <1>     ; 16-bit protected mode.  DS/ES/SS use selector 0x10 (base=0, flat).
   738 0000297D B81000              <1>     mov ax, 0x10
   739 00002980 8ED8                <1>     mov ds, ax
   740 00002982 8EC0                <1>     mov es, ax
   741 00002984 8ED0                <1>     mov ss, ax
   742                              <1> 
   743                              <1>     ; Write marker to prove PM memory access works (DS base=0 → flat).
   744 00002986 66C70610054B4F4D50  <1>     mov dword [0x0510], 0x504D4F4B  ; "PMOK"
   745                              <1> 
   746                              <1>     ; Return to real mode: clear CR0.PE.
   747 0000298F 0F20C0              <1>     mov eax, cr0
   748 00002992 24FE                <1>     and al, 0xFE
   749 00002994 0F22C0              <1>     mov cr0, eax
   750                              <1> 
   751                              <1>     ; Far jump reloads CS with real-mode segment 0xF000.
   752 00002997 EA[9C29]00F0        <1>     jmp 0xF000:.back_real
   753                              <1> 
   754                              <1> .back_real:
   755 0000299C 2EA1[CB29]          <1>     mov ax, [cs:.save_ds]
   756 000029A0 8ED8                <1>     mov ds, ax
   757 000029A2 2EA1[CD29]          <1>     mov ax, [cs:.save_es]
   758 000029A6 8EC0                <1>     mov es, ax
   759 000029A8 2EA1[CF29]          <1>     mov ax, [cs:.save_ss]
   760 000029AC 8ED0                <1>     mov ss, ax
   761 000029AE 2E8B26[D129]        <1>     mov sp, [cs:.save_sp]
   762                              <1> 
   763 000029B3 FB                  <1>     sti
   764                              <1> 
   765                              <1>     ; Verify the marker.
   766 000029B4 31C0                <1>     xor ax, ax
   767 000029B6 8ED8                <1>     mov ds, ax
   768 000029B8 66813E10054B4F4D50  <1>     cmp dword [0x0510], 0x504D4F4B
   769 000029C1 7504                <1>     jne .fail
   770                              <1> 
   771 000029C3 E8DDFA              <1>     call selftest_print_ok
   772 000029C6 C3                  <1>     ret
   773                              <1> .fail:
   774 000029C7 E801FB              <1>     call selftest_print_fail
   775 000029CA C3                  <1>     ret
   776                              <1> 
   777 000029CB 0000                <1> .save_ds: dw 0
   778 000029CD 0000                <1> .save_es: dw 0
   779 000029CF 0000                <1> .save_ss: dw 0
   780 000029D1 0000                <1> .save_sp: dw 0
   781                              <1> 
   782                              <1> ; Minimal GDT shared by PM test and extmem test.
   783 000029D3 90<rep 5h>          <1> align 8
   784                              <1> .gdt:
   785 000029D8 0000000000000000    <1>     dq 0                        ; Null descriptor (0x00)
   786                              <1> 
   787                              <1>     ; Code 16-bit (0x08): base=0xF0000, limit=0xFFFF, exec/read.
   788                              <1>     ; Base matches the real-mode CS=0xF000 so label offsets are the same.
   789 000029E0 FFFF                <1>     dw 0xFFFF                   ; Limit 15:0
   790 000029E2 0000                <1>     dw 0x0000                   ; Base 15:0
   791 000029E4 0F                  <1>     db 0x0F                     ; Base 23:16  (0x0F → base = 0x0F_0000)
   792 000029E5 9A                  <1>     db 10011010b                ; P=1, DPL=0, S=1, Type=Execute/Read
   793 000029E6 00                  <1>     db 0x00                     ; G=0, D=0 (16-bit), Limit 19:16=0
   794 000029E7 00                  <1>     db 0x00                     ; Base 31:24
   795                              <1> 
   796                              <1>     ; Data flat (0x10): base=0, limit=4GB, read/write.
   797 000029E8 FFFF                <1>     dw 0xFFFF                   ; Limit 15:0
   798 000029EA 0000                <1>     dw 0x0000                   ; Base 15:0
   799 000029EC 00                  <1>     db 0x00                     ; Base 23:16
   800 000029ED 92                  <1>     db 10010010b                ; P=1, DPL=0, S=1, Type=Read/Write
   801 000029EE 8F                  <1>     db 0x8F                     ; G=1, B=0, Limit 19:16=0xF → 4GB
   802 000029EF 00                  <1>     db 0x00                     ; Base 31:24
   803                              <1> .gdt_end:
   804                              <1> 
   805                              <1> .gdt_ptr:
   806 000029F0 1700                <1>     dw .gdt_end - .gdt - 1
   807 000029F2 [D8290F00]          <1>     dd .gdt + 0xF0000           ; Physical address of GDT
   808                              <1> 
   809                              <1> ; ===========================================================================
   810                              <1> ; Test 17: Extended memory — read/write above 1MB via protected mode.
   811                              <1> ;   Uses 16-bit PM with the flat data segment (base=0, limit=4GB) and
   812                              <1> ;   the a32 address-size prefix to reach addresses above 0xFFFF.
   813                              <1> ; ===========================================================================

    33                                  %include "boot.asm"
     1                              <1> ; =============================================================================
     2                              <1> ; boot.asm — Boot device helpers and bios_print utility
     3                              <1> ; =============================================================================
     4                              <1> 
     5                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
     6                              <1> ; bios_print — Print NUL-terminated string via INT 10h TTY.
     7                              <1> ;   Input: DS:SI = string pointer (caller must set DS = 0xF000 if needed).
     8                              <1> ;
     9                              <1> ;   This function uses direct VGA writes instead of INT 10h to avoid
    10                              <1> ;   recursion issues during early POST (before IVT is set up).
    11                              <1> ;   After IVT setup, callers can also use INT 10h directly.
    12                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    13                              <1> bios_print:
    14 00002AE6 50                  <1>     push ax
    15 00002AE7 53                  <1>     push bx
    16 00002AE8 56                  <1>     push si
    17                              <1> 
    18                              <1> .print_loop:
    19                              <1>     ; Use CS: override to read from BIOS segment.
    20 00002AE9 2EAC                <1>     cs lodsb
    21 00002AEB 84C0                <1>     test al, al
    22 00002AED 7409                <1>     jz .print_done
    23 00002AEF B40E                <1>     mov ah, 0x0E
    24 00002AF1 BB0700              <1>     mov bx, 0x0007             ; Page 0, light gray
    25 00002AF4 CD10                <1>     int 0x10
    26 00002AF6 EBF1                <1>     jmp .print_loop
    27                              <1> 
    28                              <1> .print_done:
    29 00002AF8 5E                  <1>     pop si
    30 00002AF9 5B                  <1>     pop bx
    31 00002AFA 58                  <1>     pop ax
    32 00002AFB C3                  <1>     ret
    33                              <1> 
    34                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    35                              <1> ; bios_print_dec16 — Print a 16-bit unsigned decimal number.
    36                              <1> ;   Input: AX = number to print.
    37                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    38                              <1> bios_print_dec16:
    39 00002AFC 50                  <1>     push ax
    40 00002AFD 53                  <1>     push bx
    41 00002AFE 51                  <1>     push cx
    42 00002AFF 52                  <1>     push dx
    43                              <1> 
    44 00002B00 31C9                <1>     xor cx, cx                  ; Digit count
    45 00002B02 BB0A00              <1>     mov bx, 10
    46                              <1> 
    47                              <1> .dec_div:
    48 00002B05 31D2                <1>     xor dx, dx
    49 00002B07 F7F3                <1>     div bx                     ; AX = quotient, DX = remainder
    50 00002B09 52                  <1>     push dx                     ; Save digit
    51 00002B0A 41                  <1>     inc cx
    52 00002B0B 85C0                <1>     test ax, ax
    53 00002B0D 75F6                <1>     jnz .dec_div
    54                              <1> 
    55                              <1> .dec_print:
    56 00002B0F 58                  <1>     pop ax
    57 00002B10 0430                <1>     add al, '0'
    58 00002B12 B40E                <1>     mov ah, 0x0E
    59 00002B14 53                  <1>     push bx
    60 00002B15 BB0700              <1>     mov bx, 0x0007
    61 00002B18 CD10                <1>     int 0x10
    62 00002B1A 5B                  <1>     pop bx
    63 00002B1B 49                  <1>     dec cx
    64 00002B1C 75F1                <1>     jnz .dec_print
    65                              <1> 
    66 00002B1E 5A                  <1>     pop dx
    67 00002B1F 59                  <1>     pop cx
    68 00002B20 5B                  <1>     pop bx
    69 00002B21 58                  <1>     pop ax
    70 00002B22 C3                  <1>     ret
    71                              <1> 
    72                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    73                              <1> ; bios_print_hex16 — Print a 16-bit hex value (4 hex digits).
    74                              <1> ;   Input: AX = value.
    75                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    76                              <1> bios_print_hex16:
    77 00002B23 50                  <1>     push ax
    78 00002B24 88E0                <1>     mov al, ah          ; High byte first
    79 00002B26 E81400              <1>     call bios_print_hex8
    80 00002B29 58                  <1>     pop ax
    81 00002B2A E81000              <1>     call bios_print_hex8  ; Low byte (AL from original AX)
    82 00002B2D C3                  <1>     ret
    83                              <1> 
    84                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    85                              <1> ; bios_print_hex32 — Print a 32-bit hex value (8 hex digits).
    86                              <1> ;   Input: EAX = value.
    87                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    88                              <1> bios_print_hex32:
    89 00002B2E 6650                <1>     push eax
    90 00002B30 66C1E810            <1>     shr eax, 16
    91 00002B34 E8ECFF              <1>     call bios_print_hex16   ; High 16 bits
    92 00002B37 6658                <1>     pop eax
    93 00002B39 E8E7FF              <1>     call bios_print_hex16   ; Low 16 bits (AX = lower 16 of original EAX)
    94 00002B3C C3                  <1>     ret
    95                              <1> 
    96                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    97                              <1> ; bios_print_hex8 — Print an 8-bit hex value.
    98                              <1> ;   Input: AL = value.
    99                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   100                              <1> bios_print_hex8:
   101 00002B3D 50                  <1>     push ax
   102 00002B3E 53                  <1>     push bx
   103 00002B3F 51                  <1>     push cx
   104                              <1> 
   105 00002B40 88C1                <1>     mov cl, al
   106 00002B42 C0E804              <1>     shr al, 4
   107 00002B45 E80B00              <1>     call .hex_nibble
   108 00002B48 88C8                <1>     mov al, cl
   109 00002B4A 240F                <1>     and al, 0x0F
   110 00002B4C E80400              <1>     call .hex_nibble
   111                              <1> 
   112 00002B4F 59                  <1>     pop cx
   113 00002B50 5B                  <1>     pop bx
   114 00002B51 58                  <1>     pop ax
   115 00002B52 C3                  <1>     ret
   116                              <1> 
   117                              <1> .hex_nibble:
   118 00002B53 3C0A                <1>     cmp al, 10
   119 00002B55 7204                <1>     jb .hex_digit
   120 00002B57 0437                <1>     add al, 'A' - 10
   121 00002B59 EB02                <1>     jmp .hex_out
   122                              <1> .hex_digit:
   123 00002B5B 0430                <1>     add al, '0'
   124                              <1> .hex_out:
   125 00002B5D B40E                <1>     mov ah, 0x0E
   126 00002B5F BB0700              <1>     mov bx, 0x0007
   127 00002B62 CD10                <1>     int 0x10
   128 00002B64 C3                  <1>     ret
    34                                  %include "ivt.asm"
     1                              <1> ; =============================================================================
     2                              <1> ; ivt.asm — Interrupt Vector Table setup
     3                              <1> ; =============================================================================
     4                              <1> 
     5                              <1> BIOS_SEG    equ 0xF000
     6                              <1> 
     7                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
     8                              <1> ; ivt_setup — Install all interrupt vectors at 0x0000:0x0000.
     9                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    10                              <1> ivt_setup:
    11 00002B65 06                  <1>     push es
    12 00002B66 57                  <1>     push di
    13 00002B67 50                  <1>     push ax
    14 00002B68 51                  <1>     push cx
    15                              <1> 
    16                              <1>     ; First, fill all 256 vectors with the dummy iret handler.
    17 00002B69 31C0                <1>     xor ax, ax
    18 00002B6B 8EC0                <1>     mov es, ax
    19 00002B6D 31FF                <1>     xor di, di
    20 00002B6F B90001              <1>     mov cx, 256
    21                              <1> .fill_default:
    22 00002B72 26C705[E623]        <1>     mov word [es:di], iret_handler
    23 00002B77 26C7450200F0        <1>     mov word [es:di + 2], BIOS_SEG
    24 00002B7D 83C704              <1>     add di, 4
    25 00002B80 E2F0                <1>     loop .fill_default
    26                              <1> 
    27                              <1>     ; Now install specific handlers.
    28                              <1> 
    29                              <1>     ; CPU exceptions 0x00-0x07 → iret_handler (already set).
    30                              <1> 
    31                              <1>     ; IRQ handlers (master PIC: INT 08h-0Fh).
    32 00002B82 BF2000              <1>     mov di, 0x08 * 4
    33 00002B85 26C705[AE22]        <1>     mov word [es:di], timer_tick_handler
    34 00002B8A 26C7450200F0        <1>     mov word [es:di + 2], BIOS_SEG
    35                              <1> 
    36 00002B90 BF2400              <1>     mov di, 0x09 * 4
    37 00002B93 26C705[D922]        <1>     mov word [es:di], keyboard_irq_handler
    38 00002B98 26C7450200F0        <1>     mov word [es:di + 2], BIOS_SEG
    39                              <1> 
    40                              <1>     ; IRQ 2 (cascade) → eoi_iret.
    41 00002B9E BF2800              <1>     mov di, 0x0A * 4
    42 00002BA1 26C705[E723]        <1>     mov word [es:di], eoi_iret
    43 00002BA6 26C7450200F0        <1>     mov word [es:di + 2], BIOS_SEG
    44                              <1> 
    45                              <1>     ; IRQ 3-7 → eoi_iret.
    46 00002BAC B90500              <1>     mov cx, 5
    47 00002BAF BF2C00              <1>     mov di, 0x0B * 4
    48                              <1> .master_irqs:
    49 00002BB2 26C705[E723]        <1>     mov word [es:di], eoi_iret
    50 00002BB7 26C7450200F0        <1>     mov word [es:di + 2], BIOS_SEG
    51 00002BBD 83C704              <1>     add di, 4
    52 00002BC0 E2F0                <1>     loop .master_irqs
    53                              <1> 
    54                              <1>     ; BIOS service interrupts.
    55 00002BC2 BF4000              <1>     mov di, 0x10 * 4
    56 00002BC5 26C705[1C0E]        <1>     mov word [es:di], int10h_handler
    57 00002BCA 26C7450200F0        <1>     mov word [es:di + 2], BIOS_SEG
    58                              <1> 
    59 00002BD0 BF4400              <1>     mov di, 0x11 * 4
    60 00002BD3 26C705[7823]        <1>     mov word [es:di], int11h_handler
    61 00002BD8 26C7450200F0        <1>     mov word [es:di + 2], BIOS_SEG
    62                              <1> 
    63 00002BDE BF4800              <1>     mov di, 0x12 * 4
    64 00002BE1 26C705[8223]        <1>     mov word [es:di], int12h_handler
    65 00002BE6 26C7450200F0        <1>     mov word [es:di + 2], BIOS_SEG
    66                              <1> 
    67 00002BEC BF4C00              <1>     mov di, 0x13 * 4
    68 00002BEF 26C705[F112]        <1>     mov word [es:di], int13h_handler
    69 00002BF4 26C7450200F0        <1>     mov word [es:di + 2], BIOS_SEG
    70                              <1> 
    71 00002BFA BF5000              <1>     mov di, 0x14 * 4
    72 00002BFD 26C705[8C23]        <1>     mov word [es:di], int14h_handler
    73 00002C02 26C7450200F0        <1>     mov word [es:di + 2], BIOS_SEG
    74                              <1> 
    75 00002C08 BF5400              <1>     mov di, 0x15 * 4
    76 00002C0B 26C705[7D1B]        <1>     mov word [es:di], int15h_handler
    77 00002C10 26C7450200F0        <1>     mov word [es:di + 2], BIOS_SEG
    78                              <1> 
    79 00002C16 BF5800              <1>     mov di, 0x16 * 4
    80 00002C19 26C705[BA1C]        <1>     mov word [es:di], int16h_handler
    81 00002C1E 26C7450200F0        <1>     mov word [es:di + 2], BIOS_SEG
    82                              <1> 
    83 00002C24 BF6400              <1>     mov di, 0x19 * 4
    84 00002C27 26C705[581D]        <1>     mov word [es:di], int19h_handler
    85 00002C2C 26C7450200F0        <1>     mov word [es:di + 2], BIOS_SEG
    86                              <1> 
    87 00002C32 BF6800              <1>     mov di, 0x1A * 4
    88 00002C35 26C705[861F]        <1>     mov word [es:di], int1ah_handler
    89 00002C3A 26C7450200F0        <1>     mov word [es:di + 2], BIOS_SEG
    90                              <1> 
    91                              <1>     ; Slave PIC IRQs: INT 70h-77h → slave_eoi_iret.
    92 00002C40 BFC001              <1>     mov di, 0x70 * 4
    93 00002C43 B90800              <1>     mov cx, 8
    94                              <1> .slave_irqs:
    95 00002C46 26C705[EE23]        <1>     mov word [es:di], slave_eoi_iret
    96 00002C4B 26C7450200F0        <1>     mov word [es:di + 2], BIOS_SEG
    97 00002C51 83C704              <1>     add di, 4
    98 00002C54 E2F0                <1>     loop .slave_irqs
    99                              <1> 
   100                              <1>     ; IDE primary IRQ (IRQ 14 = INT 76h on slave PIC, or INT 0Eh remapped).
   101                              <1>     ; With our PIC mapping: IRQ 14 → INT 70h + 6 = INT 76h.
   102 00002C56 BFD801              <1>     mov di, 0x76 * 4
   103 00002C59 26C705[D723]        <1>     mov word [es:di], ide_irq_handler
   104 00002C5E 26C7450200F0        <1>     mov word [es:di + 2], BIOS_SEG
   105                              <1> 
   106 00002C64 59                  <1>     pop cx
   107 00002C65 58                  <1>     pop ax
   108 00002C66 5F                  <1>     pop di
   109 00002C67 07                  <1>     pop es
   110 00002C68 C3                  <1>     ret
    35                                  %include "post.asm"
     1                              <1> ; =============================================================================
     2                              <1> ; post.asm — Power-On Self Test (POST) entry point
     3                              <1> ; =============================================================================
     4                              <1> 
     5                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
     6                              <1> ; post_entry — Main BIOS initialization. Called from reset vector.
     7                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
     8                              <1> post_entry:
     9 00002C69 FA                  <1>     cli
    10                              <1> 
    11                              <1>     ; Set up segments.
    12 00002C6A 31C0                <1>     xor ax, ax
    13 00002C6C 8ED8                <1>     mov ds, ax
    14 00002C6E 8EC0                <1>     mov es, ax
    15 00002C70 8ED0                <1>     mov ss, ax
    16 00002C72 BC007C              <1>     mov sp, 0x7C00              ; Stack below boot sector load area
    17                              <1> 
    18                              <1>     ; Initialize PIC (remap IRQs).
    19 00002C75 E86ADA              <1>     call pic_init
    20                              <1> 
    21                              <1>     ; Initialize PIT (timer at ~18.2 Hz).
    22 00002C78 E899DA              <1>     call pit_init
    23                              <1> 
    24                              <1>     ; Install all interrupt vectors.
    25 00002C7B E8E7FE              <1>     call ivt_setup
    26                              <1> 
    27                              <1>     ; Initialize BDA and EBDA.
    28 00002C7E E87FD3              <1>     call bda_init
    29 00002C81 E8F0D3              <1>     call ebda_init
    30                              <1> 
    31                              <1>     ; Initialize serial port (COM1).
    32 00002C84 E898DA              <1>     call serial_init
    33                              <1> 
    34                              <1>     ; Initialize VGA text mode (clear screen, set cursor).
    35 00002C87 E8E4DA              <1>     call video_init
    36                              <1> 
    37                              <1>     ; Enable interrupts (timer ticks start).
    38 00002C8A FB                  <1>     sti
    39                              <1> 
    40                              <1>     ; Print POST banner.
    41 00002C8B BE[9C00]            <1>     mov si, str_banner
    42 00002C8E E855FE              <1>     call bios_print
    43                              <1> 
    44                              <1>     ; Always print last INT13 diagnostic state.
    45 00002C91 BE[EF01]            <1>     mov si, str_i13_diag
    46 00002C94 E84FFE              <1>     call bios_print
    47 00002C97 2EA1[ED12]          <1>     mov ax, [cs:int13_call_count]
    48 00002C9B E85EFE              <1>     call bios_print_dec16
    49 00002C9E BE[D501]            <1>     mov si, str_cd_reentry_ah
    50 00002CA1 E842FE              <1>     call bios_print
    51 00002CA4 2EA0[EB12]          <1>     mov al, [cs:int13_last_ah]
    52 00002CA8 E892FE              <1>     call bios_print_hex8
    53 00002CAB BE[E501]            <1>     mov si, str_cd_reentry_dl
    54 00002CAE E835FE              <1>     call bios_print
    55 00002CB1 2EA0[EC12]          <1>     mov al, [cs:int13_last_dl]
    56 00002CB5 E885FE              <1>     call bios_print_hex8
    57 00002CB8 BE[EA01]            <1>     mov si, str_cd_reentry_st
    58 00002CBB E828FE              <1>     call bios_print
    59 00002CBE 2EA0[EA12]          <1>     mov al, [cs:int13_last_status]
    60 00002CC2 E878FE              <1>     call bios_print_hex8
    61 00002CC5 BE[5401]            <1>     mov si, str_crlf
    62 00002CC8 E81BFE              <1>     call bios_print
    63                              <1> 
    64                              <1>     ; If we already handed off to an El Torito boot image and reached POST
    65                              <1>     ; again, show diagnostic context directly on screen.
    66 00002CCB 2E803E[571D]00      <1>     cmp byte [cs:el_torito_handoff], 0
    67 00002CD1 7439                <1>     je .no_cd_reentry_diag

    91 00002D06 2EC606[571D]00      <1>     mov byte [cs:el_torito_handoff], 0

   122 00002D40 E8C4D9              <1>     call pic_unmask_irq14

   125 00002D43 BE[DE00]            <1>     mov si, str_ide_master
   126 00002D46 E89DFD              <1>     call bios_print

   128 00002D4F 7408                <1>     je .no_ide
   129 00002D51 BE[F900]            <1>     mov si, str_ide_found

   131 00002D57 EB06                <1>     jmp .ide_done

   134 00002D5C E887FD              <1>     call bios_print
   135                              <1> .ide_done:
   136                              <1> 
   137                              <1>     ; Run BIOS self-test diagnostics.
   138 00002D5F E89BF6              <1>     call bios_selftest
   139                              <1> 
   140                              <1>     ; Print blank line.
   141 00002D62 BE[5401]            <1>     mov si, str_crlf
   142 00002D65 E87EFD              <1>     call bios_print
   143                              <1> 
   144                              <1>     ; Start boot sequence.
   145 00002D68 CD19                <1>     int 0x19
    36                                  
    37                                  ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    38                                  ; Pad to 0xFFF0 (64KB - 16 bytes), then place the reset vector.
    39                                  ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    40 00002D6A FF<rep D286h>           times (0xFFF0 - ($ - $$)) db 0xFF
    41                                  
    42                                  reset_vector:
    43 0000FFF0 EA[692C]00F0                jmp 0xF000:post_entry       ; Far jump to POST (5 bytes)
    44 0000FFF5 00                          db 0                        ; Padding
    45 0000FFF6 30332F30332F3236            db '03/03/26'               ; BIOS date string (8 bytes at 0xFFF5)
    46 0000FFFE FF                          db 0xFF                     ; Model byte
    47 0000FFFF 00                          db 0                        ; Checksum placeholder
    48                                  
    49                                  ; Ensure we are exactly 64KB.
    50                                  times (0x10000 - ($ - $$)) db 0xFF
